# Trading Intelligence Engine ÔÇö Agent Memory

## Repository
- Path: `/workspace/project/trading_intelligence_engine`
- Package layout: `src/` (setuptools `package-dir = {"" = "src"}`), `pythonpath = ["src"]` in pytest config.
- `__init__.py` convention: the engine/data/models/config/utils/core packages have INTENTIONALLY EMPTY `__init__.py` files (no re-exports) ÔÇö import via full paths, e.g. `from engine.intelligence.signal import SignalEngine`. The orchestration packages are the EXCEPTION: `src/engine/pipeline/__init__.py` (Sprint 11F), `src/engine/reporting/__init__.py` (Sprint 11G), `src/engine/research/__init__.py` (Sprint 11H), `src/engine/experiment/__init__.py` (Sprint 11J), `src/engine/registry/__init__.py` (Sprint 11K), `src/engine/query/__init__.py` (Sprint 11L), `src/engine/suite/__init__.py` (Sprint 11M) and `src/engine/selection/__init__.py` (Sprint 11N) re-export their public API for convenience, e.g. `from engine.pipeline import HistoricalEvaluationPipeline, trending_dataset`, `from engine.reporting import EvaluationReportEngine`, `from engine.query import ExperimentQueryEngine`, `from engine.suite import SuiteRunner, SuiteRegistry`, `from engine.selection import SelectionEngine, SuiteSelectionEngine`.
- Python >= 3.11 (env runs 3.13). Tests: `python -m pytest -q`. No conftest.

## Engine public APIs (verified Sprint 11F)
- `SwingEngine(config).detect(candles) -> list[SwingPoint]` ÔÇö fractal swing detection; needs `lookback` candles on each side to confirm a swing. Mutates `swing.evidence` (mutable SwingEvidence) but never the input candles.
- `MarketStructureEngine().analyze(swings) -> list[StructurePoint]`
- `StructureAnalysisEngine().analyze(structures) -> StructureAnalysis` ÔÇö exposes `current_bias` / `previous_bias` (NOT `.bias`).
- `BOSEngine().analyze(analysis) -> BOSResult` ÔÇö exposes `bos_type` (NOT `.type`).
- `CHOCHEngine().analyze(structures, analysis, bos) -> CHOCHResult` ÔÇö exposes `choch_type` (NOT `.type`).
- `TrendEngine().analyze(analysis, bos, choch) -> TrendResult` ÔÇö exposes `.state` (matches what ConfluenceEngine reads).
- `LiquidityEngine(config).detect(swings) -> list[LiquidityPool]`
- `LiquidityEventEngine(config).analyze(pools, candles) -> list[LiquidityEvent]`
- `ConfluenceEngine().analyze(analysis, bos, choch, trend, liquidity_events=None, reference_time=None) -> ConfluenceResult` ÔÇö reads attributes DEFENSIVELY via getattr: `analysis.bias`, `bos.type`, `choch.type`, `trend.state`. Real model attribute names DIFFER (see above), so end-to-end wiring needs adapter views (see pipeline).
- `DecisionEngine().analyze(confluence) -> DecisionContext`
- `SignalEngine().analyze(decision, context=None) -> SignalResult` where `context` is `SignalContext(trigger_close, structure_break_level, liquidity_level, supplied_entry, reference_time)`. Entry precedence: supplied > trigger_close > structure_break > liquidity. Fallback stop = entry*0.98 (LONG) / *1.02 (SHORT); fallback target = entry +/- risk*2.0.
- `SignalValidationEngine().validate(signal, candles, max_candles=None) -> ValidationResult` ÔÇö DEFAULT_MAX_CANDLES=50. Reads `signal.direction.value`, `entry_price`, `stop_loss`, `take_profit`. SignalResult works directly. Entry touch: `low <= entry <= high`.
- `PerformanceAnalyticsEngine().analyze(results: Iterable[ValidationResult]) -> PerformanceAnalytics` ÔÇö never raises on empty input.

## Sprint 11F ÔÇö Pipeline (added)
- `src/engine/pipeline/historical_pipeline.py`: `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline(config).evaluate(candles) -> PipelineResult`. Walk-forward: at point T, analysis..signal see only `candles[:T+1]`; validation sees `candles[T+1:]`. One-active-signal policy: next signal allowed at `T + 1 + validation.candles_evaluated`. Suppressed points flagged via `PipelineEvaluationPoint.suppressed`.
- `src/engine/pipeline/datasets.py`: `trending_dataset()`, `flat_dataset()`, `minimal_dataset()`.
- `src/engine/models/pipeline.py`: `PipelineResult`, `PipelineEvaluationPoint`.
- `scripts/test_pipeline.py`: demo. `tests/test_pipeline.py`: 30 tests.
- Adapter views (`_AnalysisView`, `_BOSView`, `_CHOCHView`) map `current_bias`Ôćĺ`bias`, `bos_type`Ôćĺ`type`, `choch_type`Ôćĺ`type` for the confluence engine. This is orchestration glue; engines themselves are UNCHANGED (additive integration).

## Coding conventions
- Immutable frozen+slots dataclasses for models. Mutable config dataclasses (LiquidityConfig etc.) need `field(default_factory=...)` when used as defaults in a frozen dataclass.
- No print() inside engines. Type hints everywhere. Composition over inheritance.
- Demos in `scripts/test_*.py` insert `src` onto sys.path.

## Sprint 11G ÔÇö Evaluation Reporting Layer (added)
- New package `src/engine/reporting/` sits ABOVE the pipeline (dependency: models ÔćÉ intelligence ÔćÉ pipeline ÔćÉ reporting).
- `src/engine/models/evaluation.py`: frozen+slots `PipelineStatistics`, `SignalStatistics`, `TradeStatistics`, `EvaluationReport`.
- `src/engine/reporting/evaluation.py`: `EvaluationReportEngine().analyze(result: PipelineResult, label="", metadata=None) -> EvaluationReport`. Stateless, deterministic, additive. NO existing engine/model modified.
- `EvaluationReport` bundles three independent views: pipeline funnel, signal-generation stats, completed-trade stats. Retains raw `PipelineResult` by reference (`report.result`) for future comparison/robustness/Monte Carlo layers. `label` + `metadata` Mapping[str,str] identify a run.
- Trade stats are DELEGATED, not recomputed: `TradeStatistics.from_performance(result.performance)` projects the existing `PerformanceAnalytics`. No analytics logic duplicated.
- Terminal validation statuses (for `validations_completed`): WIN, LOSS, EXPIRED, AMBIGUOUS, NOT_TRIGGERED. OPEN excluded (window ran out, unresolved).
- `result.signals` tuple = generated (non-suppressed) signals only; suppressed signals live on `PipelineEvaluationPoint.suppressed`. `eligible_signals` (== eligible_decisions) == total_signals + suppressed_signals.
- `scripts/test_evaluation.py`: demo. `tests/test_evaluation.py`: 35 tests.

## Sprint 11H ÔÇö Research & Robustness Analysis Layer (added)
- New package `src/engine/research/` sits ABOVE the reporting layer (dependency: models ÔćÉ intelligence ÔćÉ pipeline ÔćÉ reporting ÔćÉ research).
- `src/engine/models/research.py`: frozen+slots research models. Enums: `MarketRegime` (TRENDING/FLAT/HIGH_VOLATILITY/LOW_VOLATILITY/UNKNOWN), `SegmentationDimension` (DIRECTION/SETUP_QUALITY/CONFIDENCE/RISK_REWARD/REGIME), `ConfidenceBucket` (LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH/VERY_HIGH), `RiskRewardBucket` (LOW_RR/MEDIUM_RR/HIGH_RR). Models: `RegimeStatistics`, `SegmentStatistics`, `SegmentedPerformance`, `ParameterResult`, `ParameterSensitivityReport`, `OutOfSampleReport`, `LeakageCheckResult`, `ResearchReport`.
- `src/engine/research/regime.py`: `MarketRegimeEngine(config).classify(candles) -> MarketRegime`. Deterministic, intentionally simple: normalised ATR (volatility) gates first, then directional efficiency (net move / path length). Uses ONLY the supplied walk-forward slice; never reads future candles. `RegimeConfig` is mutable (window=20, min_history=10, configurable thresholds).
- `src/engine/research/segmentation.py`: `PerformanceSegmentationEngine(config).segment(pairs, dimension, candles=None, evaluation_indices=None) -> SegmentedPerformance` where `pairs` = `(SignalResult, ValidationResult)` tuples. Per-segment metrics DELEGATED to `PerformanceAnalyticsEngine` (no recomputation). Confidence/RR bucket thresholds configurable via mutable `SegmentationConfig`. REGIME dimension needs `candles` + `evaluation_indices` (walk-forward slices `candles[:T+1]`).
- `src/engine/research/sensitivity.py`: `ParameterSensitivityEngine(config).analyze(parameter_name, parameter_values, evaluator) -> ParameterSensitivityReport`. Generic ÔÇö caller supplies evaluator returning `PerformanceAnalytics` or `PipelineResult` (projected via `.performance` / `.validation_results`). NO automatic overfitting: reports `best_value_by_expectancy` (descriptive, `best_value_descriptive=True`), `median_expectancy`, `expectancy_range`, `profitable_configurations`, `stability_ratio`, `sufficient_data` (>=2 configs). No `optimal`/`deploy` attribute.
- `src/engine/research/out_of_sample.py`: `OutOfSampleEngine(config).evaluate(candles, evaluator) -> OutOfSampleReport`. CHRONOLOGICAL split only (never shuffles); `OutOfSampleConfig.split_ratio` default 0.70. Degradation = oos Ôłĺ in_sample for expectancy/profit_factor/win_rate/drawdown + trade_count_change. Graceful on insufficient data / evaluator exceptions.
- `src/engine/research/leakage.py`: `LeakageAuditEngine(config).audit(result, candles=None) -> LeakageCheckResult`. 5 deterministic checks: (1) analysis at T within candle range, (2) validation begins after T, (3) no future candle supplied to analysis (validated via candles_evaluated ÔëĄ future window), (4) out-of-sample isolation (caller-declared; warning when unconfirmed), (5) chronological indices/timestamps. `passed` iff no failures. Does NOT claim mathematical zero-leakage guarantee.
- `src/engine/research/research.py`: `ResearchEngine(config).analyze(result, candles, pipeline_evaluator=None, parameter_evaluator=None, label, metadata) -> ResearchReport`. Orchestrates all sub-engines; does NOT duplicate trading logic. Conclusions are DESCRIPTIVE only (never "strategy is profitable"); uses wording like "Positive historical expectancy observed in this dataset", "Out-of-sample performance degraded...", "Insufficient trades for reliable inference", "Parameter sensitivity appears high", "No leakage violations were detected by the implemented checks".
- `src/engine/research/__init__.py` re-exports the full public API (third orchestration exception after pipeline/reporting).
- `scripts/test_research.py`: demo producing the Research Robustness Report. `tests/test_research.py`: 64 tests (regime, segmentation, sensitivity, out-of-sample, leakage, ResearchEngine, immutability, end-to-end).
- No existing engine/model modified ÔÇö additive integration only. All Sprint 11G behavior preserved.

## Sprint 11I ÔÇö Research & Robustness HARDENING (added)
This sprint hardens the research framework for serious strategy research WITHOUT adding ML, LLMs, broker integration, live trading or external APIs. It is purely research-methodology hardening. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11AÔÇô11H public API signature was changed, and all 419 prior tests still pass.

New engines (extend the existing `src/engine/research/` package):
- `src/engine/research/walk_forward.py`: `WalkForwardParameterEngine(config).evaluate(candles, parameter_name, parameter_values, evaluator) -> WalkForwardSelectionReport`. Makes the development / evaluation separation for parameter selection EXPLICIT and auditable. Chronologically splits candles into development `[:split]` and evaluation `[split:]` windows (non-overlapping). Evaluates EVERY candidate on the DEVELOPMENT window only -> `CandidateResult` list. Selects the descriptive best (highest DEVELOPMENT expectancy) -> `SelectedConfiguration` (`selected_from_development_data=True`, `selection_basis="development_expectancy"`). Evaluates ONLY the selected config on the EVALUATION window -> `out_of_sample_result`. `selection_isolated_from_evaluation=True` and `selection_verified=True` BY CONSTRUCTION (structural proof, not statistical). `WalkForwardEvaluator = Callable[[Sequence[OHLCVCandle], Any], Any]` (takes candles AND value, unlike the Sprint 11H `ParameterEvaluator` which takes only value). `WalkForwardConfig` mutable (split_ratio=0.70, min_development_candles=10, min_evaluation_candles=10, min_development_trades=1, selection_metric="expectancy"). Graceful on insufficient data / evaluator exceptions (never raises).
- `src/engine/research/robustness.py`: `ParameterRobustnessEngine(config).analyze(sensitivity_report) -> ParameterRobustnessReport`. Consumes a Sprint 11H `ParameterSensitivityReport` (composable ÔÇö no recomputation of trading logic). Distinguishes DESCRIPTIVE BEST from ROBUST/STABLE: a config is `stable` only when `profitable AND near_median` (expectancy within `band = max(|median|*band_fraction, band_floor)` of the median). `descriptive_best` is the highest-expectancy value (descriptive only); `descriptive_best_is_robust` is True only when the descriptive best is also stable (NOT automatic). `highly_dependent_on_single_config` flags when the sweep's positive result rests on one config. `robust` requires sufficient data AND >=1 stable config. `RobustnessConfig` mutable (min_completed_trades=3, band_fraction=0.25, band_floor=0.1, min_configurations=2). Also exports `build_sensitivity_for_robustness(name, results)` helper to build a minimal sensitivity report from raw `ParameterResult` list for standalone use.

New/extended models in `src/engine/models/research.py` (all frozen, backward-compatible optional fields with defaults):
- `LeakageSeverity` enum (PASS/WARNING/NOT_VERIFIED/FAILURE) + `LeakageCheck` (name, severity, reason, passed) structured per-check result.
- `LeakageCheckResult` EXTENDED with `checks: tuple[LeakageCheck, ...]` and `not_verified: tuple[str, ...]` (defaulted empty). Legacy `passed`/`checks_performed`/`failures`/`warnings` preserved.
- `ConfigurationRobustness`, `ParameterRobustnessReport`.
- `CandidateResult`, `SelectedConfiguration`, `WalkForwardSelectionReport` (with `development_window`/`evaluation_window` half-open ranges, `windows_overlap` property, `selection_isolated_from_evaluation`/`selection_verified`).
- `DataSufficiencyReport` (sufficient_trades / insufficient_trades / insufficient_regime_samples / insufficient_oos_trades / insufficient_parameter_observations + `sufficient_for_inference` gate; all thresholds stored on the report for auditability).
- `RegimeStatistics` EXTENDED with `sufficient_observations: bool` and `min_observations_for_inference: int` (defaulted); new properties `has_no_completed_trades`, `is_profitable`, `is_unprofitable` ÔÇö preserve the distinction that ZERO TRADES is unobserved, NOT zero performance.
- `OutOfSampleReport` EXTENDED with `development_window`/`evaluation_window`/`parameter_selection_isolated` (defaulted None = NOT VERIFIED).
- `ResearchReport` EXTENDED with `parameter_robustness`, `walk_forward_selection`, `data_sufficiency` (all default None).

Leakage audit hardening (`src/engine/research/leakage.py`):
- `LeakageAuditEngine.audit(result, candles=None, context=None)` ÔÇö new optional `context: LeakageAuditContext` (backward compatible: omit -> same 5-check audit, `checks_performed==5`).
- Base 5 checks now return structured `LeakageCheck` items with severities. Check 4 (OOS parameter isolation): when a `WalkForwardSelectionReport` is supplied, VERIFIED structurally (PASS); otherwise NOT_VERIFIED (never falsely PASS). Caller `out_of_sample_isolated=True` config accepted as PASS (caller assertion, not structural proof).
- New extended checks (run ONLY when walk-forward context supplied, incrementing `checks_performed` to 8): (6) development/evaluation window overlap, (7) parameter-selection isolation (`selection_isolated_from_evaluation` AND `selected_from_development_data` AND `selection_verified`), (8) no accidental reuse of evaluation results in development (selected dev expectancy must match a candidate dev expectancy).
- `LeakageCheckResult.failures` = FAILURE-severity reasons; `warnings` = WARNING+NOT_VERIFIED reasons (backward compat: Check 4 not-confirmed still surfaces in warnings); `not_verified` = NOT_VERIFIED reasons; `passed` = no failures. NEVER silently swallows a failure; NEVER reports PASS for an unprovable property.

ResearchEngine orchestration (`src/engine/research/research.py`):
- `ResearchEngine.analyze(..., walk_forward_evaluator=None, ...)` ÔÇö new optional `walk_forward_evaluator: WalkForwardEvaluator`. When supplied (with sensitivity sweep config), runs `WalkForwardParameterEngine`, builds `parameter_robustness` from the sensitivity report via `ParameterRobustnessEngine`, builds `DataSufficiencyReport`, and passes a `LeakageAuditContext` to the leakage audit (enabling checks 6-8).
- `ResearchConfig` EXTENDED with `robustness: RobustnessConfig`, `walk_forward: WalkForwardConfig`, `min_regime_observations: int = 3`, `min_oos_trades: int = 3`, `min_parameter_configurations: int = 2` (all defaulted).
- Conclusions now explicitly label DESCRIPTIVE findings ("descriptive, not predictive") vs VALIDATED findings ("validated by construction" for walk-forward selection). New conclusions for: zero-trade regimes (unobserved not zero-performance), descriptive-best-not-robust, high single-config dependency, data sufficiency gate, NOT_VERIFIED leakage items.
- Regime statistics now populate `sufficient_observations` and `min_observations_for_inference` from config.

`src/engine/research/__init__.py` re-exports the full Sprint 11I public API (CandidateResult, ConfigurationRobustness, DataSufficiencyReport, LeakageAuditContext, LeakageCheck, LeakageSeverity, ParameterRobustnessEngine, ParameterRobustnessReport, RobustnessConfig, SelectedConfiguration, WalkForwardConfig, WalkForwardEvaluator, WalkForwardParameterEngine, WalkForwardSelectionReport, build_sensitivity_for_robustness, PipelineEvaluator).

`scripts/test_research.py`: demo EXTENDED to show development/evaluation periods, candidate configurations, selected configuration, selection-from-development-only, robustness/stability, data sufficiency, structured leakage audit (with NOT VERIFIED), and conclusions. `tests/test_research_robustness.py`: 65 new tests covering walk-forward dev/OOS separation, selection-from-dev-only, OOS-cannot-change-selection, robustness (descriptive-best-vs-robust, stable-vs-unstable, high-dependency, no-overfitting), data sufficiency (all insufficiency flags + gate), regime robustness (zero-trade=unobserved, sufficiency, profitable/unprofitable), leakage (structured checks, NOT_VERIFIED semantics, overlapping windows, selection-isolation failure, evaluation-reuse failure, temporal ordering, backward compat), report correctness (sections, descriptive-vs-validated labels, determinism, frozen), and backward compatibility (legacy analyze signature, legacy model construction, legacy config defaults, Sprint 11H API importability).


## Sprint 11J ÔÇö Reproducible Research Experiment Framework (added)
New package `src/engine/experiment/` sits ABOVE the research layer (dependency: models ÔćÉ intelligence ÔćÉ pipeline ÔćÉ reporting ÔćÉ research ÔćÉ experiment). The fourth orchestration package (after pipeline/reporting/research); its `__init__.py` re-exports the public API.
- `src/engine/models/experiment.py`: frozen+slots experiment models. Enum: `ExperimentEvidenceStatus` (INSUFFICIENT/PARTIAL/SUFFICIENT). Models: `DatasetSpec`, `ReproducibilityMetadata`, `ExperimentSummary`, `ExperimentResult`, `ExperimentComparisonRow`, `ExperimentComparison`.
- `src/engine/experiment/config.py`: `ExperimentConfig` (frozen+slots) ÔÇö immutable, deterministic identity. `experiment_id`/`configuration_hash`/`canonical_representation` are properties derived from a recursive canonical serialization of the FULL config tree (label, dataset, pipeline config, research config, evaluation config, strategy_parameters, seed, metadata). `_canonicalize()` handles dataclasses, enums (by `.name`), tuples/lists, sets (sorted), mappings, primitives; engine instances embedded in sub-configs (e.g. `SegmentationConfig.regime_engine`) Ôćĺ fully-qualified class name; bare lambdas/evaluators REJECTED (TypeError) so nondeterministic callables can't enter the ID. Same config Ôćĺ same ID; changing ANY meaningful parameter (label, dataset, pipeline param, seed, strategy param, metadata) Ôćĺ different ID. NO timestamps/nondeterministic values in ID. `EvaluationConfig` (frozen+slots) toggles run_out_of_sample/run_walk_forward/run_sensitivity + parameter sweep.
- `src/engine/experiment/reproducibility.py`: `build_reproducibility_metadata(config, dataset, dataset_size, actual_content_hash) -> ReproducibilityMetadata`; `dataset_content_hash(candles) -> str` (sha256 hex of canonical candle representation). `code_version` via `importlib.metadata` ("UNKNOWN" when unavailable ÔÇö never fabricated). `random_seed` from config (None when not applicable). `parameter_values` captures deterministic strategy/pipeline params. `reproducible` flag is structural, not statistical.
- `src/engine/experiment/runner.py`: `ExperimentRunner().run(config, candles=None) -> ExperimentResult`. ORCHESTRATION ONLY ÔÇö implements no trading/validation/pipeline/research logic. Reuses `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline`, `EvaluationReportEngine`, `ResearchEngine` as-is. Builds the evaluators the research engine requires (pipeline evaluator, parameter evaluator, walk-forward evaluator) from the config; the swept parameter is `swing_lookback` (applied via `SwingConfig(lookback=int(value))`). Resolves built-in datasets (`trending`/`flat`/`minimal`) deterministically by name; custom datasets require explicit candles. Computes the actual dataset content hash and stores it on the resolved `DatasetSpec`. Builds `ExperimentSummary` (flat projection of the reused EvaluationReport + ResearchReport ÔÇö nothing recomputed) and `ExperimentEvidenceStatus` (INSUFFICIENT if completed_trades < min_trades_for_inference; SUFFICIENT if data_sufficiency gate passed; PARTIAL otherwise). Graceful on edge cases (empty/minimal/no-trades/zero-OOS) ÔÇö never raises; produces an honest INSUFFICIENT result.
- `src/engine/experiment/comparison.py`: `ExperimentComparisonEngine().compare(results) -> ExperimentComparison`. No recomputation; reads reused `ExperimentSummary`. NEVER auto-declares "best" unless evidence supports it: `best_by_expectancy`/`best_by_total_r` computed ONLY among SUFFICIENT experiments; None when none sufficient. Insufficient data explicit. Conclusions descriptive ("Highest expectancy (descriptive, among sufficient)"), never "is profitable"/"is best".
- `src/engine/reporting/experiment.py`: `ExperimentReportFormatter().format(result) -> str`. Required sections: Experiment Identity, Dataset, Configuration, Pipeline Summary, Research Summary, Robustness Summary, Walk-Forward Summary, OOS Summary, Data Sufficiency, Leakage Audit, Reproducibility, Research Conclusion. No print() inside formatter (returns str). Insufficient evidence explicit.
- `src/engine/reporting/comparison.py`: `ExperimentComparisonFormatter().format(comparison) -> str`. Side-by-side metrics table + descriptive ranking + conclusions. No misleading claims.
- `src/engine/reporting/__init__.py` EXTENDED to re-export `ExperimentReportFormatter`, `ExperimentComparisonFormatter` (reporting package convention).
- `src/engine/experiment/__init__.py` re-exports: DatasetSpec, EvaluationConfig, ExperimentComparison, ExperimentComparisonEngine, ExperimentComparisonRow, ExperimentConfig, ExperimentEvidenceStatus, ExperimentResult, ExperimentRunner, ExperimentSummary, ReproducibilityMetadata, build_reproducibility_metadata, dataset_content_hash.
- `scripts/test_experiment.py`: demo ÔÇö runs two experiments (lookback 2 vs 3 on trending dataset) and prints a full experiment report for each + an experiment comparison report.
- `tests/test_experiment.py`: 52 tests (config determinism/immutability, runner orchestration/dataset resolution/pipeline+research invocation, reproducibility metadata/seed/code-version, reporting sections/no-misleading-claims/insufficient-evidence, edge cases empty/minimal/no-trades/zero-OOS/custom-without-candles, dataset content hash).
- `tests/test_experiment_comparison.py`: 25 tests (two-experiment comparison, equal/differing results, best-by-expectancy/total-r among SUFFICIENT only, no-best-without-sufficient, best-excludes-insufficient, insufficient/partial/OOS/robustness/leakage handling, descriptive-not-predictive, immutability, determinism, reporting sections/no-misleading-claims/empty-comparison).
- Reuse: `ExperimentResult` references (never duplicates) the existing `EvaluationReport` and `ResearchReport` by composition. `ExperimentSummary`/`ExperimentComparisonRow` are thin read-only projections. `ExperimentRunner` builds the evaluators the research engine needs (mirroring `scripts/test_research.py`) and delegates; no trading logic reimplemented.
Architectural invariants preserved: models ÔćÉ intelligence ÔćÉ pipeline ÔćÉ reporting ÔćÉ research ÔćÉ experiment. No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files + reporting __init__ extension). No print() in engines/formatters (formatters return str; demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live/external-API. Descriptive conclusions only; never claims "strategy is profitable"/"is best". Experiment ID fully deterministic (no timestamps). Reproducibility metadata never fabricated (UNKNOWN/None explicit).
Architectural invariants preserved: models ÔćÉ intelligence ÔćÉ pipeline ÔćÉ reporting ÔćÉ research. No existing engine/model modified (only additive optional fields + new files). No print() in engines. No ML/LLM/broker/live/external-API. Descriptive conclusions only; never claims "strategy is profitable".

## Sprint 11K ÔÇö Research Dataset / Experiment Registry and Result Persistence Layer (added)
New package `src/engine/registry/` sits ABOVE the experiment layer (dependency: models ÔćÉ intelligence ÔćÉ pipeline ÔćÉ reporting ÔćÉ research ÔćÉ experiment ÔćÉ registry). The fifth orchestration package (after pipeline/reporting/research/experiment); its `__init__.py` re-exports the public API. The registry implements NO trading/validation/pipeline/research/comparison logic ÔÇö every engine is reused as-is. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11AÔÇô11J public API signature was changed, and all 575 prior tests still pass.
- Goal: persist completed `ExperimentResult` objects in a structured, reproducible form and later retrieve/compare them WITHOUT rerunning the underlying trading pipeline. Designed as infrastructure, not as a trading strategy.
- `src/engine/models/registry.py`: frozen+slots registry models + `SCHEMA_VERSION = 1` (module constant). `ExperimentRecordHeader` (schema_version + experiment_id), `PersistedExperimentRecord` (header + reconstructed `ExperimentResult` + canonical JSON text + raw parsed mapping).
- `src/engine/registry/exceptions.py`: `ExperimentPersistenceError` (base) + `ExperimentNotFoundError`, `ExperimentAlreadyExistsError`, `ExperimentIntegrityError`, `UnsupportedSchemaVersionError`. No errors swallowed silently.
- `src/engine/registry/serialization.py`: deterministic, self-describing JSON serialization. `serialize_experiment(result) -> str` (sorted-key canonical JSON), `deserialize_experiment(payload) -> ExperimentResult`, `parse_record(payload) -> ExperimentRecordHeader` (cheap header-only parse), `canonical_json(payload) -> str`, `serialize_experiment_bytes`. Every non-primitive value carries a small type tag (`__type__` dataclass / `__enum__` / `__datetime__` / `__tuple__` / `__engine__` / `__bytes__`) so the deserializer reconstructs the EXACT model type without parsing type annotations. Enums Ôćĺ name; tuples Ôćĺ tagged (preserve tuple vs list); datetimes Ôćĺ ISO; dataclasses (frozen OR mutable) Ôćĺ field dict; mappings Ôćĺ dict; sets Ôćĺ sorted list; embedded engine instances (e.g. `SegmentationConfig.regime_engine`) Ôćĺ class token reconstructed via no-arg constructor; bool checked before int. Type resolution via `importlib.import_module(module).ClassName` (lazy, no hard-coded class registry).
- Serialization scope (intentional, documented): the structured research/evaluation views are persisted IN FULL ÔÇö `ExperimentSummary`, `ExperimentConfig` (full identity), `DatasetSpec`, `ReproducibilityMetadata`, `EvaluationReport` (pipeline/signals/trades statistics), `ResearchReport` (overall_performance `PerformanceAnalytics`, regime_statistics, segmentation, parameter_sensitivity, parameter_robustness, walk_forward_selection, out_of_sample, data_sufficiency, leakage, conclusions, metadata), label/metadata. The raw walk-forward/pipeline engine outputs retained BY REFERENCE for future layers are NOT persisted and reconstruct as None: `EvaluationReport.result`, `ResearchReport.result`, `WalkForwardSelectionReport.out_of_sample_result`, `OutOfSampleReport.in_sample_results` / `OutOfSampleReport.out_of_sample_results` (these are heavy per-evaluation-point/per-trade raw outputs, regenerable by rerunning, never part of the persisted research record). Loaded results are valid model instances on which the existing comparison + reporting layers work unchanged.
- `src/engine/registry/persistence.py`: `ExperimentPersistence(directory=None)` ÔÇö filesystem-based, JSON, one file per experiment id (`<directory>/<experiment_id>.json`). `save(result, overwrite=False) -> Path`, `load(id) -> ExperimentResult`, `load_record(id) -> PersistedExperimentRecord`, `exists(id)`, `list_experiments() -> sorted list[str]`, `delete(id)`, `path_for(id)`. Atomic write: `tempfile.mkstemp` in the SAME directory Ôćĺ write Ôćĺ flush Ôćĺ fsync (best-effort) Ôćĺ `os.replace` (single-filesystem rename, atomic on Windows+POSIX); temp file cleaned up on any failure and a typed `ExperimentPersistenceError` raised (never a partial target). `default_registry_directory()` = `Path.cwd()/"experiments"` (no hard-coded absolute paths). Experiment ids validated against a safe-character regex to prevent path traversal. Loader validates schema_version BEFORE reconstructing any model.
- `src/engine/registry/registry.py`: `ExperimentRegistry(directory=None, persistence=None)` sits ABOVE persistence and integrates with the existing Sprint 11J comparison layer. API: `register(result, overwrite=False) -> ExperimentResult` (no silent overwrite ÔÇö `ExperimentAlreadyExistsError` unless `overwrite=True`), `get(id) -> ExperimentResult` (loads + verifies identity), `exists(id)`, `list() -> sorted list[str]`, `delete(id)`, `load_many(ids) -> list[ExperimentResult]` (preserves order; missing raises), `compare(ids) -> ExperimentComparison` (loads + delegates to the EXISTING `ExperimentComparisonEngine` ÔÇö NO comparison logic duplicated), `verify_identity(result)` (delegates to the persistence integrity check, same check applied on every register/get).
- Integrity mechanism (`ExperimentPersistence._verify_identity`): cross-checks `result.experiment_id` == `config.experiment_id` == `reproducibility.experiment_id`; `config.configuration_hash` == `reproducibility.configuration_hash`; `dataset.content_hash` == `reproducibility.dataset_content_hash`; file-name id == stored top-level id. Any mismatch Ôćĺ `ExperimentIntegrityError` (never silently accepts corrupted/tampered data). Graceful when `config` is None (hand-constructed minimal results) ÔÇö only requires a non-empty id.
- Schema/versioning: `SCHEMA_VERSION = 1` written at the top of every record. Loader rejects any record whose `schema_version != 1` with `UnsupportedSchemaVersionError` (clear message incl. found + supported) BEFORE model reconstruction. Future sprints can bump + add migration without rewriting the experiment system. `parse_record` does the cheap header-only version check for listing/inspection.
- Comparison integration: a user runs experiments Ôćĺ `register`s them Ôćĺ later `get`/`load_many`/`compare`s loaded results WITHOUT rerunning the pipeline. The demo and tests monkeypatch `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate` to explode and confirm comparison uses ONLY persisted data. `ExperimentReportFormatter` and `ExperimentComparisonFormatter` work unchanged on loaded results.
- `src/engine/registry/__init__.py` re-exports: `SCHEMA_VERSION`, `ExperimentRegistry`, `ExperimentPersistence`, `ExperimentRecordHeader`, `PersistedExperimentRecord`, all 5 exceptions, `serialize_experiment`/`serialize_experiment_bytes`/`deserialize_experiment`/`parse_record`/`canonical_json`, `default_registry_directory`.
- `scripts/test_registry.py`: demo ÔÇö runs two experiments (lookback 2 vs 3 on trending dataset), saves to a temp registry, lists, loads back, verifies identities (id/config_hash/dataset_hash), compares loaded experiments, prints a comparison report + per-experiment report; explicitly states the comparison uses persisted results rather than rerunning.
- `tests/test_registry.py`: 63 tests (serialization round-trip + determinism + id/dataset-hash/config-hash/parameter/evidence-status/conclusion/robustness/walk-forward/OOS/leakage/pipeline-summary preservation + enum round-trip + raw-result-dropped; registry list/exists/missing/duplicate-no-silent-overwrite/overwrite/delete/register-returns-result; error handling corrupted-JSON/invalid-schema-version/future-schema/integrity-mismatch-experiment-id/config-hash/dataset-hash/filename-id-mismatch/unsafe-id; atomic persistence no-temp-leftover/temp-cleanup-on-failure/replace-existing/Windows-compatible-paths/default-dir-relative/load-record/load-missing; comparison integration load-multiple/order/missing-raises/compare-persisted/compare-loaded-matches-live/uses-persisted-no-rerun/formatter-on-loaded; evidence variants insufficient/partial/sufficient; schema versioning constant/record-carries-version/parse-header/rejects-future/canonical-json-sorted/module-match; immutability frozen-models/re-serialize-loop/different-configs-different-ids/no-global-state; package surface importable API + exception hierarchy).
Architectural invariants preserved: models ÔćÉ intelligence ÔćÉ pipeline ÔćÉ reporting ÔćÉ research ÔćÉ experiment ÔćÉ registry. No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files). No print() in engines/persistence/registry (demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live/external-API/database dependency (stdlib only: json/importlib/datetime/dataclasses/enum/pathlib/tempfile/os/re). No global mutable state. No hard-coded absolute paths (pathlib throughout; default dir relative to cwd). Deterministic serialization (sorted keys, stable value encoding, no repr()/memory addresses). Experiment ID remains fully deterministic. No Sprint 11AÔÇô11J regression.

## Sprint 11L ÔÇö Experiment Query, Filtering & Analysis Layer (added)
New package `src/engine/query/` sits ABOVE the registry layer (dependency: models ÔćÉ intelligence ÔćÉ pipeline ÔćÉ reporting ÔćÉ research ÔćÉ experiment ÔćÉ registry ÔćÉ query). The sixth orchestration package (after pipeline/reporting/research/experiment/registry); its `__init__.py` re-exports the public API. The query layer implements NO trading/validation/pipeline/research/comparison/persistence logic ÔÇö every value is read from the persisted `ExperimentResult` produced by the existing engines. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11AÔÇô11K public API signature was changed, and all 638 prior tests still pass.
- Goal: discover, filter, sort, group and summarize PERSISTED experiments WITHOUT rerunning the trading pipeline. Designed as reusable infrastructure for a future UI/dashboard (not coupled to the CLI).
- `src/engine/models/query.py`: frozen+slots query models + `ExperimentQueryError`. Enums: `ExperimentSortKey` (EXPECTANCY/TOTAL_R/WIN_RATE/PROFIT_FACTOR/MAX_DRAWDOWN/COMPLETED_TRADES/EXPERIMENT_ID/LABEL), `SortOrder` (ASCENDING/DESCENDING), `ExperimentGroupDimension` (DATASET/EVIDENCE_STATUS/LABEL/CONFIGURATION). Models: `ExperimentFilter` (all-optional AND criteria), `ExperimentQueryRow` (read-only projection), `ExperimentGroup`, `ExperimentGrouping`, `MetricLeader`, `ExperimentAnalysisSummary`.
- `src/engine/query/query.py`: `ExperimentQueryEngine(registry)`. Public API: `query(filter=None, sort_key=None, sort_order=ASCENDING) -> tuple[ExperimentQueryRow, ...]`, `group(filter=None, dimension=DATASET) -> ExperimentGrouping`, `summarize(filter=None) -> ExperimentAnalysisSummary`. Loads persisted results via `registry.list()` + `registry.load_many()` (persisted data only). Sort key / order / dimension accept enum members OR case-insensitive string names (unknown values raise `ExperimentQueryError`). Deterministic stable sort: two-pass (ascending experiment-id baseline, then metric with `reverse` for DESCENDING) so ties are broken by ascending id regardless of direction. Default ordering (no sort key) is ascending experiment id.
- `ExperimentFilter` criteria (all optional, logical AND): `experiment_id`, `label`, `dataset_name`, `evidence_status`, `configuration_hash`, `dataset_content_hash`, `reproducible` (True/False/None), `min_completed_trades`/`max_completed_trades` (inclusive), `min_expectancy`/`max_expectancy` (inclusive), `parameter_values` (subset/containment match against `reproducibility.parameter_values`; experiments lacking a required key do not match ÔÇö "where available").
- Grouping: buckets rows by dimension value; groups sorted by name ascending; each group carries `experiment_ids` (sorted), `experiment_count` and a `sufficient_count`/`partial_count`/`insufficient_count` breakdown. CONFIGURATION dimension groups by configuration hash (parameter/configuration identity).
- Analysis summary (`ExperimentAnalysisSummary`): `total_experiments`, `sufficient_count`/`partial_count`/`insufficient_count`, `has_sufficient_evidence`, descriptive leaders `best_by_expectancy`/`best_by_total_r`/`lowest_drawdown` (`MetricLeader | None`, computed ONLY among SUFFICIENT experiments, `None` when none sufficient ÔÇö the core "no best without evidence" guarantee), `most_reproducible_experiment_ids` (sorted), `dataset_coverage`/`configuration_coverage`/`parameter_coverage` (Mapping[str,int], sorted), descriptive `conclusions`. INSUFFICIENT experiments are NEVER ranked as reliable winners; PARTIAL experiments are excluded from leaders.
- `src/engine/reporting/query.py`: `ExperimentQueryFormatter().format(rows) -> str`, `ExperimentGroupingFormatter().format(grouping) -> str`, `ExperimentAnalysisFormatter().format(summary) -> str`. Stateless, deterministic; return str (no print()); descriptive-only language; insufficient evidence explicit; never claims historical results predict future performance. `src/engine/reporting/__init__.py` EXTENDED to re-export the three new formatters (reporting package convention).
- `src/engine/query/__init__.py` re-exports: `ExperimentAnalysisSummary`, `ExperimentFilter`, `ExperimentGroup`, `ExperimentGroupDimension`, `ExperimentGrouping`, `ExperimentQueryEngine`, `ExperimentQueryError`, `ExperimentQueryRow`, `ExperimentSortKey`, `MetricLeader`, `SortOrder`.
- `scripts/test_experiment_query.py`: demo ÔÇö runs four experiments (two datasets ├Ś two lookbacks), synthesizes a spread of evidence statuses (one SUFFICIENT / one PARTIAL / two INSUFFICIENT), saves to a temp registry, then queries/filters/sorts/groups/summarizes from persistence and prints formatted reports; finally patches `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate` to raise and re-runs query/group/summarize to PROVE the analysis uses persisted results only (no pipeline rerun).
- `tests/test_experiment_query.py`: 96 tests (empty registry; single experiment; multiple experiments + default ordering; every filter ÔÇö id/label/dataset/evidence/configuration-hash/dataset-content-hash/reproducible/min-max-trades/min-max-expectancy/parameter-subset/combined-AND/no-match/inclusive-bounds; sorting every key ascending+descending + string coercion + stable tie-break + default-by-id; grouping by dataset/evidence/label/configuration + breakdown + deterministic order + filtered grouping + empty + string coercion; evidence handling ÔÇö counts/leaders-only-among-sufficient/insufficient-never-best/partial-excluded/lowest-drawdown-among-sufficient; determinism query/group/summarize + repeated calls; persisted-data-only ÔÇö query/group/summarize with pipeline patched to raise; missing/optional fields ÔÇö hand-constructed minimal results (config/reports None) + non-reproducible filterable; invalid query params ÔÇö unknown sort-key/order/dimension strings + bad types + case-insensitive valid; registry integration ÔÇö round-trip/reflects-new-registrations/reflects-deletes/id-matches; reporting ÔÇö formatter sections/empty-input/no-best-without-evidence/leaders-shown; immutability ÔÇö frozen query-row/filter/group/summary/metric-leader).
Architectural invariants preserved: models ÔćÉ intelligence ÔćÉ pipeline ÔćÉ reporting ÔćÉ research ÔćÉ experiment ÔćÉ registry ÔćÉ query. No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files + reporting __init__ extension). No print() in engines/formatters (formatters return str; demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live/external-API. Persistence stays separate from query/analysis (the engine holds a registry reference and loads through it; never touches the filesystem or pipeline directly). Descriptive conclusions only; never declares a "best" without SUFFICIENT evidence; never claims historical results predict future performance. No Sprint 11AÔÇô11K regression.

## Sprint 11M ÔÇö Experiment Suite / Batch Orchestration & Analysis Layer (added)
New package `src/engine/suite/` sits ABOVE the query layer (dependency: models ÔćÉ intelligence ÔćÉ pipeline ÔćÉ reporting ÔćÉ research ÔćÉ experiment ÔćÉ registry ÔćÉ query ÔćÉ suite). The seventh orchestration package (after pipeline/reporting/research/experiment/registry/query); its `__init__.py` re-exports the public API. The suite layer implements NO trading/validation/pipeline/research/experiment/registry/query/comparison logic ÔÇö every value is read from the reused `ExperimentResult` objects produced by the existing engines. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11AÔÇô11L public API signature was changed, and all 738 prior tests still pass.
- Goal: make a *batch of experiments run together as a named comparison campaign* reproducible, identifiable, persistable and analysable as a unit ÔÇö WITHOUT rerunning the trading pipeline. Designed as orchestration infrastructure, not a trading strategy. The natural next layer after 11J (run one), 11K (persist one), 11L (query many): a declared, named, persisted SUITE binding member experiments together.
- `src/engine/models/suite.py`: frozen+slots suite models + `SUITE_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1` (separate from the experiment `SCHEMA_VERSION` because a suite manifest is a structurally different artifact: an ordered list of member experiment ids, not a full experiment record). Models: `SuiteReproducibilityMetadata` (suite_id, suite_configuration_hash, suite_configuration_representation, member_experiment_ids, member_count, code_version via importlib.metadata "UNKNOWN" never fabricated, reproducible structural), `SuiteSummary` (thin projection: counts + has_sufficient_evidence + REFERENCES the delegated Sprint 11L `ExperimentAnalysisSummary` and Sprint 11J `ExperimentComparison` + descriptive conclusions), `SuiteResult` (suite_id, config, members tuple by reference, reproducibility, summary, label, metadata; properties member_experiment_ids/member_count/has_sufficient_evidence/is_empty), `SuiteComparisonRow`/`SuiteComparison` (for comparing suites; best_suite_by_member_expectancy among suites with Ôëą1 SUFFICIENT member only; None otherwise).
- `src/engine/suite/config.py`: `SuiteConfig` (frozen+slots) ÔÇö immutable, deterministic identity. `label`, `members: tuple[ExperimentConfig, ...]` (ORDER IS SIGNIFICANT ÔÇö a re-ordered suite is a different suite), `metadata`, `seed`. `suite_id`/`configuration_hash`/`canonical_representation` are properties derived via the REUSED Sprint 11J `_canonicalize` helper (imported from `engine.experiment.config` ÔÇö no canonicalization logic duplicated). `suite_id = "suite-" + sha256[:16]`. Same config Ôćĺ same id; changing any member param, member ORDER, label, metadata or seed Ôćĺ different id. No timestamps/nondeterminism. Bare lambdas/evaluators rejected from identity (reuses the 11J rule).
- `src/engine/suite/exceptions.py`: `SuiteError` (base) + `SuiteNotFoundError`, `SuiteAlreadyExistsError`, `SuiteIntegrityError`, `UnsupportedSuiteSchemaVersionError`. No errors swallowed silently.
- `src/engine/suite/manifest.py`: `SuiteManifestPersistence(directory=None)` ÔÇö THIN filesystem-based persistence for suite manifests only (NOT a second full persistence system). One file per suite id, suffix `.suite` (intentionally NOT `.json` so the Sprint 11K `ExperimentRegistry.list()` `.json` scan excludes manifests ÔÇö a manifest must NOT pollute the experiment listing; this is regression-tested). Reuses the Sprint 11K `_validate_id`/`_SAFE_ID_RE` safe-id validator and the SAME atomic-write discipline (same-dir temp + flush + fsync best-effort + `os.replace`). Schema-version checked before any interpretation; future versions rejected with `UnsupportedSuiteSchemaVersionError`; filename-id mismatch / corrupted JSON Ôćĺ `SuiteIntegrityError`. Manifest stores ONLY: schema_version, suite_id, configuration_hash, label, ordered member_experiment_ids, metadata. The heavy per-experiment data stays in the existing Sprint 11K per-experiment records. Default directory reuses the Sprint 11K `default_registry_directory()` so suite manifests live alongside their member experiment records.
- `src/engine/suite/runner.py`: `SuiteRunner(experiment_registry=None).run(config, datasets=None, register=True, overwrite=False) -> SuiteResult`. ORCHESTRATION ONLY. For each member `ExperimentConfig`: runs via the EXISTING `ExperimentRunner.run()` (no trading logic duplicated); when `register=True` and the member id already exists in the registry (and `overwrite=False`), the existing persisted result is REUSED (loaded) instead of re-running (skip-already-registered ÔÇö makes re-running a suite cheap and idempotent); otherwise registers via the EXISTING `ExperimentRegistry.register()`. Custom datasets supplied by name via a `datasets` mapping (mirrors `ExperimentRunner`'s custom-dataset contract). Builds `SuiteReproducibilityMetadata` (code_version via importlib.metadata "UNKNOWN" never fabricated) and delegates the summary to `SuiteAnalysisEngine`, then augments descriptive conclusions. Graceful on INSUFFICIENT members ÔÇö the suite still completes with that member flagged; never raises merely because a member produced insufficient evidence. No print() in runner.
- `src/engine/suite/analysis.py`: `SuiteAnalysisEngine()` ÔÇö DELEGATES, no recomputation. `summarize(members) -> SuiteSummary` projects rows via the REUSED Sprint 11L query-layer helpers `_to_row` + `_sort_by_id` + `_summarize` (imported from `engine.query.query` ÔÇö zero duplication; evidence-safe leaders preserved exactly) and builds the member comparison via the REUSED Sprint 11J `ExperimentComparisonEngine.compare()`. `compare(members)` returns the delegated `ExperimentComparison` (None for empty). `SuiteSummary.analysis_summary` IS the reused `ExperimentAnalysisSummary`; `SuiteSummary.comparison` IS the reused `ExperimentComparison` ÔÇö both by reference.
- `src/engine/suite/registry.py`: `SuiteRegistry(directory=None, experiment_registry=None, manifest_persistence=None)` sits ABOVE `ExperimentRegistry` + `SuiteManifestPersistence`. API: `register_suite(result, overwrite=False) -> SuiteResult` (no silent overwrite), `load_suite(suite_id) -> SuiteResult` (reconstructs from persisted manifest + persisted member experiments via `ExperimentRegistry.load_many()` ÔÇö NO pipeline rerun), `exists`/`list`/`delete`, `summarize_suite(suite_id) -> SuiteSummary`, `compare_suites(ids) -> SuiteComparison` (loads suites from persistence, builds per-suite rows, descriptive best-suite-by-member-expectancy among suites with Ôëą1 SUFFICIENT member only; None otherwise). `load_suite` verifies every referenced member id exists in the experiment registry ÔÇö a suite is NOT resilient to member deletion by design (honest `SuiteIntegrityError` beats silent partial data). Integrity check (`_verify_suite_identity`) cross-checks suite_id == reproducibility.suite_id == config.suite_id (when config present); configuration hash cross-checked when both present; config may be None for a manifest-reconstructed suite.
- `src/engine/reporting/suite.py`: `SuiteReportFormatter().format(result) -> str` (sections: Suite Identity, Member Experiments, Suite Summary, Reproducibility, Conclusions), `SuiteComparisonFormatter().format(comparison) -> str` (suite comparison table + descriptive ranking + conclusions). Return str (no print()); descriptive-only language; insufficient evidence explicit; never claims historical results predict future performance. `src/engine/reporting/__init__.py` EXTENDED to re-export the two new formatters (reporting package convention).
- `src/engine/suite/__init__.py` re-exports: `SUITE_SCHEMA_VERSION`, `SuiteConfig`, `SuiteRunner`, `SuiteRegistry`, `SuiteAnalysisEngine`, `SuiteManifestPersistence`, `SuiteResult`, `SuiteSummary`, `SuiteComparison`, `SuiteComparisonRow`, `SuiteReproducibilityMetadata`, `SuiteError`, `SuiteNotFoundError`, `SuiteAlreadyExistsError`, `SuiteIntegrityError`, `UnsupportedSuiteSchemaVersionError`.
- `scripts/test_suite.py`: demo ÔÇö defines a 2├Ś2 suite (2 datasets ├Ś 2 lookbacks) as ordered ExperimentConfig members, runs via SuiteRunner (reusing ExperimentRunner + ExperimentRegistry), registers the suite manifest, lists/loads from persistence, summarizes and compares suites, prints formatted suite + suite comparison reports; finally patches `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate` to raise and re-runs load_suite/summarize_suite/compare_suites to PROVE the analysis uses persisted results only (no pipeline rerun).
- `tests/test_suite.py`: 90 tests (SuiteConfig identity ÔÇö determinism/reorder-changes-id/member-param/label/metadata/seed/canonical-representation/lambda-rejected/frozen/slots; SuiteRunner ÔÇö runs-every-member/references-not-copies/skip-already-registered-reused/overwrite-reruns/graceful-on-insufficient/custom-dataset/determinism/reproducibility-metadata/no-print; manifest persistence ÔÇö round-trip/schema-version-carried/rejects-future-schema/filename-mismatch/corrupted-json/missing/no-overwrite/overwrite/list/list-ignores-experiment-records/manifest-does-not-pollute-experiment-registry/delete/unsafe-id/default-dir-relative/atomic-no-temp/deterministic-bytes; SuiteRegistry ÔÇö register-load-round-trip/load-no-pipeline-rerun/reflects-new-registrations/reflects-deletes/register-no-overwrite/register-overwrite/missing-member-raises/load-missing/summarize-from-persisted/directory-shared; SuiteAnalysis ÔÇö counts/has-sufficient/no-sufficient-no-leader/delegates-to-query-summarize/comparison-delegated/empty-comparison-none/determinism/no-print; SuiteComparison ÔÇö two-suites/best-among-sufficient-only/no-best-when-none-sufficient/descriptive-language/empty/immutable/determinism; reporting ÔÇö sections/no-misleading-claims/insufficient-explicit/returns-str/determinism/empty-members/no-print-in-formatters/comparison-sections/comparison-empty/no-best-without-evidence; backward compatibility ÔÇö 11J/11K/11L APIs importable/experiment-schema-unchanged/suite-schema-separate/minimal-suite-result-handled; immutability ÔÇö frozen suite models + slots; package surface ÔÇö importable API + exception hierarchy + reporting re-exports).
Architectural invariants preserved: models ÔćÉ intelligence ÔćÉ pipeline ÔćÉ reporting ÔćÉ research ÔćÉ experiment ÔćÉ registry ÔćÉ query ÔćÉ suite. No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files + reporting __init__ extension). No print() in engines/formatters (formatters return str; demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live/external-API/database dependency (stdlib only). No global mutable state. No hard-coded absolute paths. Deterministic suite identity (no timestamps). Suite persistence is a THIN manifest over existing experiment persistence (manifest stores only suite identity + ordered member ids; heavy per-experiment data stays in Sprint 11K records). load_suite/summarize_suite/compare_suites operate ENTIRELY from persisted data (proven by patching the pipeline to raise). Evidence safety preserved: descriptive suite-level leaders populated ONLY when Ôëą1 member is SUFFICIENT; INSUFFICIENT is unobserved NOT zero performance; never declares a "best" without SUFFICIENT evidence; never claims historical results predict future performance. No Sprint 11AÔÇô11L regression.

## Sprint 11N ÔÇö Experiment Selection & Promotion Layer (added)
New package `src/engine/selection/` sits ABOVE the suite layer (dependency: models ÔćÉ intelligence ÔćÉ pipeline ÔćÉ reporting ÔćÉ research ÔćÉ experiment ÔćÉ registry ÔćÉ query ÔćÉ suite ÔćÉ selection). The eighth orchestration package (after pipeline/reporting/research/experiment/registry/query/suite); its `__init__.py` re-exports the public API. The selection layer implements NO trading/validation/performance/pipeline/research/experiment/registry/query/suite/comparison logic ÔÇö every value is read from the persisted `ExperimentResult` (Sprint 11J/11K) or `SuiteResult` (Sprint 11M) objects produced by the existing engines. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11AÔÇô11M public API signature was changed, and all 828 prior tests still pass.
- Goal: make a *defensible selection* among persisted experiments (or persisted suites) reproducible, identifiable, persistable and reportable ÔÇö WITHOUT rerunning the trading pipeline, the experiment runner or the suite runner. Historical experiment selection is DESCRIPTIVE ONLY and must never be presented as predictive; an experiment with insufficient evidence must NEVER be promoted.
- `src/engine/models/selection.py`: frozen+slots selection models + `SELECTION_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1` (separate from experiment `SCHEMA_VERSION` and suite `SUITE_SCHEMA_VERSION` because a selection decision is a structurally different artifact). Enums: `SelectionStatus` (NOT_ELIGIBLE/CANDIDATE/SELECTED/REJECTED ÔÇö the explicit promotion status making evidence gating STRUCTURAL, not merely reported), `SelectionType` (EXPERIMENT/SUITE). Models: `SelectionCriteria` (explicit, configurable, all-optional criteria ÔÇö `require_evidence_status`, `min_expectancy`, `min_total_r`, `max_drawdown_r`, `require_robust`, `require_oos_evidence`, `require_reproducible`, `min_completed_trades`; `is_default` property), `RejectionReason` (entity_id, status, reason, evidence_status), `SelectionCandidate` (read-only projection with metrics + `eligible` + `status` + `rejection_reason`; `has_oos_evidence` property), `SelectedResult` (entity_id, label, selection_type, rationale), `PromotionDecision` (promoted, selected, rationale), `SelectionResult` (selection_id, label, selection_type, criteria, candidates tuple, rejected tuple, selected, promotion, conclusions, metadata; `has_selected`/`all_evaluated`/`candidate_count`/`selected_entity_id` properties).
- Evidence gating (STRUCTURAL): INSUFFICIENT experiment Ôćĺ NOT_ELIGIBLE, can NEVER become CANDIDATE or SELECTED. PARTIAL experiment Ôćĺ NOT_ELIGIBLE, can NEVER become SELECTED (provisional only). SUFFICIENT experiment Ôćĺ eligible to become CANDIDATE. A suite with no SUFFICIENT member Ôćĺ NOT_ELIGIBLE (a suite is never selected merely because it contains a high-performing insufficient member). When no eligible SUFFICIENT candidate exists, `selected=None` ÔÇö a winner is NEVER manufactured.
- Missing evidence is NEVER treated as positive evidence: `require_robust=True` rejects `robust is None` (missing robustness) AND `robust is False`; `require_oos_evidence=True` rejects the absence of OOS evidence (`oos_expectancy is None` or `oos_trades == 0`); `require_reproducible=True` rejects `reproducible is False`.
- Candidate rules: a SUFFICIENT candidate becomes CANDIDATE when it satisfies ALL active criteria (logical AND); it becomes REJECTED otherwise with the specific failing criterion recorded as the rejection reason. Deterministic ranking priority among CANDIDATEs: (1) sufficient evidence, (2) robustness (robust first; missing NOT treated as robust), (3) OOS evidence (has OOS first; missing NOT treated as successful OOS), (4) expectancy (higher), (5) total R (higher), (6) lower maximum drawdown, (7) reproducibility (reproducible first), (8) entity id ascending ÔÇö DETERMINISTIC tie-break, NO random selection. Two identical persisted datasets always produce the same selected entity.
- `src/engine/selection/identity.py`: `SelectionIdentity(selection_type, criteria, label, metadata)` ÔÇö deterministic identity; `selection_id = "sel-" + sha256[:16]` of canonical representation (reuses Sprint 11J `_canonicalize` helper). Same identity Ôćĺ same id; changing criteria/type/label/metadata Ôćĺ different id. No timestamps.
- `src/engine/selection/core.py`: shared selection core (`_is_eligible`, `_evaluate_criteria`, `_rank_key`, `build_selection_result`) operating on the unified `SelectionCandidate`. Evidence gating + criteria evaluation + deterministic ranking + descriptive conclusion generation. No duplicated logic.
- `src/engine/selection/engine.py`: `SelectionEngine(registry).select(criteria, label="", metadata=None) -> SelectionResult`. Loads persisted experiments via `registry.list()` + `registry.load_many()` (persisted data ONLY; NEVER invokes `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate` or `ExperimentRunner.run`). Projects each `ExperimentResult` to a `SelectionCandidate` and delegates to the shared core.
- `src/engine/selection/suite_engine.py`: `SuiteSelectionEngine(suite_registry).select(criteria, label="", metadata=None) -> SelectionResult`. Loads persisted suites via `suite_registry.list()` + `load_suite()` (persisted data ONLY). Suite evidence status derived from members (SUFFICIENT if any member SUFFICIENT; PARTIAL if none sufficient but some partial; INSUFFICIENT otherwise). Suite representative metrics = metrics of its best SUFFICIENT member (ranked by the same experiment ranking) ÔÇö suite selection effectively ranks suites by their best eligible member, naturally respecting member evidence gating.
- `src/engine/selection/exceptions.py`: `SelectionError` (base) + `SelectionNotFoundError`, `SelectionAlreadyExistsError`, `SelectionIntegrityError`, `UnsupportedSelectionSchemaVersionError`. No errors swallowed silently.
- `src/engine/selection/serialization.py`: deterministic, self-describing JSON serialization (`__enum__`/`__dataclass__`/`__tuple__` type tags). `serialize_selection(result) -> str` (sorted-key canonical JSON), `deserialize_selection(payload) -> SelectionResult`, `parse_selection_header`, `canonical_selection_json`, `serialize_selection_bytes`.
- `src/engine/selection/persistence.py`: `SelectionPersistence(directory=None)` ÔÇö filesystem-based, JSON, one file per selection id (`<directory>/<selection_id>.selection`). The `.selection` suffix (intentionally NOT `.json` and NOT `.suite`) keeps selection decisions visually distinct and prevents polluting the experiment registry listing OR the suite manifest listing in a shared directory (regression-tested). Atomic write (same-dir temp + flush + fsync best-effort + `os.replace`); reuses Sprint 11K `_SAFE_ID_RE` with a selection-layer `_validate_selection_id` raising `SelectionError`. Schema-version checked before any model reconstruction; future versions rejected with `UnsupportedSelectionSchemaVersionError`; corrupted JSON / filename-id mismatch Ôćĺ `SelectionIntegrityError`. `default_registry_directory()` reused so decisions live alongside their source records.
- `src/engine/selection/registry.py`: `SelectionRegistry(directory=None, persistence=None)`. API: `register_selection(result, overwrite=False) -> SelectionResult` (no silent overwrite), `load_selection(selection_id) -> SelectionResult` (reconstructs from persisted data ÔÇö NO pipeline/runner rerun), `exists`/`list`/`delete`/`verify_identity`.
- `src/engine/reporting/selection.py`: `SelectionReportFormatter().format(result) -> str`. Required sections: Selection Identity, Selection Criteria, Candidates, Rejected / Ineligible, Selected Result, Conclusion. Returns str (no print()); descriptive-only language; insufficient evidence explicit; always states DESCRIPTIVE ONLY / not predictive / no live-trading readiness; selected shows NONE when no eligible SUFFICIENT candidate. `src/engine/reporting/__init__.py` EXTENDED to re-export `SelectionReportFormatter`.
- `src/engine/selection/__init__.py` re-exports: `SELECTION_SCHEMA_VERSION`, `SelectionEngine`, `SuiteSelectionEngine`, `SelectionRegistry`, `SelectionPersistence`, `SelectionCriteria`, `SelectionResult`, `SelectionStatus`, `SelectionType`, `SelectionCandidate`, `RejectionReason`, `SelectedResult`, `PromotionDecision`, `SelectionIdentity`, all 5 exceptions, `serialize_selection`/`serialize_selection_bytes`/`deserialize_selection`/`parse_selection_header`/`canonical_selection_json`.
- `scripts/test_selection.py`: demo ÔÇö runs 5 representative experiments (INSUFFICIENT / PARTIAL / 3 SUFFICIENT with differing robustness+OOS so ranking matters), persists them, runs selection with explicit criteria, shows candidates/rejected/selected, demonstrates suite-level selection (all-insufficient suite NOT selected; suite with a SUFFICIENT member selected), persists the selection decision, then patches BOTH `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate` AND `ExperimentRunner.run` to raise and re-runs experiment selection + suite selection + loads the persisted selection decision to PROVE persisted-only operation, prints a clear DESCRIPTIVE-ONLY warning. Demo exits successfully.
- `tests/test_selection.py`: 69 tests (evidence gating ÔÇö insufficient-cannot-become-candidate/insufficient-cannot-become-selected/partial-cannot-become-selected/sufficient-can-become-candidate/best-eligible-sufficient-selected/no-sufficient-none; criteria ÔÇö non-robust-rejected/missing-robustness-not-robust/missing-oos-safely/present-oos-satisfies/missing-reproducibility-safely/expectancy+drawdown-bounds/min-completed-trades/criteria-reproducible/different-criteria-different-id/default-no-constraint; ranking ÔÇö deterministic-tie-break-by-id/repeated/robust-before-expectancy/oos-before-expectancy/lower-drawdown/empty-registry; persisted-only ÔÇö no-pipeline-rerun/no-runner-rerun/reads-only-persisted; suite selection ÔÇö only-insufficient-not-selected/eligible-sufficient-selected/descriptive-only/insufficient-high-performer-not-selected/ranks-by-best-sufficient-member/no-pipeline-rerun; persistence ÔÇö loaded-without-pipeline/round-trip/register-no-overwrite/register-overwrite/list+exists/delete/load-missing/schema-version/future-schema/corrupted-json/filename-mismatch/unsafe-id/serialization-deterministic/default-dir-relative/does-not-pollute-experiment-listing; integrity ÔÇö no-insufficient-influence/selected-only-among-candidates; reporting ÔÇö required-sections/descriptive-only/none-when-no-selected/returns-str/deterministic/empty-input; immutability ÔÇö frozen-models/slots/criteria-frozen/result-frozen/re-serialize-loop; backward compatibility ÔÇö prior-apis-importable/schema-separate/id-prefix/identity-deterministic/criteria-change/type-change/existing-suite-apis-unchanged; package surface ÔÇö importable-api/reporting-reexport/exception-hierarchy).
Architectural invariants preserved: models ÔćÉ intelligence ÔćÉ pipeline ÔćÉ reporting ÔćÉ research ÔćÉ experiment ÔćÉ registry ÔćÉ query ÔćÉ suite ÔćÉ selection. No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files + reporting __init__ extension). No print() in engines/formatters (formatters return str; demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live/external-API/database dependency (stdlib only). No global mutable state. No hard-coded absolute paths. Deterministic selection identity (no timestamps). Selection persistence is a THIN artifact (`.selection` suffix, schema-versioned, atomic writes) over existing experiment/suite persistence. select/load_selection operate ENTIRELY from persisted data (proven by patching the pipeline AND the experiment runner to raise). Evidence safety preserved STRUCTURALLY: INSUFFICIENT never CANDIDATE/SELECTED; PARTIAL never SELECTED; suite with no SUFFICIENT member NOT_ELIGIBLE; missing evidence never treated as positive; no winner manufactured without SUFFICIENT evidence; INSUFFICIENT is unobserved NOT zero performance. Selection is DESCRIPTIVE ONLY ÔÇö never predictive, never implies live-trading readiness. No Sprint 11AÔÇô11M regression.

## Sprint 11O ÔÇö Price Action & Candle Pattern Intelligence (added)
This sprint is the first focused directly on TRADING INTELLIGENCE rather than experiment/registry/research infrastructure. It adds a deterministic, historical-safe candle / price-action pattern detection capability that complements (does NOT replace or parallel) the existing market-structure/liquidity/confluence pipeline. The experiment/registry/suite/selection infrastructure (Sprints 11JÔÇô11N) is NOT extended ÔÇö no new architectural management layer was introduced. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11AÔÇô11N public API signature was changed, and all 897 prior tests still pass.
- Goal: inspect OHLCV candles and produce structured pattern EVIDENCE that can later be combined with the existing market-structure/liquidity/confluence system. The engine makes NO profitability/predictive claim; a pattern is a description of candle shape only.
- `src/engine/models/candle_pattern.py`: frozen+slots candle pattern models. Enums: `CandlePatternType` (DOJI/HAMMER/SHOOTING_STAR/BULLISH_ENGULFING/BEARISH_ENGULFING/INSIDE_BAR ÔÇö the six-pattern initial catalog, no others), `CandleDirection` (BULLISH/BEARISH/NEUTRAL). Models: `CandleMeasurements` (range, body, upper_wick, lower_wick, body_to_range_ratio, direction ÔÇö all from a single candle's OHLC; zero-range candle handled safely with all-zero measurements + NEUTRAL direction; `from_candle` classmethod), `CandlePattern` (pattern_type, index, timestamp, direction, measurements, score, reason, prior_index/prior_measurements for two-candle patterns, confirmed ÔÇö always False in the base detector since confirmation needs future data which is never read).
- `src/engine/config/candle_pattern_config.py`: `CandlePatternConfig` (frozen+slots) ÔÇö explicit thresholds, no magic numbers in the engine: `doji_max_body_ratio=0.1`, `hammer_min_lower_wick_to_body=2.0`, `hammer_max_upper_wick_to_body=1.0`, `shooting_star_min_upper_wick_to_body=2.0`, `shooting_star_max_lower_wick_to_body=1.0`, `engulfing_strict=True`. Negative thresholds rejected at construction.
- `src/engine/intelligence/candle_patterns.py`: `CandlePatternEngine(config).detect(candles) -> list[CandlePattern]`. Stateless, deterministic. EXPLICIT definitions (documented in the module docstring and tested):
  - DOJI (single, NEUTRAL): `body/range <= 0.1` when range>0, OR zero-range candle (open==high==low==close). score = `1 - body/range` (zero-range -> 1.0).
  - HAMMER (single, BULLISH): `body>0 AND lower_wick >= 2*body AND upper_wick <= body`. score = `min(lower_wick/(2*body), 1)`.
  - SHOOTING_STAR (single, BEARISH): mirror ÔÇö `body>0 AND upper_wick >= 2*body AND lower_wick <= body`. score = `min(upper_wick/(2*body), 1)`.
  - BULLISH_ENGULFING (two, BULLISH): prior BEARISH, current BULLISH, `current.open <= prior.close AND current.close >= prior.open` AND (strict) `current body > prior body`. score = `min(current_body/prior_body - 1, 1)`.
  - BEARISH_ENGULFING (two, BEARISH): mirror. score same formula.
  - INSIDE_BAR (two, NEUTRAL): `prior.range>0 AND current.high <= prior.high AND current.low >= prior.low AND current.range < prior.range` (strictly smaller). score = `1 - current_range/prior_range`.
  - `score` is a deterministic SHAPE-QUALITY metric in [0,1] derived ONLY from explicit measurements ÔÇö NOT a confidence/probability.
- Historical / look-ahead safety (STRUCTURAL): a pattern attributed to index T uses only `candles[T]` (single-candle) and `candles[T-1]` (two-candle). No pattern reads future candles. The base detector NEVER sets `confirmed=True` (confirmation would need future data). Regression tests prove: (a) appending/mutating candles after T leaves pattern(T) unchanged, (b) detecting on `candles[:T+1]` yields the same patterns at T as detecting on the full series, (c) a two-candle pattern at T is unaffected by mutating candle T-2.
- Pipeline integration (ADDITIVE, behaviour-preserving): `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline` now constructs a `CandlePatternEngine` (config threaded through `PipelineConfig.candle_pattern_config`). At each evaluation index T it runs the pattern engine on the visible slice `candles[:T+1]`, keeps patterns attributed to index T, and attaches them to the `PipelineEvaluationPoint.patterns` tuple + accumulates into `PipelineResult.patterns`. The pattern evidence is NOT fed into the confluence/decision/signal engines, so existing signal/decision/validation behaviour is unchanged (regression-tested: signals_generated/completed_trades and per-signal prices identical to pre-11O; the pre-11O look-ahead signal regression still passes).
- `src/engine/models/pipeline.py` EXTENDED with two optional defaulted fields: `PipelineEvaluationPoint.patterns` (tuple, default `()`) and `PipelineResult.patterns` (tuple, default `()`). Both backward-compatible (existing test helpers construct these models with keyword args and never pass `patterns`).
- `scripts/test_candle_patterns.py`: demo ÔÇö builds a deterministic 9-candle sequence embedding one example of each pattern, prints every detection with measurements + reason, demonstrates historical safety (truncated-prefix == full-series patterns at T; mutating a future candle leaves pattern(T) unchanged), and demonstrates pipeline integration (pipeline emits patterns on evaluation points; signals/completed-trades unchanged). Explicitly states patterns are NOT signals and make no profitability/predictive claim. Demo exits successfully.
- `tests/test_candle_patterns.py`: 67 tests (measurements; zero-range; doji detect/non-detect + boundaries; hammer detect/non-detect + boundary; shooting star detect/non-detect; bullish engulfing detect/non-detect/strict/non-strict/index-zero; bearish engulfing detect/non-detect/strict; inside bar detect/non-detect/equal-range/prior-zero-range; direction classification; score properties; confirmed-never-true; determinism; look-ahead append/mutation/truncation/two-candle-only-T-1; config validation + defaults; immutability; pipeline integration result/point/coverage/config/empty; regression signals-unchanged/signal-prices-match-truncated/no-mutation/smoke).
Architectural invariants preserved: NO new orchestration package (this is intelligence, not an architectural layer); models ÔćÉ intelligence ÔćÉ pipeline unchanged in direction; the `intelligence/__init__.py` remains intentionally empty (import via `from engine.intelligence.candle_patterns import CandlePatternEngine`). No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files + two optional defaulted fields on pipeline models). No print() in engines (demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live/external-API/volume/order-block/FVG/supply-demand. No profitability/predictive claim ÔÇö score is a deterministic shape-quality metric only. Historical safety STRUCTURAL. No Sprint 11AÔÇô11N regression.


## Sprint 11P ÔÇö Market Context & Price Structure Intelligence (added)
This sprint builds the next TRADING-INTELLIGENCE layer (after Sprint 11O candle patterns): a structured, descriptive MARKET CONTEXT (swing -> structure -> trend/context -> range/consolidation -> support/resistance context) that can later be combined with candle-pattern evidence to identify interesting trade setups. Sprint 11P does NOT build trade selection, BUY/SELL signals, profitability prediction, ML, LLM, or live trading. The output of Sprint 11P is MARKET CONTEXT, not a trade. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-11O public API signature was changed, and all 964 prior tests still pass.
- Reuses EXISTING intelligence (NOT duplicated): SwingEngine (fractal swing detection), MarketStructureEngine (HH/HL/LH/LL/FIRST from confirmed swings), StructureAnalysisEngine (BULLISH/BEARISH/NEUTRAL/UNKNOWN bias), StructuralLevelsEngine (rich S/R lifecycle). Sprint 11P adds the genuinely-new pieces on top.
- New models `src/engine/models/market_context.py` (frozen+slots): enums MarketTrendState (BULLISH/BEARISH/RANGE/NEUTRAL/UNKNOWN), RangeState (IN_RANGE/NOT_IN_RANGE/UNKNOWN), PriceLocation (NEAR_SUPPORT/NEAR_RESISTANCE/INSIDE_RANGE/ABOVE_RESISTANCE/BELOW_SUPPORT/UNKNOWN); models SupportResistanceContext, RangeContext, MarketTrend, MarketContext. Optional fields use None/UNKNOWN so "unobserved" is never silently "zero/false". No business logic in models.
- New config `src/engine/config/market_context_config.py` (frozen+slots, validated): RangeDetectionConfig (min_swings, range_tolerance, min_flat_count, max_recent_structure_strength), SupportResistanceContextConfig (proximity_threshold, max_levels), MarketContextConfig (bundles sub-configs + recent_structure_count). No magic numbers in engines.
- New engines (extend `src/engine/intelligence/`, intelligence/__init__.py stays intentionally empty ÔÇö import via full paths, e.g. from engine.intelligence.market_context_engine import MarketContextEngine):
  - range_detection.py: RangeDetectionEngine(config).detect(structures, candle) -> RangeContext. Deterministic. Flat swing grouping checked FIRST: recent confirmed swing highs/lows grouped, a group is "flat" when every member within range_tolerance of the group average; >= min_flat_count flat highs AND flat lows -> IN_RANGE (boundaries = flat-high avg / flat-low avg, position = (close-low)/width clamped [0,1]). Otherwise directional-dominance suppression (consecutive HH+HL / LH+LL > max_recent_structure_strength -> NOT_IN_RANGE). Insufficient swings -> UNKNOWN. Flat-ness first because a flat consolidation IS a range even when degenerate equal-price structure labels fall back to LH/LL.
  - support_resistance_context.py: SupportResistanceContextEngine(config).analyze(structures, candle) -> SupportResistanceContext. Nearest support = swing low closest to price by absolute distance; nearest resistance = swing high closest to price. Signed relative distances (level-price)/price. PriceLocation: BELOW_SUPPORT / ABOVE_RESISTANCE / NEAR_SUPPORT / NEAR_RESISTANCE / INSIDE_RANGE / UNKNOWN (proximity within proximity_threshold). This is the CONTEXT layer (location/distance) atop the existing StructuralLevelsEngine (lifecycle) ÔÇö no level lifecycle duplicated.
  - market_trend.py: MarketTrendEngine().analyze(analysis, range_context) -> MarketTrend. DESCRIPTIVE, structure-first, DISTINCT from the signal-pipeline TrendEngine (BOS/CHOCH-driven, consumed by confluence/decision/signal). Rules: IN_RANGE range -> RANGE (overrides bias); BULLISH bias + structure_intact -> BULLISH; BEARISH bias + structure_intact -> BEARISH; NEUTRAL bias + structures -> NEUTRAL; otherwise UNKNOWN. Never modifies the signal-pipeline trend engine.
  - market_context_engine.py: MarketContextEngine(config, swing_config).analyze_at(candles, index) -> MarketContext (context at index using ONLY candles[:index+1]) and analyze_sequence(candles) -> tuple[MarketContext, ...] (walk-forward). Orchestrates existing swing/structure/structure-analysis engines + the 3 new engines into one immutable snapshot. recent_structure = the last recent_structure_count StructurePoints (oldest->newest) so later layers can inspect WHY the trend was classified.
- HISTORICAL / NO-FUTURE-LEAKAGE (STRUCTURAL, regression-tested): analyze_at(candles, index) feeds ONLY candles[:index+1] to every underlying engine. The existing SwingEngine confirms a swing at index i only after lookback candles to its right are present, so a swing whose confirmation_index > index is NOT yet emitted and cannot influence the context at index. Proven by: (1) analyze_at(prefix, T) == analyze_sequence(full)[T] for the descriptive fields; (2) mutating a future candle (incl. T+1) leaves context(T) unchanged; (3) every confirmed swing at T has confirmation_index <= T. The context at T is a function of candles[:T+1] only.
- Pipeline integration (ADDITIVE, behaviour-preserving): PipelineConfig EXTENDED with enable_market_context: bool = True + market_context_config. HistoricalEvaluationPipeline constructs a MarketContextEngine (only when enabled) and at each evaluation index T computes market_context_at_t = analyze_at(visible, t), attaching it to PipelineEvaluationPoint.market_context. The market context is NOT fed into the confluence/decision/signal engines, so existing signal/decision/validation behaviour is UNCHANGED (regression-tested: signals_generated / signals_validated / completed_trades / eligible_decisions / per-point signal_state+suppressed+decision_status+decision_direction identical enable vs disable; pipeline-point context == standalone analyze_at(prefix, T)). Disabling (enable_market_context=False) reproduces the exact pre-11P pipeline (market_context=None).
- `src/engine/models/pipeline.py` EXTENDED with one optional defaulted field: PipelineEvaluationPoint.market_context: MarketContext | None = None (backward-compatible; existing test helpers construct with keyword args and never pass it). PipelineResult unchanged.
- `src/engine/reporting/market_context.py`: MarketContextFormatter().format(context) -> str / .format_sequence(contexts) -> str. Stateless, deterministic, returns str (no print()). DESCRIPTIVE language only ("descriptive market context; not a trade signal or directional prediction"); never "predicted direction" / "profitable setup".
- scripts/test_market_context.py: demo ÔÇö deterministic bullish / bearish / range synthetic datasets; prints swing detection, market structure (HH/HL/LH/LL), trend/range/S-R context, sample evaluation points, the no-future-leakage proof (PASS: prefix/full-series agrees at T; PASS: future mutation leaves context(T) unchanged), and the existing-pipeline-behaviour comparison (signals/trades before==after, PASS). Explicitly states market context is NOT trade selection. Demo exits successfully.
- tests/test_market_context.py: 50 tests (swing detection via context; structure HH/HL/LH/LL bullish/bearish/mixed; trend bullish/bearish/range/neutral/unknown + range-overrides-bias; range obvious/directional/unknown/boundary-position/config-tolerance; S-R levels/distance-signed/location-near-support/above-resistance/below-support/unknown/deterministic; future-leakage prefix==full / future-mutation-unchanged / immediate-future-mutation / confirmation-timing-respected / out-of-range; integration attaches-context / disabled-none / signal-behaviour-unchanged / trade-counts-unchanged / per-point-identity / pipeline==standalone-prefix; determinism sequence/analyze_at/range; config validation; models frozen + recent_structure; reporting returns-str/sequence/no-predictive-language/deterministic).
Architectural invariants preserved: NO new orchestration package (this is intelligence + a pipeline integration, not an architectural layer); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged in direction; intelligence/__init__.py remains intentionally empty. No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files + one optional defaulted field on PipelineEvaluationPoint + additive PipelineConfig fields). No print() in engines/formatters (formatter returns str; demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live/external-API. Market context is DESCRIPTIVE ONLY ÔÇö never predictive, never a trade signal, never implies live-trading readiness. No Sprint 11A-11O regression.

## Sprint 11Q ÔÇö Market Setup / Confluence Intelligence (added)
This sprint builds the next TRADING-INTELLIGENCE layer (after Sprint 11P market context): a structured, descriptive SETUP / CONFLUENCE assessment that combines the existing candle-pattern evidence (Sprint 11O) and market-context evidence (Sprint 11P) into one interpretable view answering: directional bias, structural alignment, price location, candle support, count of agreeing evidence sources, and whether there is enough confluence to classify the point as a potential setup.
