## PRODUCT PHASE 4 ÔÇö RISK & TRADE PLANNING (added)
This phase is NOT another intelligence / scoring layer, NOT a new orchestration package, and does NOT reopen the completed 11A-12E architecture. It is a RISK / TRADE PLANNING layer that takes an EXISTING trade candidate / trade geometry and converts it into a disciplined, user-specific trade plan. The existing decision remains AUTHORITATIVE; the existing trade geometry remains AUTHORITATIVE; Phase 4 performs deterministic calculations AROUND those existing values only. It is NOT a market-analysis engine, NOT a decision engine, NOT a prediction engine, NOT a new scoring engine, NOT an evidence engine, NOT a strategy engine, NOT a broker, NOT an execution engine and NOT a paper-trading engine. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-12E public API signature was changed, no existing engine/model was modified (only additive new files + additive view model / service method / dataclass / route / template section / CSS + a `fmt_money` Jinja filter + the workstation route now accepts `account_capital`/`risk_percent` query params), and the prior 2805 tests still pass (now 2963 with 158 new in tests/test_trade_planning.py; fixture path remains fully deterministic).
- Goal: answer "If I choose to review/take this existing trade candidate, how much should I risk and what position size follows from my risk rules?". It must NOT answer "Will this trade definitely win?" It must NOT create BUY/SELL/ENTER/EXIT/HOLD recommendations.
- `src/engine/models/trade_plan.py` (frozen+slots): enums `RiskPlanStatus` (VALID/INVALID_INPUT/GEOMETRY_UNAVAILABLE/RISK_LIMIT_EXCEEDED/QUANTITY_UNAVAILABLE; `is_valid` property ÔÇö describes the RISK PLAN, NOT the market decision, deliberately DISTINCT from `ActionabilityState` and the Sprint 11S decision classification), `QuantityStatus` (DETERMINED/FRACTIONAL_ALLOWED/FLOOR_ROUNDED/UNSIZED). `QuantitySpec` (quantity_step, contract_multiplier, allow_fractional_quantity; validated positive; the repository has NO authoritative broker/exchange contract metadata so the safe generic default `DEFAULT_QUANTITY_SPEC` ÔÇö unit step, unit multiplier, fractional allowed ÔÇö is used by default and the plan surfaces `QUANTITY_SPEC_UNAVAILABLE`; no NSE lot size or broker-specific rule is hard-coded). `TradePlan` (plan_id, instrument, timeframe, direction, existing_decision, actionability, account_capital, risk_percent, maximum_risk, entry, stop, target_1, engine_risk_distance, engine_reward_distance, engine_risk_reward_ratio, target_2 [always None], target_2_supported [always False], quantity, planned_risk, planned_reward, quantity_status, risk_plan_status, quantity_spec_available, warnings, rationale, label, metadata; `is_valid`/`has_geometry` properties; `__post_init__` invariants: a VALID plan MUST carry directional intent + complete geometry + positive quantity + `planned_risk <= maximum_risk` [no-over-risk] + target_2 None; planned_risk requires quantity + engine_risk_distance; planned_reward requires quantity + engine_reward_distance). Numeric money values stored as `Decimal`; `_to_decimal` rejects bool/NaN/infinity.
- `src/engine/config/trade_plan_config.py` (frozen+slots, validated, MINIMAL ÔÇö NO strategy/optimization/scoring parameters): `TradePlanConfig` ÔÇö max_risk_percent=10 (safety guardrail; a risk% above this is INVALID_INPUT), min_risk_percent=0, allow_fractional_quantity=True, quantity_rounding_mode="floor" (ONLY floor allowed ÔÇö floor is the only mode guaranteeing `planned_risk <= maximum_risk`; round/ceil REJECTED), monetary_precision=2, label, metadata. The existing decision / actionability / trade-geometry / target-2 / evidence semantics are intentionally NOT configurable.
- `src/engine/intelligence/trade_planning.py` (`intelligence/__init__.py` stays intentionally empty ÔÇö import via `from engine.intelligence.trade_planning import TradePlanningEngine`): `TradePlanningEngine(config).plan(*, instrument, timeframe, account_capital, risk_percent, geometry=None, direction=None, existing_decision="", actionability="", quantity_spec=None, entry=, stop=, target_1=, risk_distance=, reward_distance=, risk_reward_ratio=, label="", metadata=None) -> TradePlan`. Pure, deterministic, stateless. All financial math in `Decimal`. The geometry may be supplied as a geometry object exposing the `GeometryView`/`TradeCandidate` attributes OR as explicit kwargs (explicit kwargs override). ACCOUNT RISK vs ENGINE RISK kept separate: `maximum_risk = account_capital * risk_percent / 100`; `raw_quantity = maximum_risk / engine_risk_distance` (risk per contract = `engine_risk * contract_multiplier`); when fractional allowed the raw value at full Decimal precision is used (planned_risk == maximum_risk exactly, never over-risks); when integer-only the quantity is FLOOR-rounded to the largest integer multiple of quantity_step whose planned_risk <= maximum_risk (floor is the only safe rounding mode). `planned_risk = quantity * risk_per_contract` (MAXIMUM PLANNED LOSS); `planned_reward = quantity * (engine_reward * contract_multiplier)` (POTENTIAL PLANNED REWARD ÔÇö deterministic, NOT an expected return). Long/short symmetric: risk = |entry - stop| for both directions. Validation never silently repairs invalid inputs: capital<=0 / risk%<=0 / risk% above max / below min -> INVALID_INPUT; missing entry/stop/risk_distance or zero/negative risk_distance -> GEOMETRY_UNAVAILABLE; non-directional -> GEOMETRY_UNAVAILABLE; smallest valid integer position exceeds maximum_risk -> RISK_LIMIT_EXCEEDED; no quantity spec supplied -> `QUANTITY_SPEC_UNAVAILABLE` warning + safe generic default. A defensive no-over-risk re-floor guards Decimal quantize edge cases. The public API takes NO candle/future-market-data argument.
- `src/engine/intelligence/trade_planning_serialization.py`: deterministic, self-describing JSON (`__enum__`/`__dataclass__`/`__decimal__`/`__tuple__` type tags), `TRADE_PLAN_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1`. `serialize_trade_plan`/`serialize_trade_plan_bytes`/`deserialize_trade_plan`/`parse_trade_plan_header`/`canonical_trade_plan_json`. `Decimal` values stored as strings so monetary precision survives the round trip. Round trip is LOSSLESS for every audit field (plan_id, instrument, timeframe, direction, existing_decision, actionability, account_capital, risk_percent, maximum_risk, entry/stop/target_1, engine_risk_distance/engine_reward_distance/engine_risk_reward_ratio, target_2 [None]/target_2_supported [False], quantity, planned_risk, planned_reward, quantity_status, risk_plan_status, quantity_spec_available, warnings, rationale, label, metadata). Schema-version checked before model reconstruction; future versions rejected with `ValueError`; malformed JSON / non-object / missing-plan-key raises `ValueError`. No `pickle`/`eval`/`exec`. Deterministic sorted-key bytes.
- `src/engine/reporting/trade_planning.py`: `TradePlanFormatter(precision=2).format(plan) -> str`. Stateless, deterministic, returns str (no print()). Required sections: TRADE PLAN header, INSTRUMENT, ACCOUNT RISK, TRADE GEOMETRY (reused verbatim), POSITION SIZE (Quantity, Quantity status, Maximum planned loss, Potential planned reward + the explicit note that planned reward is deterministic from quantity ├Ś reward distance and is NOT an expected return / NOT a prediction), STATUS, WARNINGS, RATIONALE. Every report ends with the explicit WARNING: "This is a deterministic risk calculation based on the supplied account parameters and the existing trade geometry. It is NOT a prediction or guarantee of future performance. The existing decision engine remains authoritative; this plan does NOT modify it and does NOT constitute a BUY/SELL/ENTER/EXIT/HOLD recommendation." No predictive/probability/buy-sell-enter-exit-hold/trading-recommendation language. Following 11O-12E reporting convention, imported via full path (NOT re-exported from reporting/__init__.py).
- Dashboard integration (ADDITIVE, behaviour-preserving): `dashboard/views.py` EXTENDED with `TradePlanView` (frozen+slots presentation projection of an already-computed `TradePlan`; `is_valid`/`has_geometry` properties) + `trade_plan_view_to_jsonable` (Decimal values rendered as strings for precision + parallel `_float` fields). `dashboard/services.py` EXTENDED with `TradePlanRequest` (frozen dataclass: instrument, account_capital, risk_percent, setup_timeframe, context_timeframe, quantity_spec, label, metadata) + `DashboardAnalysisService.plan_trade(request) -> TradePlanView` (ORCHESTRATION ONLY ÔÇö reuses `analyze` for the existing current analysis' geometry/decision/actionability, then delegates the deterministic calculation to `TradePlanningEngine`; the existing decision/geometry are reused VERBATIM, never recomputed; completed-candle boundary + no-look-ahead inherited from `analyze`; accepts NO future/future_candles argument; never calls OutcomeEvaluator/pipeline). `DashboardAnalysisService.__init__` now also holds a `TradePlanningEngine(TradePlanConfig())`. `_to_trade_plan_view` helper projects the plan model into the view (pure projection, no recomputation). `dashboard/app.py` EXTENDED with the `/api/trade-plan` route (GET; params instrument/timeframe/account_capital/risk_percent; reuses the existing current analysis' geometry verbatim; NEVER accepts arbitrary entry/stop/target; invalid inputs become INVALID_INPUT; returns structured JSON; never a BUY/SELL recommendation) + the workstation `/workstation` route now accepts `account_capital`/`risk_percent` query params and builds a `TradePlanView` when both are supplied (failure-isolated: an exception during planning is caught and the section is omitted, never raised). A `fmt_money` Jinja filter (Decimal-aware, 2dp, comma-grouped) added. `dashboard/templates/workstation.html` EXTENDED with a TRADE PLAN / RISK PLANNING section (form with account_capital/risk_percent inputs + "Plan risk" button; Account Risk / Trade Geometry / Position Size metric grids; rationale + warnings; explicit note distinguishing Maximum Planned Loss / Potential Planned Reward from an expected return). `dashboard/static/dashboard.css` EXTENDED (additive) with `.trade-plan-card`/`.trade-plan-form`/`.subhead` styles. Existing routes/sections unchanged.
- NO-LOOK-AHEAD (HARD REQUIREMENT, structurally enforced + regression-tested): (1) trade planning uses ONLY the existing analysis result (the geometry/decision/actionability from `analyze`); (2) the public `plan`/`plan_trade`/`TradePlanRequest` APIs accept NO `future`/`future_candles` argument (signature inspected); (3) `OutcomeEvaluator.evaluate` is NEVER called (patched-to-raise test passes for both engine + service); (4) `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate` is NEVER called (patched-to-raise test passes); (5) appending a future candle does NOT change a fixed-T trade plan (inherited from `analyze`'s completed-candle boundary); (6) a forming candle is never fed to the engine (inherited from `analyze`).
- DECISION AUTHORITY: the existing Sprint 11S decision classification (REJECTED/WATCH/QUALIFIED/PREFERRED) is reused VERBATIM on every plan ÔÇö never renamed to BUY/SELL/ENTER/EXIT/HOLD, never upgraded/downgraded. A valid risk plan must NOT upgrade a WATCH to QUALIFIED; a valid plan must NOT turn QUALIFIED into BUY (regression-tested). `RiskPlanStatus` is deliberately DISTINCT from market `ActionabilityState`.
- TRADE GEOMETRY PROVENANCE: entry/stop/target_1/engine_risk_distance/engine_reward_distance/engine_risk_reward_ratio are reused VERBATIM from the Sprint 11R `TradeCandidate` (reached via the reused scan decision). The plan NEVER recomputes a second entry/stop/target/R:R and NEVER invents Target 2 (target_2 = None, target_2_supported = False in the model, the API, the HTML, the formatter, and the serialization). Geometry-unavailable state surfaced honestly ("GEOMETRY_UNAVAILABLE"; no fabricated level).
- EVIDENCE SEPARATION: the `plan` API takes NO evidence argument; evidence is NEVER used to calculate position size and NEVER converted into a risk percentage (no "STRONG evidence = risk 2%" rule). The 11Y hard gate + UNAVAILABLE-vs-INSUFFICIENT evidence distinction are untouched (Phase 4 does not touch evidence).
- FINANCIAL NUMERIC SAFETY: all financial math in `Decimal`; binary floating-point never used for money; `_to_decimal` coerces int/float/str to Decimal and rejects bool/NaN/infinity. Rounding policy: `floor` only (the config rejects `round`/`ceil`); fractional quantities use full Decimal precision so `planned_risk == maximum_risk` exactly; integer-only floors to the largest integer multiple of `quantity_step` whose planned_risk <= maximum_risk. Tests cover exact division, fractional quantity, quantity floor, tiny/large risk distance, very small/large account, very small/maximum/invalid risk %, zero values, NaN/infinity, Decimal behavior.
- `tests/test_trade_planning.py`: 158 tests across areas A-AT (model validation; config validation; account-capital validation; risk-percentage validation; maximum-risk calculation; entry/stop/target preservation; existing R:R preservation; LONG sizing; SHORT sizing [symmetric, absolute distance]; quantity rounding [fractional-allowed, integer floor, exact-determined, quantity-step snap]; planned-risk [formula, never-exceeds-maximum, none-when-unsized]; planned-reward [formula, none-when-reward-unavailable, deterministic-not-prediction]; risk-limit enforcement; zero/negative risk distance; missing geometry; missing target [still sizes risk-only]; invalid numbers; NaN/infinity; deterministic ids [identical/different-capital/different-risk%/different-instrument/different-geometry/decimal-normalization/no-random]; shuffle/order independence [metadata order, repeated calls]; serialization round-trip [all fields, Decimal-preserved, bytes, deterministic, schema-version, invalid/geometry-unavailable plans]; malformed serialization [bad JSON, missing-plan-key, non-object]; future-schema rejection; input immutability [geometry not mutated, frozen model, no state leak]; reference/geometry preservation; decision preservation [all 4 classifications reused verbatim, WATCH stays WATCH, no BUY/SELL rename]; actionability preservation + risk_plan_status distinct; evidence separation [no evidence arg, quantity depends only on capital/risk%/risk_distance]; no-look-ahead [no future arg, evaluator-patched, pipeline-patched]; service no-look-ahead [plan_trade no future arg, evaluator+pipeline patched]; workstation integration [section, form, plan built]; API validation [invalid capital/risk%/above-max, no BUY/SELL]; API schema [all fields, target_2 unsupported, Decimal-as-string]; HTML rendering [warning text, no execution button, safe terminology]; error states [all 5 statuses]; target 2 unsupported; geometry unavailable; risk-plan warnings [quantity-spec-unavailable, geometry, risk-limit]; existing regression [all routes preserved, /api/trade-plan exists, health, pipeline baseline 4/3]; reporting [returns-str, sections, warning, no BUY/SELL recommendation, target 2, negative-precision-rejected, deterministic, invalid plan]; view model [jsonable, frozen]; financial rounding edge cases [exact, tiny/large risk distance, small/large account, decimal-not-float, floor-never-over-risks]).
- `scripts/test_trade_planning.py`: demo ÔÇö visibly proves all 22 required demonstrations (valid LONG plan; valid SHORT plan; account-risk calculation; maximum monetary risk; position sizing; quantity rounding [floor never over-risks]; planned risk; planned reward; existing R:R preservation; invalid account capital; invalid risk percentage; incomplete geometry; unsupported quantity specification; decision remains unchanged; actionability remains unchanged; evidence remains unchanged [separate]; target 2 remains unsupported; serialization round trip; deterministic ID; no-look-ahead [evaluator + pipeline patched to raise then restored]; existing workstation integration; pipeline baseline signals=4/trades=3) + a full trade-plan report. 59 PASS / 0 FAIL. Ends with "Product Phase 4 demo completed successfully." Demo exits 0.
Architectural invariants preserved: NO new orchestration package (risk planning is a calculation + reporting + dashboard integration, not an architectural layer); NO new intelligence/trading logic (every geometry value read from the reused Sprint 11R `TradeCandidate` via the existing `analyze`; the planner only calculates account-level risk/quantity around those existing values); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged in direction; intelligence/__init__.py remains intentionally empty; reporting/__init__.py NOT extended (formatter imported via full path, matching 11O-12E convention). No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files + additive view model / service method+dataclass / route / template section / CSS / Jinja filter + the workstation route accepts additive query params). No print() in engines/formatters (formatter returns str; demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live-trading/order-execution/paper-trading/autonomous-trading/portfolio-management. A trade plan is DESCRIPTIVE ONLY ÔÇö never a trade signal, never predictive, never a probability, never a guarantee of profitability, never implies live-trading readiness, never a statistical-significance claim, never a BUY/SELL/ENTER/EXIT/HOLD recommendation, never a replacement of existing decision/scoring/ranking/selection/risk-geometry/execution logic. The existing decision is AUTHORITATIVE (never upgraded/downgraded/renamed to BUY/SELL). Existing `TradeCandidate` geometry reused VERBATIM (never recomputed; no second entry/stop/target/R:R). Target 2 remains unsupported. 11Y hard gate + UNAVAILABLE-vs-INSUFFICIENT evidence distinction preserved (Phase 4 does not touch evidence). ACCOUNT RISK vs ENGINE RISK kept separate. No future leakage (planner consumes already-computed geometry only; proven by patching the outcome evaluator AND the pipeline to raise; regression-tested). Floor rounding guarantees `planned_risk <= maximum_risk` (never over-risks). Deterministic (no wall-clock, no randomness; canonical normalized inputs in the plan_id). Serialization round-trips losslessly. Existing pipeline regression unchanged (signals=4, trades=3); 12C/12D/12E/Product-Phase-1/Phase-2/Phase-3/dashboard demos unchanged (33/45/49/33/49/54/38 PASS). No Sprint 11A-12E / Product-Phase-1/2/3 regression.

## PRODUCT PHASE 5 ÔÇö PAPER TRADING & REAL-WORLD VALIDATION (added)
This phase is the FINAL PLANNED PRODUCT PHASE. No Product Phase 6 was created. This phase is NOT another intelligence / scoring layer, NOT a new orchestration package, and does NOT reopen the completed 11A-12E architecture. It is a PAPER TRADING / RECORDING / VALIDATION layer that records what the system's EXISTING trade opportunities would have done if followed in real / near-live market conditions. The existing decision remains AUTHORITATIVE; the existing trade geometry remains AUTHORITATIVE; the existing Product Phase 4 trade plan is reused VERBATIM. Phase 5 performs NO new position sizing, NO market analysis, NO decision logic, NO prediction, NO probability, NO broker, NO BUY/SELL/ENTER/EXIT/HOLD recommendation. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-12E public API signature was changed, no existing engine/model was modified (only additive new files + additive dashboard view models / service methods+dataclasses / routes / template / nav / imports), and the prior 2963 tests still pass (now 3077 with 114 new in tests/test_paper_trading.py; fixture path remains fully deterministic).
- Goal: establish a disciplined feedback loop ÔÇö SYSTEM OBSERVATION Ôćĺ SYSTEM DECISION Ôćĺ TRADE PLAN Ôćĺ PAPER TRADE Ôćĺ REAL MARKET OBSERVATION Ôćĺ ACTUAL RESULT Ôćĺ MEASURED VALIDATION ÔÇö WITHOUT risking real money and WITHOUT retroactively rewriting the original decision. It is OBSERVATIONAL VALIDATION ONLY.
- `src/engine/models/paper_trade.py` (frozen+slots): enums `PaperTradeStatus` (WAITING_FOR_ENTRY/OPEN/CLOSED/INVALIDATED/CANCELLED), `PaperExitReason` (TARGET_HIT/STOP_HIT/BOTH_TOUCHED/MANUAL_CLOSE/EXPIRED/NO_GEOMETRY/CANCELLED ÔÇö DELIBERATELY DISTINCT from Sprint 11W `OutcomeStatus`; `is_win`/`is_loss`/`is_ambiguous` properties). `PaperTrade` (paper_trade_id="pt-"+sha256[:16], instrument, timeframe, created_at, evaluation_timestamp, direction, existing_decision, setup_type, plan_id, entry/stop/target_1/target_2[always None]/target_2_supported[always False], engine_risk_distance/engine_reward_distance/engine_risk_reward_ratio, planned_quantity/planned_risk/maximum_risk/account_capital/risk_percent, status, entry_timestamp, actual_entry_price, exit_timestamp, actual_exit_price, exit_reason, realized_pnl, realized_r, bars_held, warnings, label, metadata, sequence; `has_geometry`/`has_target` properties; `__post_init__` invariants: target_2 None + supported False; CLOSED requires exit_reason+exit_timestamp+price/R per determinate/ambiguous rules; CANCELLED carries CANCELLED reason + no entry/price/R; INVALIDATED carries NO_GEOMETRY reason + no exit/price/R; non-terminal carries no exit state; CANCELLED has no entry). Numeric money/R values are `Decimal`.
- `src/engine/config/paper_trade_config.py` (frozen+slots, validated): `PaperTradeConfig` ÔÇö max_entry_bars=20 (positive), max_holding_bars=50 (positive), label, metadata, snapshot(). NO strategy/optimization/scoring parameters.
- `src/engine/intelligence/paper_trading.py` (`intelligence/__init__.py` stays intentionally empty ÔÇö import via `from engine.intelligence.paper_trading import PaperTradingEngine`): `PaperTradingEngine(config).create(...) -> PaperTrade` / `.track(trade, completed_candles, reference_now) -> PaperTrade` / `.close_manually(trade, exit_price, exit_timestamp) -> PaperTrade` / `.cancel(trade) -> PaperTrade`. Pure, deterministic, stateless. create reuses the existing decision/geometry/plan fields VERBATIM (accepts a `plan` object OR explicit kwargs); non-directional OR missing entry/stop/risk OR zero risk -> INVALIDATED (exit_reason NO_GEOMETRY; no fabricated levels). Entry rule (conservative, deterministic): a COMPLETED candle after creation touches the entry reference (LONG `low <= entry`; SHORT `high >= entry`); entry price = the planned entry reference; no touch within max_entry_bars -> remains WAITING_FOR_ENTRY (no fabricated entry). Exit rule (reuses Sprint 11W touch semantics): once OPEN, completed candles strictly AFTER the entry candle are watched; LONG target `high >= target`, stop `low <= stop`; SHORT mirror; same-candle both-touch -> BOTH_TOUCHED (ambiguous; a winner/loser is NEVER manufactured; realized_r/realized_pnl None); neither within max_holding_bars -> EXPIRED (mark-to-close at the last in-window close). A single track() call advances the trade through entry THEN exit on the available completed candles (entry candle consumed for entry; exits detected on subsequent candles only ÔÇö avoids fabricating which happened first on the entry candle itself). Terminal trades are returned UNCHANGED by track. Illegal manual-close/cancel transitions raise ValueError (never silently converted into success). Public API takes NO candle/future-market-data argument beyond the explicit completed-candles window + reference_now.
- `src/engine/intelligence/paper_trading_serialization.py`: deterministic, self-describing JSON (`__enum__`+`__enum_class__`/`__dataclass__`/`__datetime__`/`__decimal__`/`__tuple__` type tags), `PAPER_TRADE_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1`. `serialize_paper_trade`/`serialize_paper_trade_bytes`/`deserialize_paper_trade`/`parse_paper_trade_header`/`canonical_paper_trade_json`. `Decimal` values stored as strings so monetary/R precision survives the round trip. Round trip is LOSSLESS for every audit field. Schema-version checked before model reconstruction; future versions / malformed JSON rejected with ValueError. No pickle/eval/exec. Deterministic sorted-key bytes.
- `src/engine/models/paper_trade_performance.py` (frozen+slots): enum `PaperTradeGroupDimension` (INSTRUMENT/DIRECTION/DECISION/SETUP_TYPE/TIMEFRAME). `PaperTradeStatistics` (counts total/waiting/open/closed/cancelled/invalidated + wins/losses/ambiguous/expired/manual_close; rates win_rate/loss_rate; R metrics total/average/median_realized_r, gross_positive_r/gross_negative_r, profit_factor, valid_r_count; P&L total/average_realized_pnl, valid_pnl_count). `PaperTradeGroup`, `PaperTradeBreakdown`, `PaperTradePerformanceAnalytics` (analytics_id="perfpt-"+sha256[:16]; is_empty). DELIBERATELY DISTINCT names from the Sprint 11X `HistoricalPerformanceStatistics` / Sprint 11E `PerformanceAnalytics`.
- `src/engine/intelligence/paper_trade_performance.py` (`intelligence/__init__.py` stays intentionally empty): `PaperTradePerformanceEngine().analyze(trades, label, metadata) -> PaperTradePerformanceAnalytics`. Pure, deterministic, downstream-only. Consumes ALREADY-COMPUTED `PaperTrade` objects; inputs NEVER mutated. NO FABRICATED VALUES: win/loss rate = wins/(wins+losses) ÔÇö BOTH_TOUCHED/NO_GEOMETRY/INSUFFICIENT_DATA EXCLUDED (None when denominator zero); R aggregates computed ONLY over trades with a valid realized_r (TARGET_HIT/STOP_HIT/EXPIRED); BOTH_TOUCHED (realized_r None) EXCLUDED; profit_factor None when no valid negative R; P&L averages computed ONLY over trades with valid realized_pnl. Deterministic ordering (no unordered iteration; unavailable sentinel sorts last).
- `src/engine/reporting/paper_trading.py`: `PaperTradeFormatter(precision=2).format(trade) -> str`, `PaperTradeJournalFormatter().format(trades) -> str`, `PaperTradePerformanceFormatter(precision=2).format(analytics) -> str`. Stateless, deterministic, return str (no print()). Required sections distinguish SYSTEM DECISION from PAPER-TRADE RESULT. Every report ends with the explicit WARNING: "Paper trading is observational validation... does NOT guarantee future performance, does NOT constitute financial advice, does NOT place any real order... paper-trade results NEVER rewrite the original system decision and are NOT a BUY/SELL/ENTER/EXIT/HOLD recommendation." No predictive/probability/statistical-significance/buy-sell language. Following 11O-12E convention, imported via full path (NOT re-exported from reporting/__init__.py).
- `src/dashboard/paper_trade_store.py`: `PaperTradeStore(directory=None)` ÔÇö filesystem-based, schema-versioned JSON persistence, one file per paper-trade id (`<directory>/<pt-id>.json`). Atomic write (same-dir temp + flush + fsync best-effort + os.replace); reuses the Sprint 11K safe-id validator (regex) to prevent path traversal. API: save/load/load_all/exists/list_trades/delete/path_for. Loader validates schema_version BEFORE model reconstruction; future versions rejected; corrupted JSON / filename-id mismatch -> `PaperTradeIntegrityError`. Exceptions: `PaperTradeStoreError` (base) + `PaperTradeNotFoundError`/`PaperTradeIntegrityError`. `default_paper_trade_directory()` = `Path.cwd()/"paper_trades"` (no hard-coded absolute paths). No pickle/eval/exec; no database server; no cloud. Paper trades survive page refreshes / process restarts.
- Dashboard integration (ADDITIVE, behaviour-preserving): `src/dashboard/views.py` EXTENDED with `PaperTradeView`/`PaperTradeJournalView` (frozen+slots presentation projections; `to_paper_trade_view`/`paper_trade_view_to_jsonable`/`paper_trade_journal_view_to_jsonable`; Decimal P&L rendered as strings + parallel `_float` fields). `src/dashboard/services.py` EXTENDED with `PaperTradeRequest`/`PaperTradeTrackRequest`/`PaperTradeManualCloseRequest` (frozen dataclasses) + `DashboardAnalysisService.create_paper_trade`/`track_paper_trade`/`manually_close_paper_trade`/`cancel_paper_trade`/`load_paper_trade`/`paper_trade_journal` (ORCHESTRATION ONLY ÔÇö reuses `analyze`+`plan_trade` for the existing analysis/geometry/plan, then delegates to `PaperTradingEngine`; completed-candle boundary + no-look-ahead inherited from `analyze`; accepts NO future/future_candles argument; never calls OutcomeEvaluator/pipeline). `DashboardAnalysisService.__init__` now holds an optional `paper_trade_store`. `src/dashboard/app.py` EXTENDED with routes: `GET /paper-trading` (HTML journal+performance), `GET /api/paper-trades`, `GET /api/paper-trades/{id}`, `POST /api/paper-trades` (create from existing analysis+plan), `POST /api/paper-trades/{id}/track`, `POST /api/paper-trades/{id}/close` (manual), `POST /api/paper-trades/{id}/cancel`. `_build_default_service` wires a `PaperTradeStore` (overridable via `DASHBOARD_PAPER_TRADE_DIR`). `templates/paper_trading.html` (NEW) + nav link in `base.html`. ALL existing routes preserved.
- NO-LOOK-AHEAD (HARD REQUIREMENT, structurally enforced + regression-tested): the tracker inspects ONLY completed candles with `timestamp <= reference_now`; forming candles (timestamp > reference_now) and future-dated candles are EXCLUDED. The Sprint 11W `OutcomeEvaluator.evaluate` and `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate` are NEVER invoked to determine current paper-trade state (the touch logic is implemented directly in the paper-trading engine; proven by patch-to-raise tests). A previously-resolved (terminal) paper trade is NEVER altered by future candles. The public APIs accept NO `future`/`future_candles` argument (signature inspected).
- DECISION AUTHORITY: the existing Sprint 11S decision classification is reused VERBATIM on every paper trade ÔÇö never renamed to BUY/SELL/ENTER/EXIT/HOLD, never upgraded/downgraded. A LOSS never rewrites a QUALIFIED decision; a WIN never confirms it. The journal STRICTLY distinguishes SYSTEM DECISION from PAPER-TRADE RESULT.
- TRADE GEOMETRY / PLAN PRESERVATION: entry/stop/target_1/engine_risk_distance/engine_reward_distance/engine_risk_reward_ratio are reused VERBATIM from the Sprint 11R `TradeCandidate` (via the reused trade plan). The paper-trading layer performs NO new position sizing and never recomputes geometry. Target 2 remains `None` + `target_2_supported=False` (in the model, the API, the HTML, the formatter, and the serialization). NO_GEOMETRY trades carry no fabricated levels.
- AMBIGUITY HANDLING (preserves Sprint 11W semantics): same-candle both-touch -> BOTH_TOUCHED (a winner/loser is NEVER manufactured; realized_r/realized_pnl/actual_exit_price None); BOTH_TOUCHED is EXCLUDED from win/loss + R + P&L aggregates; NO_GEOMETRY is EXCLUDED from R/P&L. Observed zero never confused with not-available.
- DETERMINISM (no wall-clock, no randomness, no unordered iteration): the paper_trade_id = "pt-"+sha256[:16] of canonical (instrument/timeframe/direction/decision/setup/entry/stop/target/risk/reward/rr/quantity/planned_risk/capital/risk_pct/created_at/label/metadata/sequence) identity ÔÇö same inputs -> same id; different created_at OR sequence -> different id (so two separate paper trades from the same opportunity do NOT collapse into one record). Repeated + shuffled-input evaluation produce identical analytics ids. Serialization is deterministic sorted-key bytes.
- `tests/test_paper_trading.py`: 114 tests across areas A-AL (creation; invalid creation; state transitions; entry detection; waiting-for-entry; stop detection; target detection; manual close; BOTH_TOUCHED ambiguity; NO_GEOMETRY; missing geometry/target; decision preservation [all 4 classifications, LOSS does not rewrite decision, no BUY/SELL]; geometry preservation; plan preservation; Decimal accounting + realized P&L/R [LONG/SHORT/stop/target/expired-mark-to-close]; aggregate performance [empty/mixed/both-touched-excluded/profit-factor-none]; grouping instrument/setup/decision/timeframe; persistence [round-trip/reload-after-restart/corrupted/missing/unsafe-id/delete/empty-db/integrity-mismatch]; deterministic serialization [round-trip/bytes/canonical/schema-version/header/future-schema-rejected/malformed/closed-trade]; no-look-ahead [future-cannot-alter-resolved/future-candles-do-not-change-earlier-state/forming-excluded/future-dated-rejected/outcome-evaluator-patched/pipeline-patched/no-lookahead-through-aggregation]; input immutability [track-does-not-mutate/frozen]; failure isolation; workstation integration [create/journal/load-after-restart/cancel]; API schema [page/empty/create+get/no-buysell/existing-routes-preserved]; backward compatibility [service-without-store/methods-raise-without-store/intelligence-init-empty/reporting-init-not-extended]; ambiguous/unresolved serialization; regression [pipeline-baseline-4-3/trade-planning/production/backtest/robustness importable]; reporting [returns-str/disclaimer/no-predictive/negative-precision/deterministic]; deterministic id [same-inputs/different-created-at/distinct-sequence]; config [defaults/frozen/invalid/snapshot]).
- `scripts/test_paper_trading.py`: demo ÔÇö visibly proves all 22 required demonstrations (creation; valid/invalid states; entry tracking; stop detection; target detection; BOTH_TOUCHED ambiguity; P&L/R calculation; persistence; reload; performance aggregation; workstation/API integration; no-look-ahead [evaluator+pipeline patched to raise then restored]; future-candle protection; forming-candle protection; Decimal accounting; decision preservation [LOSS does not rewrite decision]; geometry preservation; plan preservation; target 2 unsupported; serialization round trip; deterministic ID; pipeline baseline signals=4/trades=3) + a sample paper-trade report. 92 PASS / 0 FAIL. Ends with "Product Phase 5 paper-trading demo completed successfully." Demo exits 0.
Architectural invariants preserved: NO new orchestration package (paper trading is recording/validation + reporting + dashboard integration, not an architectural layer); NO new intelligence/trading logic (every decision/geometry/plan value read from the reused Sprint 11A-12E engines via the existing `analyze`+`plan_trade`; the paper-trading engine implements only deterministic entry/exit touch tracking + Decimal P&L/R accounting around those existing values); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged in direction; intelligence/__init__.py remains intentionally empty; reporting/__init__.py NOT extended (formatters imported via full path, matching 11O-12E convention). No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files + additive dashboard view models/service methods+dataclasses/routes/template/nav/imports). No print() in engines/formatters (formatters return str; demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live-trading/order-execution/real-money/autonomous-trading/portfolio-management. A paper trade is DESCRIPTIVE ONLY ÔÇö never a trade signal, never predictive, never a probability, never a guarantee of profitability, never implies live-trading readiness, never a statistical-significance claim, never a BUY/SELL/ENTER/EXIT/HOLD recommendation, never a replacement of existing decision/scoring/ranking/selection/risk-geometry/execution logic. The existing decision is AUTHORITATIVE (never upgraded/downgraded/renamed to BUY/SELL); a paper-trade RESULT is a SEPARATE concern from the SYSTEM DECISION (a LOSS never rewrites a QUALIFIED decision). Existing `TradeCandidate` geometry + Product Phase 4 trade plan reused VERBATIM (never recomputed). Target 2 remains unsupported. BOTH_TOUCHED never fabricated into a win/loss; NO_GEOMETRY never produces invented levels; unavailable metrics remain None. No future leakage (tracker inspects completed candles only; OutcomeEvaluator + pipeline patched to raise; regression-tested). Deterministic (no wall-clock, no randomness; canonical normalized inputs in the paper_trade_id; shuffle-invariant analytics id). Persistence is schema-versioned JSON (atomic writes; safe-id; corruption fails safely; survives restart). Existing pipeline regression unchanged (signals=4, trades=3); 12C/12D/12E/Product-Phase-1/2/3/4/dashboard demos unchanged (33/45/49/33/49/54/59/38 PASS). No Sprint 11A-12E / Product-Phase-1/2/3/4 regression. This is the FINAL PLANNED PRODUCT PHASE; no Product Phase 6 was created.

## PRODUCT PHASE 5 — PAPER TRADING OPERATIONS (operational increment, added)
This increment is NOT another intelligence / scoring layer, NOT a new orchestration package, and does NOT reopen the completed 11A-12E architecture. It is a THIN, deterministic ORCHESTRATION layer (`dashboard/paper_trade_operations.py`) that turns the existing Product Phase 5 paper-trading capability into a controlled, real / near-live PAPER TRADING OPERATIONS workflow using the existing Product Phase 1 live-data provider + the existing Product Phase 3 workstation + the existing Product Phase 4 trade-planning layer. It is still PAPER TRADING ONLY — no real money, no broker orders, no live execution, no position management at a broker, no automatic financial transactions. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-12E / Product-Phase-1-5 public API signature was changed, no existing engine/model was modified (only additive new files + additive view models / service method+dataclass / route / template section / reporting formatter + the workstation route now accepts `account_capital`/`risk_percent` query params already added in Phase 4), and the prior 3077 tests still pass (now 3155 with 78 new in tests/test_paper_trading_operations.py; fixture path remains fully deterministic).
- Architecture (unchanged in direction): LIVE/NEAR-LIVE DATA → completed-candle boundary → existing DashboardAnalysisService/scanner → existing authoritative Decision → existing TradeCandidate geometry → existing TradePlan → Paper Trading Operations → existing PaperTrade lifecycle → persistent PaperTradeJournal → workstation/API/reporting. The operations layer is ORCHESTRATION ONLY.
- Reuses EXISTING components (NOT duplicated): the Product Phase 1 `DashboardDataProvider` (prefer `DASHBOARD_PROVIDER=yahoo` when available; deterministic `FixtureDataProvider` for tests; NO silent fallback from a failed live provider to fixtures during an operational run), the Product Phase 1 `split_completed_candles` completed-candle boundary, the Product Phase 3 `DashboardAnalysisService.analyze`/`scan_watchlist`, the Product Phase 4 `TradePlanningEngine`/`TradePlan`, the Product Phase 5 `PaperTradingEngine.create`/`track`/`close_manually`/`cancel` lifecycle, and the Product Phase 5 `PaperTradeStore` journal. The operations layer implements NO market-analysis / decision / scoring / geometry / position-sizing / prediction / execution logic.
- `dashboard/paper_trade_operations.py`: `OperationsConfig` (frozen+slots; account_capital, risk_percent, setup_timeframe, context_timeframe, label, metadata — NO strategy/optimization/scoring parameters); `OperationalStatus` enum (READY/STALE/NO_DATA/ERROR/NOT_READY — a SEPARATE concern from `ProviderStatus` and `FreshnessState`; never collapsed into one score); `InstrumentOperationResult` + `OperationsCycleResult` (frozen+slots); `PaperTradingOperations(service, config=None).run_once(instruments=None, reference_now=None, started_at=None, config=None) -> OperationsCycleResult` — ONE deterministic observation cycle. `cycle_id = "opcycle-"+sha256[:16]` of canonical identity. `OPERATIONS_DISCLAIMER` fixed non-configurable constant.
- Operational workflow (`run_once`): (1) resolve watchlist (None → `service.available_instruments()`; explicitly empty → empty cycle, honest not a fallback); (2) Phase 1 — advance ALL existing non-terminal (WAITING_FOR_ENTRY/OPEN) paper trades against completed candles CHRONOLOGICALLY (no look-ahead; `_track_one` uses `min(cycle reference_now, provider latest-completed)` so a caller-supplied reference_now is never bypassed); (3) Phase 2 — for each instrument (sorted, failure-isolated): fetch provider series, run `analyze`, determine eligibility, create a paper trade ONLY when `actionability == READY_FOR_REVIEW` (eligible + complete geometry + QUALIFIED/PREFERRED) using the EXISTING `create_paper_trade` path; NO trade for NO_OPPORTUNITY/INVALID/TRADE_GEOMETRY_UNAVAILABLE/insufficient geometry; duplicate prevention via deterministic trade id + `created_at = evaluation_timestamp`; (4) persist via the existing store; (5) return a structured `OperationsCycleResult`. NO infinite loop — `run_once()` is the core API (a continuous loop, if ever added, simply calls `run_once()` with a configurable interval).
- COMPLETED-CANDLE SAFETY (NON-NEGOTIABLE, structurally enforced + regression-tested): new paper trades use ONLY the latest COMPLETED setup candle; the forming candle never creates/changes/closes a trade; future-dated candles are rejected; `run_once()` accepts NO `future`/`future_candles`/`lookahead` argument; the Sprint 11W `OutcomeEvaluator.evaluate` is NEVER invoked (patched-to-raise test passes); the `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate` is NEVER invoked (patched-to-raise test passes); a fixed-T analysis is unaffected by appending a future candle (entry/stop/target/decision/status unchanged); only candles `<= reference_now` are processed.
- DUPLICATE PREVENTION (essential for operational use): running the cycle repeatedly against the same completed candle does NOT create duplicate trades. The same (instrument + timeframe + evaluation timestamp + existing opportunity identity) resolves to the same deterministic trade id; a duplicate is reported as `duplicate=True` + `duplicate_paper_trade_id` + `duplicates_skipped` count (not a new trade). Tested: same candle, same instrument, different instrument, different candle, different setup identity.
- ONE-CANDLE-AT-A-TIME / CHRONOLOGICAL: if multiple previously-unseen completed candles arrive after downtime/restart, they are processed in chronological order so an intervening target is never skipped (tested: entry → target → later stop resolves to TARGET_HIT, not STOP_HIT). Duplicate candle processing is idempotent.
- RESTART / RECOVERY: the operations layer survives process restart. On startup it loads existing WAITING_FOR_ENTRY/OPEN trades from the journal and continues tracking them without duplicating or resetting state (status/entry timestamp/actual entry/stop/target/realized R/realized P&L/exit reason/trade id all preserved). A restart does NOT reset any terminal state. Tested: create → persist → destroy service → recreate service → reload → continue tracking.
- FAILURE ISOLATION: one instrument failure (provider exception / malformed candle / empty response / unsupported timeframe / unsupported instrument / raised exception in `analyze`) never aborts the complete cycle; the failure is surfaced as an error and no trade is fabricated. The cycle continues with the remaining instruments.
- LIVE-DATA BEHAVIOR: prefer `DASHBOARD_PROVIDER=yahoo` when configured and available. If live data is unavailable, report provider failure honestly; do NOT create a paper trade from unavailable data; do NOT pretend the system is live; do NOT convert unavailable data into a successful analysis; NO silent fallback from a failed live provider to historical fixtures during an operational run. Provider status + freshness remain visible (separate concerns from operational status).
- DECISION / GEOMETRY / PLAN PRESERVATION (regression-tested): the existing Sprint 11S decision classification (REJECTED/WATCH/QUALIFIED/PREFERRED) is reused VERBATIM — never renamed to BUY/SELL/ENTER/EXIT/HOLD, never upgraded/downgraded; a paper-trade RESULT never rewrites the original system decision (a LOSS never becomes REJECTED; a WIN never becomes PREFERRED/BUY). Trade geometry (entry/stop/target/risk_distance/reward_distance/risk_reward_ratio/setup_type) is reused VERBATIM from the Sprint 11R `TradeCandidate`; the trade plan (account_capital/risk_percent/maximum_risk/planned_quantity/planned_risk/planned_reward) is reused VERBATIM from Product Phase 4; Decimal accounting preserved; Target 2 remains `None` + `target_2_supported=False` everywhere. Incomplete geometry → no normal paper trade (no fabricated levels).
- LIFECYCLE (reused verbatim from Product Phase 5): WAITING_FOR_ENTRY → OPEN (completed candle touches entry) → CLOSED (completed candle reaches stop/target). Same-candle both-touch → BOTH_TOUCHED (ambiguous; `realized_r`/`realized_pnl`/`actual_exit_price` NEVER fabricated). Neither within horizon → EXPIRED (mark-to-close). MANUAL_CLOSE is a human action. `ManualTradeConfig`/`PaperTradeConfig` reused unchanged.
- DETERMINISM (no wall-clock, no randomness, no unordered iteration): identical inputs (market data / provider response / watchlist / configuration / journal state) produce identical `cycle_id` + outcome. `cycle_id` hashes canonical (sorted instruments + setup/context timeframe + reference_now + provider + label + metadata + started_at). Instrument-order shuffle-invariant (instruments sorted before processing). Tests: same inputs → same outcome; shuffle invariance; repeated run identical.
- `dashboard/views.py` EXTENDED with `InstrumentOperationRowView` + `OperationsCycleView` (frozen+slots read-only projections) + `to_operations_cycle_view` + `operations_cycle_view_to_jsonable` (deterministic, JSON-safe; no internal Python objects exposed). `dashboard/services.py` EXTENDED with `OperationsRequest` (frozen dataclass) + `DashboardAnalysisService.run_paper_trading_cycle(request) -> OperationsCycleView` (ORCHESTRATION ONLY; delegates to `PaperTradingOperations.run_once`; caches the last cycle on `last_operations_cycle` for the workstation; accepts NO future argument; never calls OutcomeEvaluator/pipeline). `dashboard/app.py` EXTENDED with `POST /api/paper-trading/run-once` (returns structured JSON: status/cycle_id/started_at/completed_at/provider/freshness/instruments_scanned/trades_created/trades_updated/trades_closed/duplicates_skipped/errors/active_trades/warnings/results/rationale/limitations; NEVER a BUY/SELL recommendation) + the workstation `/workstation` route surfaces `last_cycle`. `dashboard/templates/workstation.html` EXTENDED with a Paper Trading Operations section (manual "Run Paper Trading Cycle" form + last-cycle metrics grid + per-instrument table; explicit "THIS IS PAPER TRADING. NO REAL ORDERS ARE SENT." banner). All existing routes preserved.
- `engine/reporting/paper_trading_operations.py`: `OperationalReportFormatter(width=60).format(result) -> str`. DUCK-TYPED (accepts `OperationsCycleResult` OR `OperationsCycleView` so the reporting layer keeps NO dependency on the dashboard package — the `models <- intelligence <- pipeline` invariant with reporting ABOVE models and BELOW dashboard is preserved; reads attributes defensively via getattr). Stateless, deterministic, returns str (no print()). Sections: Operational Cycle, Errors (failure-isolated), Warnings, Per-Instrument Outcomes (strictly separating OPERATIONAL CYCLE / SYSTEM DECISION / TRADE GEOMETRY / TRADE PLAN / PAPER-TRADE RESULT), Rationale, Limitations. Ends with the fixed `OPERATIONS_DISCLAIMER`. No predictive/probability/statistical-significance/buy-sell-enter-exit-hold/trading-recommendation language. Following 11O-12E convention, imported via full path (NOT re-exported from reporting/__init__.py).
- `tests/test_paper_trading_operations.py`: 78 tests across areas A-AJ (run_once happy path; live provider abstraction; fixture provider; completed/forming/future candle; trade creation; duplicate prevention; WAITING_FOR_ENTRY/OPEN tracking; STOP_HIT/TARGET_HIT/BOTH_TOUCHED [LONG+SHORT]; manual close compatibility; persistence; restart recovery; multiple unseen candles; chronological processing; instrument failure isolation; provider error; empty data; malformed data; unsupported instrument/timeframe; deterministic cycle; shuffle invariance; no-look-ahead [no future arg in signature, OutcomeEvaluator+pipeline patched to raise, future candle cannot change entry/stop/target/decision, only completed candles processed]; decision preservation [LOSS does not rewrite decision, no BUY/SELL rename]; geometry preservation; trade-plan preservation; Target 2 unsupported; API schema [no BUY/SELL, existing routes preserved]; workstation integration [operations section, last cycle]; reporting [returns str, disclaimer, no predictive language, negative width rejected, works on view]; pipeline baseline signals=4/trades=3; regression compatibility; operational status variants [ERROR/NO_DATA/READY/STALE]; restart state preservation; immutability). Tests use injected `_FakeProvider`/`_FakeService` — NO live Yahoo network dependency.
- `scripts/test_paper_trading_operations.py`: demo — visibly proves all 18 required demonstrations (provider abstraction + one cycle; completed-candle enforcement; trade creation; duplicate prevention; entry/stop/target tracking; BOTH_TOUCHED honesty; persistence + restart; multiple-candle recovery; failure isolation; no-look-ahead [evaluator+pipeline patched to raise then restored; future candle does not change fixed-T state]; decision/geometry/plan preservation [LOSS does not rewrite decision]; workstation/API integration; deterministic behavior) + a sample operations report + pipeline baseline 4/3. 50 PASS / 0 FAIL. Ends with "Paper Trading Operations demo completed successfully." Demo exits 0.
Architectural invariants preserved: NO new orchestration package (operations is orchestration + reporting + dashboard integration, not an architectural layer); NO new intelligence/trading logic (every decision/geometry/plan/lifecycle value read from the reused Sprint 11A-12E + Product-Phase-1-5 engines; the operations layer only orchestrates + projects); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged in direction; intelligence/__init__.py remains intentionally empty; reporting/__init__.py NOT extended (formatter imported via full path, NO engine->dashboard dependency). No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files + additive view models/service method+dataclass/route/template section/reporting formatter). No print() in engines/formatters (formatter returns str; demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live-trading/order-execution/real-money/autonomous-trading/portfolio-management/WebSockets/background-polling. Paper trading operations are DESCRIPTIVE ONLY — never a trade signal, never predictive, never a probability, never a guarantee of profitability, never implies live-trading readiness, never a BUY/SELL/ENTER/EXIT/HOLD recommendation, never a replacement of existing decision/scoring/ranking/selection/risk-geometry/execution logic. The existing decision is AUTHORITATIVE (never upgraded/downgraded/renamed to BUY/SELL); a paper-trade RESULT is a SEPARATE concern from the SYSTEM DECISION. Existing trade geometry + Product Phase 4 trade plan reused VERBATIM (never recomputed). Target 2 remains unsupported. BOTH_TOUCHED never fabricated into a win/loss; NO_GEOMETRY never produces invented levels; unavailable metrics remain None. No future leakage (completed-candle boundary structurally enforced + regression-tested for forming-candle exclusion, future-candle rejection, evaluator-not-called, pipeline-not-called, no-future-argument, fixed-T-stability, chronological processing). Deterministic (no wall-clock, no randomness; sorted instruments in cycle_id; shuffle-invariant). Existing pipeline regression unchanged (signals=4, trades=3); all prior demos unchanged. No Sprint 11A-12E / Product-Phase-1-5 regression.

## OPERATOR CLI — run_paper_trading_cycle.py (added)
- `scripts/run_paper_trading_cycle.py`: THIN operator-facing CLI running exactly ONE paper-trading operations cycle via the EXISTING `DashboardAnalysisService.run_paper_trading_cycle(OperationsRequest(...))` (Product Phase 5 operations). No new intelligence/decision/geometry/risk/lifecycle logic; CLI + formatting only.
- Provider: `os.environ.setdefault("DASHBOARD_PROVIDER", "yahoo")` — defaults to live/near-live Yahoo without overwriting an explicit value. Persistence: `PaperTradeStore(directory=default_paper_trade_directory())`. sys.path computed from `__file__` (works from any cwd).
- Args (all optional): `--instruments` (comma list, default NIFTY,RELIANCE,TCS,HDFCBANK,ICICIBANK), `--timeframe` (default 15m), `--capital` (default 100000), `--risk-percent` (default 1); capital/risk validated as positive finite decimals at the CLI boundary (kept as strings; Decimal coercion stays in the planner).
- Exit codes: 0 whenever the cycle executed (READY/STALE/NO_DATA; per-instrument failures are reported honestly, never treated as CLI failure); 2 for bad CLI args (argparse / empty watchlist); 1 only when the cycle could not be executed (import/service/runtime exception). Zero opportunities is exit 0.
- Report renders ONLY existing `OperationsCycleView` fields (provider/status/freshness/reference_now/scanned/analysed/per-row decision+actionability+direction+created/duplicate/reason/totals/errors/warnings) + the mandatory banner "NO REAL ORDERS ARE SENT — PAPER TRADING ONLY" (top + bottom).
- `tests/test_run_paper_trading_cycle.py`: 39 tests (args/defaults/validation, instruments parsing, provider-env defaulting, formatter sections/banner/duplicate/error/empty/determinism/no-recommendation-language, exit behavior, end-to-end fixture cycle, default store directory, deterministic cycle id, no-look-ahead [OutcomeEvaluator + pipeline patched to raise]).
- Environment note: the dashboard TestClient (starlette 1.6) needs `httpx` (or `httpx2`) installed to collect the API tests; `pip install -r requirements.txt` + `pip install httpx`.
- Full suite post-CLI: 3231 passed (39 new). Paper-trading demos unchanged: 92 PASS (test_paper_trading.py), 50 PASS (test_paper_trading_operations.py).

## PRODUCT PHASE 6A — HISTORICAL MARKET DATA FOUNDATION (added)
This phase is the FIRST increment of the historical-market-intelligence foundation. It establishes a reliable, deterministic, validated historical OHLCV data layer that will later support historical setup research and evidence generation (Product Phase 6B — FUTURE, NOT started). It is DATA FOUNDATION ONLY: it does NOT call the decision engine, does NOT generate trade candidates/decisions, does NOT create paper trades, and does NOT compute historical "evidence" (win rate / average R / profit factor / expected return / setup success probability). All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-12E / Product-Phase-1-5 public API signature was changed, no existing engine/model was modified (only additive new files + additive dashboard view model / service method / route / template / nav link + additive CLI + additive demo + additive tests + .gitignore/README updates), and the prior 3231 tests still pass (now 3329 with 98 new in tests/test_historical_data_foundation.py; the demo `scripts/test_historical_data_foundation.py` passes 15 checks; fixture path remains fully deterministic).
- Goal: Historical Provider → Historical Market Data Service → Canonical OHLCV → Validation/normalization → Historical dataset persistence → (FUTURE research/evidence layer). The historical layer lives in `engine/data/historical_*` (data/__init__.py remains intentionally empty — import via full paths). It reuses the existing `OHLCVCandle` and `DataValidator` semantics (no candle/OHLC logic duplicated).
- Models (`src/engine/models/historical_data.py`, frozen+slots): enums `HistoricalIngestionStatus` (AVAILABLE/EMPTY/PARTIAL/INVALID/ERROR), `ProviderResponseStatus` (OK/EMPTY/ERROR/UNSUPPORTED), `HistoricalDataError` (categorical issue reasons incl. NAIVE_TIMESTAMP/FUTURE_DATED/DUPLICATE_TIMESTAMP/UNORDERED/EMPTY_RESPONSE/MALFORMED_RESPONSE/UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUMENT/UNSUPPORTED_TIMEFRAME/PROVIDER_ERROR/INVALID_RANGE/MISSING_TIMESTAMP/MISSING_OHLC_VALUE/INVALID_OHLC/HIGH_BELOW_LOW/NEGATIVE_VOLUME), `GapKind` (POSSIBLE_MARKET_CLOSURE/UNEXPECTED_GAP — a VALID TRADING SEQUENCE is the ABSENCE of gaps). Models: `HistoricalDataRequest` (instrument/timeframe/start/end + optional provider/adjusted/metadata/allow_future_end; `__post_init__` rejects non-empty-instrument violations, naive timestamps, start>=end; instrument normalized strip+upper), `HistoricalDataIssue`, `HistoricalGap` (kind/previous/next/missing_count/span_seconds/reason), `HistoricalProvenance` (provider/instrument/timeframe/requested window/actual first+last accepted candle/ingestion_timestamp/records received-accepted-rejected with sum invariant/status/reason), `HistoricalFetchResult`, `HistoricalStoreResult` (added/existing/total/path), `HistoricalIngestResult`, `HistoricalDatasetSlice` (evaluation_time boundary; is_empty), `ResearchUniverse` (configurable allow-list, default NIFTY/RELIANCE/TCS/HDFCBANK/ICICIBANK; `__contains__` case-insensitive), `DEFAULT_RESEARCH_UNIVERSE`.
- Timeframe/timestamp utilities (`src/engine/data/historical_times.py`): `HISTORICAL_TIMEFRAME_SECONDS` canonical map (1m/2m/3m/5m/15m/30m/1h/90m/4h/1D), `canonical_timeframe` (aliases 15M→15m, 60m→1h, 1d→1D, unknown→None never guessed), `timeframe_seconds`, `supported_timeframes`, `normalize_to_utc` (aware→UTC; naive→None never silently accepted).
- Provider abstraction (`src/engine/data/historical_provider.py`): `HistoricalMarketDataProvider` Protocol (provider_name/is_available/supports/fetch(request) -> HistoricalProviderResponse with provider_name+status+candles+reason; failures reported via status, never raised). Providers: `InMemoryHistoricalProvider` ("in-memory-import"; caller-supplied records keyed by (instrument,timeframe); deterministic, no API key/internet), `DeterministicLocalHistoricalProvider` ("local-deterministic"; clearly-labelled synthetic series hashed from instrument+timeframe; out-of-the-box CLI/demo; NEVER used to fill validation gaps), `YahooHistoricalDataProvider` (OPTIONAL, reuses existing `YahooFinanceProvider.get_history`; graceful when yfinance/pandas missing; `resolve_symbol` isolated symbol map). Yahoo remains the LIVE/near-live provider; the historical provider is a separate additive path. Provider is replaceable without changing the research layer (service depends only on the protocol).
- Validation (`src/engine/data/historical_validation.py`): `HistoricalDataValidator.validate(records, instrument, timeframe, reference_now, allow_future=False) -> (accepted, issues)`. REUSES `DataValidator.validate_candle` semantics. Rules: naive timestamps REJECTED; aware normalized to UTC; future-dated (> reference_now) REJECTED unless allow_future; missing OHLC/timestamp/malformed records REJECTED; duplicates rejected (first kept); out-of-order sorted with UNORDERED issue; empty response reported EMPTY_RESPONSE; defensive malformed-response guard for non-OHLCVCandle records.
- Gap detection (`src/engine/data/historical_gaps.py`): `GapDetectionConfig(closure_seconds=2*86400, validated non-negative)` + `detect_gaps(candles, timeframe, config=None) -> tuple[HistoricalGap,...]`. Classification: missing expected candles whose timestamps are ALL weekend -> POSSIBLE_MARKET_CLOSURE; gap span <= closure_seconds -> POSSIBLE_MARKET_CLOSURE; else UNEXPECTED_GAP. Missing candles reported, never fabricated. NOT a full exchange-calendar subsystem.
- Persistence (`src/engine/data/historical_store.py`): `HistoricalDataStore(directory=None)` — one dir per (instrument,timeframe): `<root>/<INSTRUMENT>/<timeframe>/candles.json` + `provenance.jsonl` (append-only, one line per ingestion). Atomic same-dir temp + os.replace; safe-id regex prevents path traversal; schema_version=1 checked on load. Ingestion MERGES by candle timestamp → repeated ingestion is idempotent (added=0 on re-run). `load_candles`/`load_provenance`/`exists`/`list_datasets`/`delete`/`path_for`. `default_historical_data_directory()` = `Path.cwd()/data/historical` (git-ignored). Serialization (`src/engine/data/historical_serialization.py`, HISTORICAL_SCHEMA_VERSION=1): sorted-key canonical JSON for candles/provenance; no pickle/eval/exec. Errors: `HistoricalDataStoreError` (base) + `HistoricalDatasetNotFoundError` + `HistoricalDataIntegrityError` + `StoredDatasetInfo`.
- Service (`src/engine/data/historical_service.py`): `HistoricalMarketDataService(provider=None, providers=None, universe=None, store=None, gap_config=None)`. API: `fetch_historical(request, reference_now=None)` (provider → normalize+validate → gaps → provenance; request-level failures reported INVALID/EMPTY/ERROR with issues, never raised), `validate_historical`, `store_historical`, `ingest(request)` (fetch+store; persists AVAILABLE/PARTIAL/EMPTY, skips INVALID/ERROR), `load_historical(instrument, timeframe, start=None, end=None, evaluation_time=None)` (returns candles <= evaluation_time when set; LOOK-AHEAD PROTECTION). Provider registry sorted by name; default provider DeterministicLocalHistoricalProvider; universe validation; future-end rejected unless allow_future_end; outcome evaluator / historical pipeline NEVER called; no hidden future-candle parameter.
- No-look-ahead (regression-tested): future-dated candles rejected; evaluation boundary excludes later candles; same dataset at fixed T unchanged when future candles appended; public API signatures accept no future/future_candles/lookahead parameter; `OutcomeEvaluator.evaluate` + `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate` patched to raise — ingestion still works.
- Dashboard integration (minimal status surface per spec): `dashboard/views.py` EXTENDED with `HistoricalDatasetStatusView` (frozen+slots; provider/status/record_count/first/last/reason) + `historical_dataset_view_to_jsonable`. `dashboard/services.py` EXTENDED with optional `historical_service` constructor param + `historical_datasets()` + `historical_dataset_jsonable()` (empty tuple when no service/store — honest). `default_service(...)` EXTENDED with optional `historical_service` param (defaults to a local deterministic service with default store). `dashboard/app.py` EXTENDED with `GET /historical-data` (HTML) + `GET /api/historical-data` (JSON). Templates: `historical_data.html` (NEW table; empty state honest) + base.html nav link. NO win rates / trade recommendations shown.
- CLI (`scripts/ingest_historical_data.py`): THIN operator CLI over `service.ingest`. Args: --instrument/--timeframe/--start/--end/--provider (default local-deterministic)/--data-dir/--allow-future-end. Exit codes: 0 AVAILABLE/PARTIAL; 1 EMPTY/INVALID/ERROR/runtime; 2 bad args. Report prints INGESTION sections + validation checks (PASS/PARTIAL/FAIL), rejected-record reasons, gap summary, storage accounting.
- Demo (`scripts/test_historical_data_foundation.py`): 15 PASS checks (bounded ingestion, idempotency, provenance, validation rules, gap detection, look-ahead protection + fixed-T stability, multi instrument/timeframe, ordering invariance, empty response, dashboard surface, pipeline baseline 4/3). Exits 0.
- Tests (`tests/test_historical_data_foundation.py`): 98 tests across areas A-Z+CLI+dashboard (A provider contract; B request validation; C timezone normalization; D OHLC validation; E volume; F duplicates; G chronological ordering; H future-candle rejection; I empty response; J provider exception; K malformed response; L persistence; M idempotency; N reload; O provenance; P gap detection; Q multiple instruments; R multiple timeframes; S ordering invariance; T deterministic serialization; U no-look-ahead; V outcome-evaluator-patched-to-raise; W pipeline-patched-to-raise; X Yahoo live regression; Y existing path regression; Z pipeline baseline 4/3; CLI integration 4 tests; dashboard integration 3 tests). All network-free (injected fake providers / tmp_path stores).
Roadmap: Product Phase 6A — HISTORICAL MARKET DATA FOUNDATION — COMPLETE. Product Phase 6B — HISTORICAL RESEARCH CORPUS — see below (COMPLETE). Product Phase 6C delivered the corpus (the roadmap naming shifted: 6B foundation -> 6C corpus -> 6D setup/outcome research FUTURE).
Architectural invariants preserved: historical layer lives in engine/data (data/__init__.py stays empty); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged; no existing engine/model modified (only additive new files + additive dashboard fields/methods/routes/template + CLI/demo/tests + .gitignore/README). No ML/prediction/BUY-SELL/evidence-claim/trade-candidates/decisions/paper-trades from the historical layer. The LIVE path (Yahoo live provider + completed-candle boundary) is untouched. Existing pipeline regression unchanged (signals=4, trades=3).

## PRODUCT PHASE 6C — HISTORICAL RESEARCH CORPUS (added)
This phase builds the research-ready historical corpus ON TOP of the Phase 6B historical data foundation. It answers: "What did the market look like at a historical evaluation time T, using ONLY information actually available at that point?". It is RESEARCH PREPARATION ONLY: no trading strategy, no decision-engine change, no trade candidates, no paper trades, no evidence/outcome computation (Phase 6D — FUTURE, NOT started). All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-12E / Product-Phase-1-6B public API signature was changed, no existing engine/model was modified (only additive new files + .gitignore), and the prior 3393 tests still pass (now 3473 with 80 new in tests/test_research_corpus.py; demo `scripts/test_research_corpus.py` passes 16 checks; fixture path remains fully deterministic).
- Models (`src/engine/models/research_corpus.py`, frozen+slots): enum `CorpusPointStatus` (VALID/INSUFFICIENT_HISTORY/MISSING_DATA/DATA_GAP/INVALID; `is_usable`). Models: `CorpusDataQuality` (source/window counts, first/last, unexpected+closure gap counts, invalid_records, reused gaps/issues), `CorpusTimeframeSlice` (point-in-time slice; `boundary_inclusive` True for setup `<= T`, False for context `< T`; `evaluation_time` must be aware), `HistoricalMarketState` (the Phase 6D payload: setup/context slices + reused Sprint 11P `MarketContext` for setup+context + reused Sprint 11U `MTFAlignment` + latest-usable timestamps + `structure_unavailable_reasons`), `CorpusEvaluationPoint` (status + optional state + history_count + reason; VALID must carry state, non-VALID must not), `CorpusBuildIssue`, `CorpusInstrumentDataset`, `ResearchCorpusReport` (requested/loaded/missing instruments+timeframes, per-instrument quality, evaluation status counts with sum invariant, rejected_future_records, provider, storage_status, ingestion_version, issues), `HistoricalResearchCorpus` (corpus_id + datasets + evaluation_points + report; `valid_points` property).
- Config (`src/engine/config/research_corpus_config.py`, frozen+slots, validated): `ResearchCorpusConfig` — setup_timeframe="15m"/context_timeframe="1D" (canonicalized via the reused `canonical_timeframe`; context==setup rejected; `""` disables context), min_setup_history=10, min_context_history=1, sample_every=1, start/end (aware, start<end), skip_gapped_points=True, label/metadata; `snapshot()`. NO strategy/scoring parameters. The 51-instrument universe is NOT configured here — the corpus consumes the existing configurable `ResearchUniverse`.
- Engine (`src/engine/data/research_corpus.py`): `HistoricalResearchCorpusEngine(service, config, context_engine=None, alignment_engine=None)`. data/__init__.py stays intentionally empty — import via full paths. Public API: `evaluation_points_for(instrument, start=None, end=None, every=None)` (canonical candle-boundary grid; never arbitrary wall-clock timestamps), `evaluation_point(instrument, evaluation_time) -> CorpusEvaluationPoint` (explicit statuses; naive evaluation_time raises ValueError), `get_state(instrument, evaluation_time) -> HistoricalMarketState | None` (the Phase 6D research API), `build(instruments=None, label="", metadata=None) -> HistoricalResearchCorpus` (instruments default to the configured universe; shared grid = union of setup candle boundaries sampled every sample_every). `evaluation_grid(candles, start, end, every)` module helper.
- POINT-IN-TIME (structural + regression-tested): setup prefix = candles `<= T`; context prefix = candles strictly `< T` (an HTF candle at/after T is NEVER treated as completed); structure computed as "prefix up to T -> MarketContextEngine.analyze_at" (never compute-on-full-then-slice); prefix-corpus vs full-corpus agree at T; future mutation (setup + context) leaves state(T) unchanged; `OutcomeEvaluator.evaluate` + `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate` patched to raise — corpus still works; public API has no future/future_candles/lookahead parameter.
- Status gating per point (in order): missing setup dataset/empty prefix -> MISSING_DATA; prefix < min_setup_history -> INSUFFICIENT_HISTORY; context configured: no completed context candle -> MISSING_DATA, context < min_context_history -> INSUFFICIENT_HISTORY; skip_gapped_points AND unexpected gap in evaluated prefix -> DATA_GAP; else VALID. Skipped points carry explicit reason; never padded/fabricated.
- Serialization (`src/engine/data/research_corpus_serialization.py`, RESEARCH_CORPUS_SCHEMA_VERSION=1): `serialize_report`/`deserialize_report`/`canonical_report_json`/`parse_corpus_header` + `quality_to_dict`/`quality_from_dict`/`build_issue_*` (reuses Phase 6B `serialize_gap`/`serialize_issue`; local decoders). Lossless for the report. Metadata persistence (`src/engine/data/research_corpus_store.py`): `ResearchCorpusStore(directory=None)` — THIN manifest-only persistence (identity + configuration snapshot + report; NO candles/states/slices — the Phase 6B store remains the single candle persistence mechanism). `save(corpus, configuration=(), overwrite=False)`/`load`/`exists`/`list_manifests`/`delete`; atomic writes; safe-id; schema + filename-id integrity checks; `default_research_corpus_directory()` = `Path.cwd()/"data"/"research_corpus"` (git-ignored); exceptions `ResearchCorpusStoreError`/`ResearchCorpusNotFoundError`/`ResearchCorpusIntegrityError`.
- Reporting (`src/engine/reporting/research_corpus.py`, full-path import, reporting/__init__ NOT extended): `ResearchCorpusFormatter().format(corpus)` (Configuration/Coverage/Evaluation Points/Source+Storage/Issues/Rationale + DISCLAIMER) and `.format_point(point)` (shows `Latest usable setup candle (<= T)`, `Latest completed context candle (< T)`, `Future candles (> T): excluded`, reused structure + MTF alignment). Returns str; no predictive/BUY-SELL language.
- corpus_id = "corpus-" + sha256[:16] of canonical identity (CORPUS_VERSION + instruments + config + grid + label + metadata). Deterministic; instrument-order invariant.
Roadmap: Product Phase 6C — HISTORICAL RESEARCH CORPUS — COMPLETE. Product Phase 6D — HISTORICAL SETUP/OUTCOME RESEARCH — FUTURE (not started; will consume `get_state`/`evaluation_point`/`build`).
Architectural invariants preserved: corpus lives in engine/data (data/__init__.py stays empty); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged (engine/data/research_corpus.py imports Sprint 11P/11U intelligence — a one-way edge, no cycle); no existing engine/model modified (only additive new files + .gitignore). No ML/prediction/BUY-SELL/decision/strategy from the corpus. The LIVE path and the Phase 6B ingestion path are untouched. Existing pipeline regression unchanged (signals=4, trades=3).

## PRODUCT PHASE 6D — HISTORICAL SETUP RESEARCH (added)
This phase is the HISTORICAL SETUP RESEARCH layer on top of the Phase 6C corpus. It searches the corpus for historical occurrences of the EXISTING setup/structure architecture and evaluates what actually happened afterward: `Phase 6C corpus -> evaluation point T -> reused 11O candle patterns + reused 11Q setup confluence + reused 11R trade candidate + reused 11S trade decision (DETECTION, data <= T / context < T) -> reused Sprint 11W OutcomeEvaluator (OUTCOME, data strictly > T within forward horizon) -> reused Sprint 11X statistics + Sprint 11Y EvidenceStrength vocabulary (EVIDENCE)`. It is RESEARCH ONLY: no new trading strategy, no decision-engine replacement, no prediction/ML/LLM, no live/paper-trading behavior change. The existing decision architecture remains authoritative (a rejected / geometry-incomplete setup keeps its existing classification). All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-12E / Product-Phase-1-6C public API signature was changed, no existing engine/model was modified (only additive new files + .gitignore + docs), and the prior 3473 tests still pass (now 3564 with 91 new in tests/test_historical_setup_research.py; demo scripts/test_historical_setup_research.py passes 15 checks; fixture path remains fully deterministic).
- Models (`src/engine/models/setup_research.py`, frozen+slots): enum `SetupResearchStatus` (RESEARCHED / NO_OCCURRENCES / INSUFFICIENT_DATA / CORPUS_UNAVAILABLE / INVALID_REQUEST — a data problem is NEVER silently reported as "no setups exist"). Models: `SetupResearchRequest` (instrument [normalized strip+upper], setup_timeframe="15m", context_timeframe="1D", start_time/end_time [aware, start<end], forward_horizon>=1, minimum_history>=1, trend_filter/range_filter/mtf_alignment_filter/setup_type_filter/direction_filter ["" = no filter], label/metadata; deterministic — same corpus + request + config -> same result), `SetupOccurrence` (verbatim projection of the reused 11Q classification/direction/confluence + 11R candidate status/setup_type/geometry refs + 11S decision classification/score + 11P trend/range + 11U MTF alignment; geometry_available invariant), `SetupResearchObservation` (occurrence + reused 11W HistoricalOutcome, kept as SEPARATE concerns; is_completed/is_ambiguous properties), `SetupEvidence` (counts + reused 11X HistoricalPerformanceStatistics BY REFERENCE + reused 11Y EvidenceStrength; occurrence_count == completed+ambiguous+unresolved+no_geometry invariant), `SetupResearchResult` (research_id="setup-research-"+sha256[:16] of canonical request+config+corpus+occurrence identity; points_examined/valid_points/occurrences_detected/counts/observations/evidence/grouped_evidence/skip_counts/limitations; reproducible from corpus+request+config).
- Config (`src/engine/config/setup_research_config.py`, frozen+slots, validated): `SetupResearchConfig` — occurrence_classifications=("POTENTIAL_SETUP",) [validated against reused SetupClassification members], group_dimensions=("SETUP_TYPE","DIRECTION","TREND","MTF_ALIGNMENT") [RANGE_STATE also supported; ClassVar GROUP_DIMENSIONS], evidence sample gates mirroring 11Y semantics (min_sample_total=5/min_resolved=3/min_valid_r=3; strong_min_sample=20/strong_min_resolved=10/strong_min_valid_r=10; strong >= min validated); snapshot(). NO strategy/scoring parameters.
- Engine (`src/engine/data/setup_research.py`): `HistoricalSetupResearchEngine(corpus_engine, config, *, pattern_engine=None, setup_engine=None, candidate_engine=None, decision_engine=None)`. data/__init__.py stays intentionally empty — import via full paths. Public API: `research(request, *, label="", metadata=None) -> SetupResearchResult` and `detect(request) -> tuple[SetupOccurrence, ...]` (detection-only; auditable proof that detection has NO outcome dependency). NEITHER accepts a future/future_candles/lookahead parameter (signature-tested).
- POINT-IN-TIME (structural + regression-tested): DETECTION at T consumes only the corpus VALID point's prefix (setup <= T, context strictly < T — the 6C guarantee); OUTCOME measurement delegates to the reused 11W `OutcomeEvaluator(OutcomeConfig(max_holding_bars=request.forward_horizon))` which structurally inspects only candles strictly after T within the horizon. Tests prove: truncated-prefix detection == full detection at T; future-candle mutation inside the horizon changes the outcome but NOT detection at T; mutation beyond the horizon changes NEITHER; mutating a context candle at/after T changes nothing; `OutcomeEvaluator.evaluate` + `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate` patched to raise — detection still works.
- Occurrence detection reuses the existing chain VERBATIM: patterns at last index -> `SetupConfluenceEngine.assess` -> classification in config.occurrence_classifications -> `TradeCandidateEngine.generate` (close=prefix[-1].close) -> `TradeDecisionEngine.decide`. The request's minimum_history is an ADDITIONAL stricter gate (points below it counted in the explicit MINIMUM_HISTORY skip bucket). Request timeframes must match the corpus configuration else INVALID_REQUEST. Outcome candles come from the FULL stored series (the research window bounds detection points only; the evaluator's >T + horizon filter is the structural guard).
- Outcome methodology (all reused 11W): TARGET_HIT / STOP_HIT / EXPIRED (mark-to-close) / BOTH_TOUCHED (ambiguous same-candle both-touch — realized_r/exit_price None, never manufactured) / NO_GEOMETRY (incomplete geometry — levels never invented) / INSUFFICIENT_DATA (no forward candles). MFE/MAE absolute + R-normalized; realized R for determinate exits only.
- Evidence aggregation (all reused): per group `_compute_statistics` (11X) over the reused 11W outcomes; strength classification mirrors 11Y gates (sample size is a HARD GATE — small sample always INSUFFICIENT regardless of observed win rate); no expectancy/confidence/significance claims. UNAVAILABLE evidence = evidence None when no observations (never conflated with INSUFFICIENT).
- Serialization (`src/engine/data/setup_research_serialization.py`, SETUP_RESEARCH_SCHEMA_VERSION=1): `serialize_result`/`serialize_result_bytes`/`deserialize_result`/`canonical_result_json`/`parse_result_header`. Established `__enum__`+`__enum_class__`/`__dataclass__`/`__datetime__`/`__tuple__` tag convention; LOSSLESS round trip (incl. embedded 11W HistoricalOutcome/OutcomeSubject + 11X statistics + 11Y EvidenceStrength); schema version checked BEFORE reconstruction; future versions/malformed payloads rejected with ValueError; no pickle/eval/exec; deterministic sorted-key bytes.
- Persistence (`src/engine/data/setup_research_store.py`): `SetupResearchStore(directory=None)` — follows the 6C persistence architecture; one JSON file per result (`<dir>/<research_id>.research.json`); atomic same-dir temp + fsync best-effort + os.replace with temp cleanup; safe-id regex (path-traversal proof); no silent overwrite; load verifies schema + filename-id integrity (`SetupResearchIntegrityError`); `default_setup_research_directory()` = `Path.cwd()/"data"/"setup_research"` (git-ignored). Exceptions: SetupResearchStoreError/SetupResearchNotFoundError/SetupResearchIntegrityError.
- Reporting (`src/engine/reporting/setup_research.py`, full-path import, reporting/__init__ NOT extended): `SetupResearchFormatter(precision=2, width=72).format(result) -> str` — sections: header, Request, Evaluation Points (+ skip counts), Occurrences & Outcomes, Evidence (status/sample/completed/ambiguous/unresolved/geometry-unavailable/wins/losses/expired/win-rate/avg+median R/avg MFE/MAE), Evidence By Group, Observations, Rationale, Limitations, WARNING. Returns str; unavailable shown as "unavailable"; no predictive/BUY-SELL language; ends with the fixed disclaimer "Historical evidence is descriptive and observational. It is not a prediction, recommendation, or guarantee of future performance..."
- Demo `scripts/test_historical_setup_research.py`: 15 PASS checks (corpus build; evaluation range; occurrence detection; deterministic outcomes incl. geometry-unavailable explicit; evidence aggregation incl. insufficient-on-small-groups + regime grouping + deterministic filters; report rendering; no-look-ahead future-mutation [detection+outcome identical beyond horizon] + detection-has-no-outcome-dependency [evaluator patched to raise]; serialization + persistence round trips; pipeline baseline signals=4/trades=3). Exits 0.
