- Tests `tests/test_historical_setup_research.py`: 91 tests across areas A-L (request validation [valid/empty-instrument/naive-time/bad-range/bad-horizon/bad-min-history/invalid-timeframe-INVALID_REQUEST/timeframe-mismatch/context-disabled/unsupported-instrument-CORPUS_UNAVAILABLE/filter-flags/metadata-sorted]; occurrence detection [detected/no-occurrence/multiple-deterministic-order/repeated-identical/watch-not-default/watch-when-configured/decision-preserved-no-silent-promotion]; point-in-time [prefix<=T/future-mutation-changes-outcome-not-detection/beyond-horizon-unchanged/context-strictly-<T/no-forbidden-API-params/detection-no-outcome-dependency/detection-no-pipeline-dependency]; outcome [target-hit/stop-hit/insufficient-data/expired/both-touched-ambiguous/missing-geometry-explicit/MFE-MAE/risk]; aggregation [sample-size/count-invariants/averages+median/insufficient-marked/unavailable-when-empty/strength-gates-config/statistics-reused-by-reference]; regime filtering [direction match+non-match/setup-type/trend/multiple-AND/deterministic/mtf]; serialization [round-trip/bytes/deterministic/canonical/header/future-schema/malformed/missing-body/empty-result]; store [round-trip/exists+list/no-silent-overwrite/overwrite/missing/delete/integrity-mismatch/unsafe-id/default-dir]; corpus interaction [missing dataset/empty dataset/insufficient history/stricter minimum/gap skips]; reporting [returns-str/sections/disclaimer/no-fabricated-metrics-language/empty/deterministic/validation]; model validation [geometry invariant/naive-time/evidence-count/result-count/frozen+slots/status-properties]; regression [pipeline baseline 4/3/deterministic research id/different-request-different-id/observations==detect/store-suffix-separation]).
Roadmap: Product Phase 6D — HISTORICAL SETUP RESEARCH — COMPLETE. Product Phase 6E — HISTORICAL + CURRENT INTELLIGENCE — COMPLETE (see below). Phase 6F (Live Paper Validation) remains FUTURE (not started; 6F will consume the 6E historical-context integration).
Architectural invariants preserved: research lives in engine/data (data/__init__.py stays empty); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged in direction (engine/data/setup_research.py imports 11O/11Q/11R/11S/11W/11X/11Y intelligence — a one-way edge, no cycle, same as 6C); no existing engine/model modified (only additive new files + .gitignore + docs + AGENTS.md). No ML/prediction/BUY-SELL/new-strategy/decision-rewrite. The LIVE path (Yahoo live provider + completed-candle boundary + paper-trading operations) is untouched. Phase 6A/6B/6C behavior unchanged (demos: 15/16 PASS intact). Existing pipeline regression unchanged (signals=4, trades=3).

## PRODUCT PHASE 6E — HISTORICAL + CURRENT INTELLIGENCE (added)
This phase integrates the completed Phase 6D historical setup research output with the existing current-market analysis as DESCRIPTIVE historical context. It is an EVIDENCE INTEGRATION layer, NOT a new trading decision engine: the existing decision architecture remains AUTHORITATIVE; historical evidence NEVER creates/modifies/upgrades/downgrades/overrides the existing decision classification, actionability, direction, geometry, trade plan or paper-trading eligibility. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-12E / Product-Phase-1-6D public API signature was changed, no existing engine/model was modified (only additive new files + additive dashboard view/model + additive DashboardTradeView field + additive to_jsonable key + additive service/source + additive tests/demo/docs), and the prior 3564 tests still pass (now 3641 with 77 new in tests/test_historical_evidence_context.py; demo scripts/test_historical_evidence_context.py passes 14 checks; fixture path remains fully deterministic).
- Architecture: Phase 6C corpus -> Phase 6D research (PERSISTED via SetupResearchStore) -> Phase 6E historical evidence lookup -> current market assessment -> combined current + historical intelligence view. The lookup NEVER re-runs the 6D research process (detection/outcome/aggregation), never reads candles, and has no future/future_candles/lookahead parameter (works with OutcomeEvaluator + pipeline patched to raise, regression-tested).
- Models (`src/engine/models/historical_context.py`, frozen+slots): `HistoricalEvidenceRequest` (matching criteria at T: instrument/timeframes/evaluation_time [aware] + optional setup_type/direction/trend_state/range_state/mtf_alignment; empty = no filter; match_dimensions/match_key properties), `HistoricalContextStatus` (AVAILABLE/NO_MATCH/RESEARCH_UNAVAILABLE — an AVAILABILITY status, NOT a competing strength enum), `HistoricalEvidenceContext` (context_id="hectx-"+sha256[:16]; counts + reused 11X statistics [None when unavailable] + reused 11Y EvidenceStrength [None when unavailable] + research_ids provenance + fixed HISTORICAL_CONTEXT_LIMITATIONS; __post_init__ invariants: AVAILABLE must carry statistics+strength+>=1 occurrence, non-AVAILABLE must carry none).
- Engine (`src/engine/data/historical_evidence_lookup.py`): `HistoricalEvidenceLookupEngine(store=None, config=None).lookup(request, label, metadata) -> HistoricalEvidenceContext`. data/__init__.py stays intentionally empty. POINT-IN-TIME (structural): occurrence eligible only when occurrence.evaluation_time < T; contributes to statistics/strength only when the reused 11W outcome was already known at T (outcome_timestamp <= T, or terminal outcome with no timestamp — BOTH_TOUCHED/EXPIRED/NO_GEOMETRY/INSUFFICIENT_DATA). Matching reuses the 6D vocabulary with logical AND; timeframe comparison canonicalized (15M == 15m). Statistics = reused 11X `_compute_statistics` over matched already-known outcomes; strength = reused 11Y EvidenceStrength via the SetupResearchConfig sample gates (sample size is a HARD GATE). NO_MATCH vs RESEARCH_UNAVAILABLE distinguished (research exists vs no research for pair); non-RESEARCHED results carry no usable observations. Reuses the SetupResearchStore only — NO new store/serializer (the 6D store is the persistence mechanism; the context reuses objects by value, nothing fabricated).
- Reporting (`src/engine/reporting/historical_evidence_lookup.py`): `HistoricalEvidenceContextFormatter(precision=2, width=72).format(context) -> str`. Full-path import (reporting/__init__.py NOT extended). Sections: Context Identity / Current Assessment / Evidence Availability / Occurrences / Historical Outcome Statistics / Provenance / Rationale / Limitations + WARNING. Returns str; unavailable shown as "unavailable"; no predictive/BUY-SELL language.
- Dashboard integration (additive): `dashboard/views.py` EXTENDED with `HistoricalContextView` (frozen+slots; honest UNAVAILABLE sentinels) + `historical_context_view_to_jsonable`; `DashboardTradeView.historical_context: HistoricalContextView` additive defaulted field (backward-compatible); `to_jsonable` gains an additive `historical_context` block (existing keys/fields unchanged). `dashboard/services.py` EXTENDED with `HistoricalEvidenceSource(store=None, lookup_engine=None, config=None).context_for(request, setup_tf, ctx_tf, result, candidate) -> HistoricalContextView` (builds the lookup request from the current scan result VERBATIM, cleans ""/NONE/UNKNOWN labels to no-filter; no-look-ahead); `DashboardAnalysisService.__init__` gains optional `historical_evidence_source`; `_build_view` attaches the context failure-isolated (a lookup failure -> honest UNAVAILABLE view, never corrupts the current assessment); `default_service` gains the optional passthrough. `dashboard/__init__.py` untouched. NO template changes needed (JSON/API surface).
- Decision authority (regression-tested): REJECTED + STRONG evidence -> REJECTED (paper trades_created == 0); NO_OPPORTUNITY + evidence -> NO_OPPORTUNITY; QUALIFIED/READY actionability + UNAVAILABLE -> unchanged. The full current view (decision/actionability/geometry/market_overview/warnings) is IDENTICAL with and without the evidence source; operations cycles are byte-identical with and without evidence (trades_created/updated/closed/results equal). Historical evidence never bypasses PaperTradingOperations.
- Geometry preserved (regression-tested): entry/stop/target/risk/reward/R:R/target-2 (None + target_2_supported=False) reused VERBATIM; evidence NEVER fabricates entry/stop/target (incomplete geometry stays incomplete); no entry/stop/target is derived from historical statistics.
- `tests/test_historical_evidence_context.py`: 77 tests across areas A-J (model validation; availability [matching/no-match/research-unavailable/insufficient/11Y-vocabulary/statistics-preserved]; matching [setup_type/direction/trend/range/mtf/AND-matching/canonical-timeframe/instrument-isolation/timeframe-mismatch]; point-in-time [at-T excluded/after-T excluded/unresolved-at-T excluded/all-unresolved NO_MATCH/ambiguous/expired/no-geometry/no-future-param/determinism/evaluator-patched/pipeline-patched/monotonic-history/id-differs-by-T/no-mutation]; strength gates [weak/moderate/strong/count-invariants]; reporting; dashboard integration [unavailable-no-source/attached/additive-json/existing-keys/failure-isolated/REJECTED+STRONG/NO_OPPORTUNITY/QUALIFIED+UNAVAILABLE/decision-unchanged/no-geometry-fabrication/frozen/passthrough]; paper trading [REJECTED+STRONG->0 trades/cycle-identical/pipeline-baseline-4-3]; backward compat [defaults/api-additive/api-without-source/source-no-store/no-reexport]; end-to-end determinism/match-key/provenance).
- `scripts/test_historical_evidence_context.py`: demo — 14 PASS checks (current assessment; evidence lookup; comparable occurrence count; outcome statistics where available; evidence strength reused vocabulary; evidence unavailable honest; REJECTED+STRONG stays REJECTED; evidence attachment never alters decision; no geometry fabrication; REJECTED+STRONG creates NO paper trade; point-in-time boundary enforced; lookup consumes persisted research only [evaluator patched]; API payload additive; pipeline baseline 4/3). Fully offline (fixture data + temp stores). Exits 0.
- `docs/phase_6e_historical_current_intelligence`: concise documentation (what 6E does; evidence meaning; matching; point-in-time guarantees; strength interpretation; why evidence never overrides the decision; why evidence is not prediction; 6C/6D/6E relationship; what remains for 6F; limitations; non-goals).
Roadmap: Product Phase 6E — HISTORICAL + CURRENT INTELLIGENCE — COMPLETE. Product Phase 6F — LIVE PAPER VALIDATION — COMPLETE (see below).
Architectural invariants preserved: lookup lives in engine/data (data/__init__.py stays empty); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged in direction; intelligence/__init__.py remains intentionally empty; reporting/__init__.py NOT extended (formatter via full path). No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files + additive dashboard field/blocks + additive tests/demo/docs). No ML/prediction/BUY-SELL/decision-rewrite/geometry-rewrite/paper-trading-rewrite/provider-changes. The LIVE path (Yahoo live provider + completed-candle boundary + paper-trading operations) is untouched. No provider changes; Yahoo request-window logic untouched. Existing pipeline regression unchanged (signals=4, trades=3).

## SESSION KEEP-AWAKE LIFECYCLE FIX (Windows scheduler hardening) — 3rd iteration
- Problem (live helper wrongly classified stale; replaced every few cycles): THREE root causes found across iterations. (1) Batch checked `tasklist /FI "PID eq X" | find "python"` — ambiguous under the venv redirector parent/child duality and PID-reuse-unsafe. (2) `OpenProcess` alone returns a valid handle for a terminated process while any handle exists — must use `GetExitCodeProcess` != STILL_ACTIVE. (3) FINAL AND DECISIVE: the manager spawned the helper via the venv shim (`%PYTHON%` / `sys.executable`); the venv redirector PARENT governs the real child's lifetime, so when the shim exits shortly after each cycle the helper dies silently and the marker goes stale. The identity check itself was never wrong.
- Fix (automation utilities only; NO trading/decision/risk/paper-trading logic touched): `scripts/windows/keep_awake_manager.py` — resolves the helper interpreter via `sys.base_executable` (`resolve_helper_python`; explicit override still honored), so the helper is ONE native process with no shim parent. STRICT identity: `describe_marker(pid_file) -> (pid, token, status, reason)`; a token-carrying marker is NEVER downgraded to PID-only liveness (PID reuse must never alias) and unverifiable/unmatched tokens = stale. DETACHED start (no console; best-effort `CREATE_BREAKAWAY_FROM_JOB`), bounded PID confirmation, named session mutex singleton, stale-classification logs include pid + token + OpenProcess/GetProcessTimes/token-compare/mutex-held detail. `OpenProcess`+`GetExitCodeProcess` for liveness (POSIX zombies = dead). `run_paper_trading_cycle.bat` calls the manager. `keep_awake.py` writes real-interpreter `os.getpid()` + start token; `--mutex-name` guard exits BEFORE acquiring power request if mutex held; `ES_CONTINUOUS | ES_SYSTEM_REQUIRED` acquire / `ES_CONTINUOUS` release contract unchanged; `ES_DISPLAY_REQUIRED` never used; owner-only marker cleanup; non-Windows no-op; manager always exit 0.
- Tests: `tests/test_keep_awake.py` 64 tests (real processes, no mocks except ONE narrowly-scoped monkeypatch simulating an unreadable token — required because POSIX cannot fabricate Windows permission failures deterministically; each marked). Coverage: time/marker parsing, liveness incl. GetExitCode semantics + zombie, STRICT token identity (match/mismatch/unverifiable), describe statuses + reasons, `resolve_helper_python` (explicit honored / base interpreter default / never the venv shim), start/reuse/replace lifecycle, stale-log diagnostics content, confirm timeout, session end, cleanup, owner-only removal, Windows graceful no-op, structural guarantees, CLI exit-0. Full suite: 3393 passed (3329 + 64 new).
- Manual Windows verification: the helper is now a SINGLE native `C:\Python3xx\python.exe` process (no venv parent/child pair). `Get-CimInstance Win32_Process | Where-Object { $_.CommandLine -match 'keep_awake\.py' }` returns exactly ONE line while a helper lives; the marker's PID names that process; the second marker line is its GetProcessTimes start token.


## PRODUCT PHASE 6F — LIVE PAPER VALIDATION (added)
This phase is the LIVE PAPER VALIDATION layer on top of the completed Phase 6A-6E historical-research pipeline and the Product Phase 1-5 live/paper-trading architecture. It answers: "When the system sees a current market state, what did historical research say about similar setups, and how does that historical expectation compare with the actual subsequent paper-trading outcome?". It is VALIDATION / OBSERVATION ONLY: no new trading strategy, no decision-engine change, no prediction/ML/LLM, no broker/execution, no BUY/SELL recommendation, no second scheduler, no second outcome engine. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-12E / Product-Phase-1-6E public API signature was changed, no existing engine/model was modified (only additive new files + .gitignore + docs + AGENTS.md), and the prior 3641 tests still pass (now 3740 with 99 new in tests/test_live_paper_validation.py; demo scripts/test_phase_6f.py passes 44 checks; fixture path remains fully deterministic).
- Architecture: live/near-live data -> completed-candle boundary -> existing analyze (authoritative decision) -> existing Phase 6E historical evidence context (view.historical_context) -> existing PaperTradingOperations.run_once (track + create + persist) -> LiveValidationObservation per instrument -> LiveValidationStore -> historical-vs-actual comparison report.
- Models (`src/engine/models/live_validation.py`, frozen+slots): enums `LiveValidationStatus` (OBSERVED/PAPER_TRADE_CREATED/PAPER_TRADE_ACTIVE/COMPLETED/NO_OPPORTUNITY/REJECTED/NOT_READY/ERROR — non-predictive; a rejected/no-opportunity observation is still valid) + `LiveValidationCycleStatus` (READY/NO_DATA/STALE/ERROR/NOT_READY, distinct from the operations OperationalStatus). `LiveValidationObservation` (validation_id "lval-"+sha256[:16] of the SETUP identity only, so outcome-advancing updates keep the same id; instrument/timeframes/evaluation_timestamp/provider/provider_status/freshness/decision/actionability/direction/geometry_available/historical_context_status/historical_evidence_strength/historical_sample_size/historical win_rate+avg_r+profit_factor/research_ids provenance/paper_trade_id reference/outcome_status/outcome_timestamp/realized_r Decimal/validation_status/recorded_at/updated_at/revision/warnings/limitations; invariants: trade-referencing statuses must carry paper_trade_id, non-referencing must not; realized_r/outcome_timestamp only on COMPLETED). `LiveValidationCycleResult` (cycle_id "lvcycle-"+sha256[:16], operations_cycle_id reference, counts, errors, warnings).
- Serialization (`src/engine/intelligence/live_validation_serialization.py`, LIVE_VALIDATION_SCHEMA_VERSION=1): established __enum__+__enum_class__/__dataclass__/__datetime__/__decimal__/__tuple__ tags; lossless round trip; schema checked BEFORE reconstruction; malformed/future-schema rejected. intelligence/__init__.py stays empty.
- Persistence (`src/dashboard/live_validation_store.py`): `LiveValidationStore(directory=None)` — one JSON document per observation (`<id>.validation`; the non-.json suffix prevents polluting the paper-trade/experiment/research store listings in a shared directory), atomic same-dir temp + os.replace, safe-id regex, corruption + filename-id integrity checks, append/history journal (`<id>.validation.history.jsonl`, one line per revision), idempotent by deterministic id. `default_live_validation_directory()` = ./data/live_validation (git-ignored). The existing PaperTradeStore schema is NOT modified — observations REFERENCE paper-trade ids.
- Engine (`src/dashboard/live_validation.py`): `LiveValidationConfig` (MINIMAL: account_capital/risk_percent/setup_timeframe/context_timeframe/label/metadata) + `LivePaperValidation(service, config=None, operations=None, validation_store=None).run_once(instruments=None, reference_now=None, started_at=None)`. Steps: (1) run the EXISTING operations cycle (eligibility gate unchanged); (2) per instrument (sorted, failure-isolated) build the observation from the existing analysis view + attached Phase 6E context + the operations per-instrument paper-trade linkage (created > duplicate > closed > updated); (3) persist idempotently (unchanged -> no rewrite; advanced -> revision+1 + history); (4) refresh previously-recorded OPEN observations whose referenced trade advanced (COMPLETED with actual outcome when terminal). Status mapping: trade lifecycle takes precedence over decision-based states (so an earlier setup's resolving trade is tracked); REJECTED decision -> REJECTED; NO_OPPORTUNITY/no decision -> NO_OPPORTUNITY; analysed without trade -> OBSERVED; not analysed / exception -> ERROR.
- Reporting (`src/engine/reporting/live_validation.py`, full-path import, reporting/__init__ NOT extended): `LiveValidationFormatter(precision=2, width=72).format_cycle(result)` (historical-vs-live comparison table: instrument/eval time/decision/actionability/evidence status/strength/sample/statistics/paper trade created?/outcome/realized R/validation status) + `.format_observation(obs)` (three strictly separated sections: CURRENT STATE / HISTORICAL EVIDENCE / PAPER-TRADE OUTCOME). Returns str; no predictive/BUY-SELL language; fixed disclaimer "Historical evidence is observational research context. It does not predict future returns, guarantee profitability, or override the authoritative decision engine."
- CLI (`scripts/run_live_paper_validation.py`): THIN operator CLI over run_once (the single explicit entry point the EXISTING scheduler/keep-awake launcher can invoke — no second scheduler; existing 09:15-16:00 session behavior, keep-awake, completed-candle handling, Yahoo provider and paper-trading-only limitation preserved). Defaults DASHBOARD_PROVIDER=yahoo without overwriting; default instruments ("NIFTY",)+COMBINED_UNIVERSE (the existing configured universe, never hard-coded); --research-dir/--validation-dir overridable; exit 0/1/2 convention; PAPER-TRADING-ONLY banner top+bottom.
- No-look-ahead (regression-tested): run_once has no future/future_candles/lookahead parameter; OutcomeEvaluator.evaluate + HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate patched to raise — validation still works; fixed-T observation unaffected by appended future candles; outcome_timestamp strictly after evaluation_timestamp.
- Multi-instrument: instruments=None -> service.available_instruments() (existing configurable universe); sorted, shuffle-invariant, per-instrument failure isolation.
- `tests/test_live_paper_validation.py`: 99 tests across areas A-J + reporting + CLI (observation model; point-in-time; historical evidence AVAILABLE/NO_MATCH/RESEARCH_UNAVAILABLE/UNAVAILABLE; decision preservation [REJECTED+STRONG stays REJECTED, no fabricated geometry, plan reused verbatim]; paper trading [creation through existing operations, duplicates, lifecycle reused]; outcomes [active stays active, completed records outcome, BOTH_TOUCHED realized_r None]; failure isolation [instrument/provider/lookup/persistence]; persistence [atomic/reload/corruption/idempotency/provenance/schema/no-.json-pollution]; multi-instrument [existing universe, no hard-coded five]; regression [pipeline baseline 4/3, operations outcome identical to direct run]; CLI [args/env/exit codes/end-to-end fixture cycle/deterministic cycle id]).
- `scripts/test_phase_6f.py`: demo — 44 PASS checks covering the 12 required demonstrations (current observation; evidence attached; decision preserved; creation; tracking; completed outcome; historical-vs-actual comparison; NO_OPPORTUNITY; failure isolation; no-look-ahead; persistence/reload; deterministic id) + pipeline baseline 4/3. Fully offline. Exits 0.
- `docs/phase_6f_live_paper_validation`: architecture/lifecycle/data-flow/comparison/point-in-time/persistence/failure-handling/scheduler-integration/paper-trading-only/interpretation-limitations.
Roadmap: Product Phase 6F — LIVE PAPER VALIDATION — COMPLETE. This was the FINAL planned product phase.
Architectural invariants preserved: NO new orchestration package (validation is orchestration + persistence + reporting over existing components); NO new intelligence/trading logic (every decision/geometry/plan/evidence/lifecycle value read from reused components); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged in direction; intelligence/__init__.py remains intentionally empty; reporting/__init__.py NOT extended. No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files + .gitignore + docs + AGENTS.md). The existing decision is AUTHORITATIVE (never upgraded/downgraded/renamed to BUY/SELL); historical evidence is descriptive context only. Existing TradeCandidate geometry + Product Phase 4 trade plan reused VERBATIM; Target 2 remains unsupported. No real orders. Existing pipeline regression unchanged (signals=4, trades=3); all prior demos unchanged. No Sprint 11A-12E / Product-Phase-1-6E regression.

## PHASE 6B YAHOO HISTORICAL SYMBOL-RESOLUTION FIX (bug fix)
- Bug: `YahooHistoricalDataProvider` (engine/data/historical_provider.py) defaulted to an EMPTY symbol map, so canonical names (NIFTY/RELIANCE/...) were sent to Yahoo verbatim and returned zero candles.
- Fix (provider layer only): `_default_yahoo_symbol_map()` derives the default map from the canonical universe (`engine.config.universe` BENCHMARK_INDEX + COMBINED_UNIVERSE) — NIFTY -> ^NSEI, every constituent -> `<NSE>.NS`; unknown instruments pass through verbatim; explicit `symbol_map` constructor arg still overrides. No second universe; provider contract unchanged; no trading/decision/geometry/paper-trading logic touched.
- Tests: 8 deterministic symbol-resolution tests appended to tests/test_historical_data_foundation.py (required mapping, full-universe coverage, passthrough, override, determinism, fetch sends mapped symbol, RELIANCE 1D returns candles via injected fake backend). Full suite: 3748 passed; Phase 6A/6B demo 15/15 PASS.

## PHASE 6B YAHOO HISTORICAL TIMEZONE-NORMALIZATION FIX (bug fix)
- Bug: yfinance returns timezone-NAIVE timestamps for 1D data; all candles were rejected by the existing NAIVE_TIMESTAMP validation (0 accepted/stored).
- Fix (provider layer only): `_yahoo_timestamp_to_utc()` in engine/data/historical_provider.py normalizes Yahoo timestamps to timezone-aware UTC BEFORE canonical OHLCVCandle validation — naive values interpreted as UTC (daily bars are exchange-day labels), aware values converted via astimezone(UTC). Validation NOT weakened; canonical OHLCV contract preserved.
- Tests: 10 deterministic tests in tests/test_historical_data_foundation.py (naive->UTC, aware->UTC, chronological order, mixed naive/aware, manually-created naive candle still rejected, RELIANCE+NIFTY fake 1D fetch -> 23/23 accepted). Full suite: 3758 passed. Real verification: NIFTY+RELIANCE 1D both 23 received/23 accepted/0 rejected/Validation PASS/23 stored.

## PHASE 6B HISTORICAL PERSISTENCE CONSISTENCY FIX (bug fix + hardening)
- Reported: ingestion said 23 stored but candles.json empty. Diagnosis: the empty files were STALE artifacts of the earlier buggy runs (pre-symbol-fix Yahoo returned an all-NaN frame -> provider EMPTY -> ingest() persists an EMPTY dataset for auditability; the INVALID all-rejected run is never persisted). The store merge logic itself was correct (re-ingesting over a stale empty file merges; proven by test).
- Hardening (store/service layer only): HistoricalDataStore.store() now RELOADS the persisted file after the atomic write and raises HistoricalDataIntegrityError on count mismatch; total_candles is the RELOADED count (never the in-memory count) so reported counts always reflect what is actually persisted+reloadable. HistoricalStoreResult.reload_verified (additive defaulted field) set True by store_historical. CLI report gains "Reload check : PASS (N candles reloaded)". Atomic write + integrity guarantees preserved; validator untouched.
- Tests: 9 new in tests/test_historical_data_foundation.py (NIFTY+RELIANCE fake-Yahoo ingest->persist->reload round trip; persisted file actually contains candles; stale-empty-file merge [the exact reported state]; idempotent re-ingestion counts; total read back from disk; post-write verification raises on mismatch [narrowly-scoped monkeypatch simulating a platform write revert]; load_historical after ingest; CLI reload-check line). File: 125 passed. Full suite: 3767 passed. Demo: 15/15.
- ENV NOTE: demo check 10 (dashboard empty state) expects a CLEAN ./data/historical store; run the demo BEFORE real ingestion commands (or after clearing data/historical).

## PHASE 6B YAHOO HISTORICAL INTRADAY RETENTION / WINDOWED FETCH FIX (bug fix)
- Bug: YahooHistoricalDataProvider sent the WHOLE requested range in ONE get_history call; Yahoo rejects intraday requests whose START is older than its retention window (~7d 1m, ~60d <=30m/90m, ~730d 1h) -> "15m data not available... must be within the last 60 days" -> 0 records for any long intraday range.
- Fix (provider layer only; mirrors the established live-provider Yahoo range fix): `_INTERVAL_LIMIT_DAYS` (1m=6, 2m/5m/15m/30m/90m=58, 1h=725, 1D=1825 safety-margined) + `_INTRADAY_LIMITED`; fetch() now (1) clips the requested start to the retention floor (anchor - max_days; intraday only — 1D never clipped), honestly reporting the clip in the response reason (data before the floor genuinely does not exist on Yahoo; NEVER fabricated), (2) splits the effective range into bounded windows, tolerating per-window empty/error (all-windows-error -> ERROR as before), (3) merges/dedupes/sorts, normalizing timestamps to aware UTC per candle (same timezone fix as before). fetch gains additive kwarg reference_now (default now(UTC)); the service threads reference_now to providers that accept it (inspect.signature fallback for legacy providers without the kwarg). Provider contract preserved.
- Tests: 12 deterministic tests (window bounds, floor clipping, fully-expired range honest EMPTY with NO hopeless requests, merge/dedupe/chronology, daily single-window unchanged + never clipped, all-windows-error ERROR, partial-window failure tolerance, naive anchor, service threading, legacy provider without kwarg, 15m fake ingest persist+reload). File: 137 passed. Full suite: 3779 passed. Demo: 15/15.
- Real verification: NIFTY 15m 2024-08-01..2026-08-01 -> 609 received/accepted/stored/reloaded (the retrievable ~last-58-day portion); RELIANCE 15m -> 610; NIFTY 1D unchanged (23).

## UPSTOX HISTORICAL PROVIDER (additive provider integration)
- Added `UpstoxHistoricalDataProvider` (provider name `"upstox-historical"`), an OPTIONAL real-HTTP historical provider in `src/engine/data/historical_provider.py` implementing the SAME contract as `YahooHistoricalDataProvider` (`provider_name`/`is_available`/`supports`/`fetch` -> `HistoricalProviderResponse`). It is a PROVIDER-INTEGRATION add-on to the existing Phase 6B historical-data layer ONLY: no trading intelligence, decision, setup-detection, geometry, trade-plan, paper-trading, live-trading, Phase 6C/6D/6E/6F behavior is modified. Storage goes through the EXISTING `HistoricalMarketDataService` + `HistoricalDataStore` (canonical `data/historical/` layout; no parallel Upstox storage).
- ENV: `UPSTOX_ANALYTICS_TOKEN` (read lazily; `is_available()` False and `fetch()` -> honest ERROR when absent). Token sent ONLY in `Authorization: Bearer <token>`; NEVER hard-coded/logged/printed/committed.
- Instrument-key resolution is ISOLATED to the provider (`_default_upstox_instrument_key_map`): only verified keys are mapped — currently `RELIANCE -> NSE_EQ|INE002A01018`. Unknown instruments raise `KeyError` and `fetch` reports `UNSUPPORTED` (never silently requests an invalid key; `RELIANCE.NS` is NOT a valid Upstox key). No second universe (derived from `engine.config.universe`).
- MONTHLY CHUNKING (critical): `_upstox_monthly_chunks` splits a requested intraday window into bounded calendar-month chunks (`2022-01-01 -> 2023-01-01` -> 12 requests); each chunk's `to_date` capped at `request.end`; returned candles outside `[request.start, request.end]` are dropped (range semantics preserved). Multi-chunk responses merged (dedupe by timestamp) and normalized to chronological order BEFORE the canonical validation layer. A failed chunk never silently becomes success: all-chunks-fail -> ERROR, partial-chunk success -> OK with the `reason` reporting the chunk error count.
- Timeframe support (FIRST SCOPE): `15m` only (`unit=minutes`, `interval=15`); `supports` returns False / `fetch` returns `UNSUPPORTED` for everything else (no resampling/fabrication).
- Timestamps: Upstox returns timezone-AWARE `+05:30` ISO strings; `_upstox_timestamp_to_utc` parses ISO strings (e.g. `2022-01-03T09:15:00+05:30`) or accepts aware datetimes and converts to UTC. Naive values return None -> the existing NAIVE_TIMESTAMP validation is never weakened.
- Request: `GET https://api.upstox.com/v3/historical-candle/{instrument_key}/minutes/15/{to_date}/{from_date}` with `Accept: application/json`, `Accept-Encoding: gzip, deflate` (urllib's default `identity` is rejected by the Upstox gateway), `Authorization: Bearer <token>`, finite timeout (default 30s). Response validated BEFORE constructing OHLCVCandle objects: `status == "success"`, `candles` list, row shape `[timestamp, open, high, low, close, volume, open_interest]` (open_interest ignored), finite numeric OHLCV, parseable timestamps.
- USER-AGENT FIX (live verification, 2026-08): the Cloudflare edge in front of `api.upstox.com` rejects urllib's DEFAULT `Python-urllib/x.y` User-Agent with HTTP 403 / Error 1010 (`browser_signature_banned`), which made the live provider return EMPTY/ERROR even though the endpoint returns HTTP 200 with real candles when ANY non-default User-Agent (curl/requests/Mozilla) is used. `_fetch_one` now ALWAYS sends `User-Agent: python-urllib/upstox-historical-provider` (`UPSTOX_USER_AGENT` constant, exported in `__all__`). Verified LIVE from the sandbox: `provider.fetch()` returns 550 RELIANCE 15m Dec-2022 candles (status OK, tz-aware UTC, first `2022-12-01T03:45Z`, last `2022-12-30T09:45Z`) even with a dummy/no token (the 15m historical endpoint does not require auth). Regression tests: `test_user_agent_header_sent` + `test_default_user_agent_constant_is_explicit` (urllib canonicalizes header names to title-case on the wire, e.g. `User-agent`). ALSO FIXED: `scripts/verify_upstox_live.py` called the non-existent `store.load_historical()` (crashed after ingestion); now uses `store.load_candles()`. Full suite: 3849 passed (was 3847).
- CLI: `python scripts/ingest_historical_data.py --instrument RELIANCE --timeframe 15m --start 2022-12-01 --end 2023-01-01 --provider upstox-historical` works; provider registered in `_PROVIDERS`; reports provider/range/received/accepted/rejected/validation/storage/reload via the existing structure.
- Response decoding: urllib does NOT auto-decompress `Content-Encoding: gzip`; `_decode_body` handles gzip/deflate/identity (both the fake and production paths go through it). Tests: `tests/test_upstox_historical.py` (60 deterministic, network-free tests using an injected fake `urlopen` that receives the actual `urllib.request.Request` + token; no real Upstox token required). Demo: `scripts/test_upstox_historical.py` (17 PASS checks, fake backend, no network). Live operator check: `scripts/verify_upstox_live.py` (RELIANCE 15m one monthly period; exits 1 when `UPSTOX_ANALYTICS_TOKEN` unset; token never printed). Docs: `docs/upstox_historical_provider`.
- FIRST-SCOPE LIMITATION: no full-universe downloader, no auto-download on startup, no historical ingestion scheduler, not connected to Phase 6F. Upstox does NOT automatically download data merely because the provider exists — ingestion remains an explicit operator action through `ingest_historical_data.py`. No regression: Yahoo/deterministic providers unchanged; existing pipeline baseline unchanged (signals=4, trades=3).

## CHECKPOINT 3A — HISTORICAL CORPUS PROVIDER EXTENSION (research-universe keys + 1D)
- Scope: PROVIDER EXTENSION ONLY. No bulk historical ingestion started, no 275-invocation matrix, no trading-intelligence / decision / geometry / trade-plan / paper-trade / live-provider / completed-candle / Phase 6C-6F / prediction / ML / evidence changes.
- Instrument-key mapping (`_default_upstox_instrument_key_map`) now covers the CONFIRMED research universe: `RELIANCE -> NSE_EQ|INE002A01018` (unchanged), `TCS -> NSE_EQ|INE467B01029`, `HDFCBANK -> NSE_EQ|INE040A01034`, `ICICIBANK -> NSE_EQ|INE090A01021`, `NIFTY -> NSE_INDEX|Nifty 50`. Equities use NSE_EQ/ISIN; the index key contains a pipe + space. No second universe (still derived from `engine.config.universe`). No guessed keys — all use the established controlled Upstox verification/ISINs.
- NIFTY URL encoding: `urllib.parse.quote(key, safe="|")` keeps the pipe literal and percent-encodes the space -> `NSE_INDEX|Nifty%2050`; round-trips via `unquote`; tests assert no raw space in the URL.
- 1D support: `_INTERVALS` adds `"1D": (UPSTOX_UNIT_DAYS, "1")` with `UPSTOX_UNIT_DAYS = "days"` (the verified V3 spelling `days/1`, NOT `day/1`). URL: `/historical-candle/{key}/days/1/{to_date}/{from_date}`. 15m behavior unchanged; monthly chunking kept for 1D.
- DAILY RESPONSE NORMALIZATION RULE (deterministic + provider-specific + documented + tested + non-fabricating): a valid Upstox daily candle is timestamped `00:00:00+05:30` (IST exchange-day label, per official V3 daily examples). In `_parse_candles_filtered(payload, unit=UPSTOX_UNIT_DAYS)`, rows whose IST time-of-day is non-midnight are embedded intraday rows and are FILTERED before canonical OHLCVCandle construction (counted + reported in the fetch reason; an all-intraday daily response -> honest empty chunk). Malformed/non-finite/naive/unparseable rows still raise -> honest chunk ERROR. 15m responses are never filtered. Backward-compatible `_parse_candles(payload)` kept (no filter); `_fetch_one` gains `unit=` kwarg.
- RANGE SEMANTICS: 15m acceptance stays strict UTC `[start, end]`; 1D acceptance compares the candle's IST trading-day label to `[request.start.date(), request.end.date()]` (matches the inclusive day labels the API was asked for; otherwise the genuine first requested day — UTC 18:30 prior day — would be dropped). `/to_date/from_date/` ordering preserved; no reversed dates; all timestamps aware UTC.
- Tests: `tests/test_upstox_historical.py` 103 passed (70 prior + 33 new covering A-T: TCS/HDFCBANK/ICICIBANK/NIFTY mappings, NIFTY URL encoding, RELIANCE regression, 1D support, 15m support, /days/1 usage, 1D date ordering + range clipping + timezone normalization, real V3 nested `data.candles` parsing, daily filtering, malformed/non-numeric/naive daily rows rejected, duplicate daily timestamps, provider ordering invariance, no future daily candles, idempotent 1D persistence). Existing 15m tests updated ONLY where 1D/NIFTY became supported (unsupported-timeframe tests now use 30m/1h; unknown-instrument tests use `NOTANINSTRUMENT`). No test weakened: fake HTTP only, no internet.
- Demo: `scripts/test_upstox_historical.py` 26 PASS (RELIANCE 15m, TCS/NIFTY/HDFCBANK/ICICIBANK mappings, NIFTY URL encoding, 1D `/days/1` fetch + daily filtering + UTC normalization + idempotent 1D store persistence, pipeline baseline 4/3). Docs: `docs/upstox_historical_provider` updated (research universe, 1D/days-1, key map, daily normalization rule, date semantics, limitations). No historical research performance documented; Phase 6B NOT started. Regression: `tests/test_historical_data_foundation.py` 137 passed; pipeline baseline unchanged (signals=4, trades=3).

## CHECKPOINT 3B — HISTORICAL CORPUS PREPARATION (ingestion/coverage planning layer, added)
- Scope: PLANNING LAYER ONLY, sitting between the Checkpoint 3A provider extension and the Phase 6C research corpus. It computes the deterministic ingestion / coverage WORK MATRIX for a research corpus (research universe × timeframes × monthly window chunks), reports CURRENT stored coverage per dataset from the EXISTING Phase 6B store, and names the exact missing chunk requests an operator still has to fetch. It is a deterministic planner — it NEVER fetches market data, NEVER talks to a provider, NEVER reads candles, and does NOT call the Phase 6C corpus engine or any decision/scoring/geometry/evidence/paper-trading logic. Execution of a planned request goes through the EXISTING `HistoricalMarketDataService` + `HistoricalDataStore` (via `scripts/ingest_historical_data.py`), so a planned chunk is ingested exactly like a manually-specified one. No trading-intelligence / decision / geometry / trade-plan / paper-trade / live-provider / completed-candle / Phase 6C-6F / prediction / ML / evidence changes. No new external dependencies (stdlib only: dataclasses, datetime, enum, hashlib, typing, itertools).
- Models `src/engine/models/corpus_plan.py` (frozen+slots): enum `DatasetCoverageStatus` (MISSING = no stored dataset/no stored candles; EMPTY = persisted dataset exists but holds zero candles; PARTIAL = stored candles cover a subset of the planned monthly chunks; COMPLETE = every planned monthly chunk contains at least one stored candle; UNAVAILABLE = no store configured so coverage cannot be measured; `is_complete`/`is_usable` properties). Models `DatasetCoverage` (per-dataset chunk coverage: covered/missing chunk keys, `chunks_required`/`chunks_covered` counts, status; `__post_init__` keeps the status-vs-count invariants consistent), `CorpusPreparationRow` (one (instrument, timeframe) row: dataset, timeframe, provider_supported, status, counts, missing_chunk_keys), `CorpusPreparationPlan` (`plan_id = "prep-"+sha256[:16]` of canonical identity [instruments sorted + timeframes + plan window + provider + label + metadata]; counts/properties `dataset_count`/`supported_row_count`/`complete_count`/`partial_count`/`missing_count`/`empty_count`/`unavailable_count`/`unsupported_count`/`required_request_count`/`covered_request_count`/`missing_request_count`/`is_empty`/`has_missing`/`is_fully_covered`). No business logic in models.
- Config `src/engine/config/corpus_plan_config.py` (frozen+slots, validated): `CorpusPlanConfig` — `timeframes` (default `("15m", "1D")` mirroring the Phase 6C setup/context pair; canonicalized via the reused `canonical_timeframe`, unknown labels rejected), `start`/`end` (aware, `start < end`, REQUIRED — no unbounded planning mode), `provider` (default `"upstox-historical"` — the capability gate), `label`/`metadata`. `validate_plan_window()` helper. NO strategy/optimization/scoring parameters; universe is NOT configured here (the planner consumes the EXISTING configurable `ResearchUniverse` / explicit instrument sequence, never hard-codes it).
- Planner `src/engine/data/corpus_plan.py` (data/__init__.py stays intentionally empty — import via `from engine.data.corpus_plan import CorpusPreparationPlanner`): `CorpusPreparationPlanner(config, store=None, provider=None)` + module helper `monthly_chunks_for_window(start, end)` (delegates to the Phase 6B `_upstox_monthly_chunks` — chunking logic reused, not duplicated). `plan(instruments=None, label="", metadata=None) -> CorpusPreparationPlan`: resolves the universe (defaults to `ResearchUniverse`), reads stored overview via the reused `HistoricalDataStore.list_datasets()`/`StoredDatasetInfo`, computes per-row coverage from the existing store, gates capability via `provider.supports`/`provider_name` (non-Upstox providers or a provider whose name is not the configured gate -> row status not computed; no fabrication), merges chunk coverage for CHUNK-HONEST accounting (a row is COMPLETE only when every planned monthly chunk contains at least one stored candle; a stored candle outside any planned chunk never counts), and produces a deterministic plan. FIXED invariant: a `UNAVAILABLE` row (no store configured) still lists its required chunk keys in `missing_chunk_keys` (never silently empty). `coverage_summary()` and `plan_to_jsonable()` helpers. `__all__ = ["CorpusPreparationPlanner", "monthly_chunks_for_window"]`.
- Reporting `src/engine/reporting/corpus_plan.py` (full-path import; reporting/__init__.py NOT extended, matching 11O-12E convention): `CorpusPreparationFormatter(width=72).format(plan) -> str` — required sections: Corpus Preparation Plan header (plan id, window, instruments, timeframes, provider), Coverage Summary (per-status counts + request accounting), Per-Dataset Coverage table, Missing Chunks (exact chunk request keys an operator must ingest), Warnings, Rationale + the fixed disclaimer that this is an ingestion PLANNING artifact (it does not fetch data, does not predict anything, does not create trades/positions, and does not modify the authoritative decision/scoring logic). `format_missing_request_keys(...) -> str`. Returns str; unavailable shown as "unavailable"; no predictive/BUY-SELL language.
- Operator CLI `scripts/prepare_corpus_data.py`: THIN planning-only CLI (execution remains `scripts/ingest_historical_data.py`). Args: `--provider` (default `"upstox-historical"`), `--timeframes` (default `15m,1D`), `--start`/`--end`, `--instruments` (comma list; default = research universe), `--label`, `--metadata`, `--json` (sorted-key JSON of `plan_to_jsonable`). Text mode prints the full `CorpusPreparationFormatter` report; JSON mode prints a single machine-readable object. Exit codes: 0 for a successfully computed plan, 2 for bad args (unparseable window/instruments/timeframes), nonzero only on runtime failure. Full report printed to stdout; no print() inside engine/formatter modules (the CLI may print). No `UPSTOX_ANALYTICS_TOKEN` required — planning is provider-capability-gated and FAKE/network-free.
- Demo `scripts/test_corpus_preparation.py`: deterministic, 19 PASS checks (monthly chunking; MISSING/EMPTY/PARTIAL/COMPLETE classification incl. fully-past window; UNAVAILABLE-no-store with missing chunk keys preserved; request accounting; capability gating row order [timeframe-outer → instrument-inner]; corrupt candles.json honestly EMPTY not MISSING; plan_id determinism + prefix; no-look-ahead none (plan reads store metadata only); existing-path regression `engine.pipeline.HistoricalEvaluationPipeline` + `PipelineConfig` import untouched; pipeline baseline signals=4/trades=3). Exits 0.
- Tests `tests/test_corpus_preparation.py`: 63 tests (models/status vocabulary/invariants/properties; config defaults + validation incl. unknown timeframe/naive window/bad range; monthly chunking edge cases incl. year boundary + single month; coverage classification missing/empty/partial/complete/fully-past-window/unavailable; store isolation [planner reads only `list_datasets`, never mutates]; provider capability gating incl. non-upstox + provider-name gate; chunk-honest accounting [stored candle outside planned window never counts]; plan identity determinism + different-window/label/instrument -> different id; formatter sections incl. missing-chunk keys + disclaimer + no-predictive-language; CLI text+JSON modes + exit codes + bad-args; immutability frozen+slots; no-shared-state). No internet; uses injected fake store/provider.
- Full suite post-3B: 3945 passed (3882 + 63 new), 1 pre-existing StarletteDeprecationWarning. Pipeline baseline unchanged (signals=4, trades=3); Checkpoint 3A `tests/test_upstox_historical.py` 103 + `tests/test_historical_data_foundation.py` unchanged; demos unchanged.
- LOCAL/Windows-only items (deferred by design; OpenHands Cloud has no `UPSTOX_ANALYTICS_TOKEN`): real Upstox ingestion (~275-invocation matrix) via `python scripts/prepare_corpus_data.py --provider upstox-historical --start ... --end ...` to print the missing-chunk matrix, then `scripts/ingest_historical_data.py --provider upstox-historical --instrument ... --timeframe ... --start ... --end ...` per chunk; re-run the planner after each ingestion to see coverage progress. No trading/decision/paper-trading effect.

## CHECKPOINT 3B (step 2) — SAFE, RESUMABLE HISTORICAL CORPUS INGESTION RUNNER (added)
This is the execution half of Checkpoint 3B (the Checkpoint 3B PLANNER layer above provides the work matrix; this runner EXECUTES the missing chunks). It is an ORCHESTRATION LAYER ONLY sitting on the completed Phase 6A-6D historical-data architecture; it implements NO trading/scoring/prediction/decision/evidence logic, NO provider HTTP client, and NO second persistence database. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-12E / Product-Phase-1-6F / Checkpoint-3A / Checkpoint-3B-planner public API signature was changed, no existing engine/model was modified (only new additive files), and the prior 3945 tests still pass (now 3980 with 35 new in tests/test_corpus_ingestion.py; demo scripts/test_corpus_ingestion.py passes 8/8 checks; fixture path remains fully deterministic and OFFLINE).
- Purpose: `CorpusPreparationPlanner -> current missing chunks -> HistoricalDataRequest -> existing HistoricalMarketDataService -> existing HistoricalDataStore -> successful chunk persisted -> next missing chunk`. Sequential execution only (no concurrency; avoids Upstox API pressure, deterministic failures, simple recovery, understandable logs). The runner derives its work from the planner/store state; it never needs a manual list of completed chunks.
- New engine `src/engine/data/corpus_ingestion.py` (data/__init__.py stays intentionally empty — import via `from engine.data.corpus_ingestion import CorpusIngestionEngine`): `CorpusIngestionEngine(planner, service, config, reporter)`. Models: `CorpusIngestionConfig` (provider, timeframes=None (from planner when None), instruments=None (universe default), require_upstox_token flag, reference_now, label, metadata), `CorpusJob` (index, instrument, timeframe, start, end, key), `CorpusBacklog` (from planner rows: jobs ordered timeframe-outer -> instrument-inner, missing/skip/load counts), `CorpusChunkOutcome` (+ status constants COMPLETED/FAILED/SKIPPED), `CorpusIngestionSession`/`CorpusIngestionSummary` (planned_missing/completed/skipped/failed/candles_added/remaining/failed_chunks/skip_reasons). Public API: `build_backlog(start, end, instruments=None)`, `run(backlog=None, *, start=None, end=None, instruments=None)`; `run()` performs the credential precheck BEFORE any ingestion step. Chunk keys (planner `missing_chunk_keys`) are RECONSTRUCTED into (start, end) datetimes from the `monthly_chunks_for_window(start, end)` grid — `key.split(":")` is NEVER used because ISO timestamps contain colons.
- RESUMABILITY IS DERIVED FROM THE CORPUS, NOT A PERMANENT COMPLETION DB: each chunk goes through the EXISTING `HistoricalMarketDataService.ingest()` (fetch -> validate -> gap-detect -> atomic store merge by candle timestamp; idempotent). On a rerun `CorpusPreparationPlanner.plan()` reports the covered chunks (store-derived) and they simply do NOT appear in the backlog (handled by the planner, whole-chunk skip). A defensive per-chunk `_chunk_covered` precheck ALSO skips mid-run any chunk the store already covers (belt-and-braces safety net). A failed chunk is never marked covered: EMPTY/ERROR/validation-failure/unexpected-exception FAILS only that chunk (a typed per-chunk boundary catches exceptions, records a safe reason, continues); the failed chunk remains in the store as a zero-usable-candles dataset so the next run re-attempts it.
- CREDENTIAL PRECHECK (REQUIRED, enforced in the engine AND the CLI): BEFORE any API/ingestion request the runner verifies `UPSTOX_ANALYTICS_TOKEN` via `check_upstox_analytics_token()` using the SAME mechanism as the existing `UpstoxHistoricalDataProvider` (`engine.data.historical_provider.UPSTOX_TOKEN_ENV`); unset/empty -> `CorpusIngestionError` ("UPSTOX_ANALYTICS_TOKEN is not set..."), clean exit, ZERO API requests, ZERO store mutations. `UPSTOX_ACCESS_TOKEN` is NEVER used as a fallback and NEVER copied into `UPSTOX_ANALYTICS_TOKEN`. The engine defensively redacts `Bearer <token>` patterns and the three token env-var names from every progress/failure line; the CLI/engine never print credentials or Authorization headers.
- Operator CLI `scripts/ingest_corpus_data.py`: thin CLI over the engine. Args: `--start`/`--end` (required, aware), `--provider` (default `upstox-historical`), `--timeframes` (nargs `+`, default `15m 1D`), `--instruments` (nargs `+`, default research universe), `--data-dir`, `--label`. Progress `[i/N] INSTR TF START -> END ... PASS (N candles)` / `... FAIL: <safe reason>` / `... SKIPPED (already covered)`; final summary block (Planned missing/Completed/Skipped/Failed/Candles added/Remaining) + skipped reasons + failed chunk list. Exit codes: 0 the cycle executed (per-chunk failures reported, never a CLI failure), 1 credential/runtime failure before the loop (no API work), 2 bad args. Report banners: `HISTORICAL DATA ONLY — no prediction, no evidence computed`.
- POINT-IN-TIME / NO-LOOK-AHEAD (regression-tested): each chunk request is bounded to the planner's monthly chunk window; the deterministic reference time (`CorpusIngestionConfig.reference_now`) defaults to the plan window END (or the latest stored candle timestamp for the backlog datasets when newer) so re-ingesting a chunk whose data is already partially stored is never rejected as future-dated. No future information is introduced; `OutcomeEvaluator.evaluate` / `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate` are NEVER invoked (patched-to-raise tests pass).
- Tests `tests/test_corpus_ingestion.py`: 35 tests across the 11 required areas (work derived from the existing planner; already-covered chunks skipped; missing chunk ingested; successful ingestion persisted; failed chunk does not stop subsequent chunks; failed chunks not falsely covered [EMPTY -> zero usable candles, `covered_now=False`, rerun re-attempts]; rerun does not re-fetch completed chunks [fetch-tracking provider: only the missing chunk is re-fetched]; missing `UPSTOX_ANALYTICS_TOKEN` prevents execution [engine boundary + CLI subprocess]; `UPSTOX_ACCESS_TOKEN` NOT accepted as a fallback [CLI env-removal test]; no credential/token printed [a provider that embeds the token in its reason + os.environ scan]; existing planner behavior unchanged). Fakes/in-memory providers ONLY — the automated suite makes NO real Upstox calls. The test `InMemoryHistoricalProvider` is window-filtered (`RangeFilteredProvider` helper: returns only candles within `[request.start, request.end]`, out-of-window -> honest EMPTY) so per-chunk tests are correct; synthetic daily fixtures are offset to 04:00 UTC so no candle sits on a month-boundary instant (real Upstox candles are IST-labeled and never align with the UTC month tick).
- Demo `scripts/test_corpus_ingestion.py`: 8/8 PASS (work derivation, sequential ingestion + persistence, failure isolation, deterministic progress lines, rerun derives work from store, no re-fetch/re-persist, credential precheck blocks cleanly, offline CLI run + persist). Fully offline.
- ARCHITECTURAL INVARIANTS PRESERVED: NO new orchestration package; NO new intelligence/trading logic (every value read from reused Phase 6A-6D engines; the runner only orchestrates + projects); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged; intelligence/__init__.py stays empty; reporting/__init__.py NOT extended (no new formatter — the CLI prints the report). No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files). No print() in engines (CLI/demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live-trading/paper-trading/decision/evidence changes. The only intended side effect is persistence of historical OHLCV through the existing historical ingestion/store pipeline. Existing pipeline regression unchanged (signals=4, trades=3); Checkpoint 3B planner demo/tests unchanged (19 checks / 63 tests); full suite 3980 passed. Do NOT start the real ~359-chunk ingestion automatically — it is an explicit local operator action (this sandbox has no `UPSTOX_ANALYTICS_TOKEN` by default; the provider/token are configured on the operator's Windows machine).

## CHECKPOINT 3B (step 3) — READ-ONLY HISTORICAL CORPUS INTEGRITY AUDIT (added)
- Scope: AUDIT-ONLY layer over the COMPLETED Phase 6A-6D historical corpus architecture. It inspects the EXISTING persisted multi-instrument / multi-timeframe historical corpus and reports its condition WITHOUT modifying anything: READ -> CHECK -> REPORT. It is NOT a trading-intelligence / decision / evidence / prediction / provider layer and does NOT reopen the completed 11A-12E / Product-Phase-1-6F / Checkpoint-3A / Checkpoint-3B architecture. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-12E / Product-Phase-1-6F / Checkpoint-3A / Checkpoint-3B public API signature was changed, no existing engine/model was modified (only the 5 new additive files below), and the prior 3980 tests still pass (now 4026 with 46 new in tests/test_corpus_audit.py; fixture path remains fully deterministic and OFFLINE).
- New files: `src/engine/models/corpus_audit.py`, `src/engine/data/corpus_audit.py`, `src/engine/reporting/corpus_audit.py`, `scripts/audit_historical_corpus.py`, `tests/test_corpus_audit.py`.
- SAFETY RULES (audit is strictly read-only): NEVER calls the Upstox API; NEVER requires `UPSTOX_ANALYTICS_TOKEN`; NEVER ingests data; NEVER writes / repairs / rewrites candle or store files; NEVER alters the planner, the provider, or prediction/evidence/trading/replay code; NEVER silently discards invalid candles; NEVER normalizes or "fixes" bad OHLC; NEVER prints credentials. The audit instantiates NO provider (the engine builds a `CorpusPreparationPlanner` over the store without a provider object; the audit engine source contains no `UpstoxHistoricalDataProvider` / `urlopen` / token reference).
- Reuse (NOT duplicated domain rules): `HistoricalDataStore` loading / `exists` / `path_for`; the canonical `OHLCVCandle` / `DataValidator` contract; Phase 6A `detect_gaps()` + `GapDetectionConfig` (preserving `POSSIBLE_MARKET_CLOSURE` vs `UNEXPECTED_GAP` terminology — weekends/holidays are NOT reported as corruption); `CorpusPreparationPlanner` coverage semantics + `DatasetCoverageStatus` (MISSING / EMPTY / PARTIAL / COMPLETE / UNAVAILABLE) + `monthly_chunks_for_window`. No parallel definitions.
- Per-dataset checks (10 datasets = 5 instruments x 15m/1D): (1) existence; (2) candle count; (3) first/last timestamp range (canonical aware representation); (4) chronology (monotonic PASS/FAIL); (5) duplicate timestamps (PASS (0 duplicates) or count); (6) timezone awareness (every timestamp aware); (7) OHLC validity (`low <= open <= high`, `low <= close <= high`, finite/numeric values) — invalid count + first few offending timestamps, rows REPORTED NEVER REPAIRED; (8) volume validity (the existing non-negative-volume rule, matching `OHLCVCandle`/`DataValidator`); (9) chronological gaps via `detect_gaps`; (10) planner-derived coverage over a requested window (default 2023-01-01 -> 2026-08-01; classify COMPLETE / PARTIAL / MISSING / EMPTY / UNAVAILABLE).
- Tolerant raw-row scanning (`_analyze_rows`): every per-row check runs on the RAW persisted rows so an invalid row that could never load into an `OHLCVCandle` (NaN/inf OHLCV, open or close outside [low, high], high < low, negative volume) is still DETECTED and reported with its timestamp (capped at `_MAX_ISSUE_TIMESTAMPS = 5`); never dropped, never repaired. A corrupt JSON / malformed payload surfaces as a `load_error` and the checks fail honestly (the dataset is NEVER silently skipped). Naive stored timestamps are compared for chronology/duplicates via a UTC compare-key (`_ts_key`) so coverage never crashes on a naive row; naive rows are independently flagged by the timezone check.
- Known anomaly handling (explicit requirement): `KNOWN_CORPUS_ANOMALY` = HDFCBANK / 15m / [2024-06-01, 2024-07-01) message "Open price must lie between low and high." The formatter's KNOWN ANOMALY block states the provider anomaly was previously observed and then independently reports the ACTUAL persisted OHLC row count + offending timestamps + corpus coverage + stored candle range + assessment. A clean persisted dataset is reported as clean (NO invalid OHLC); the dataset is NOT auto-classified as failed merely because the ingestion attempt previously encountered this provider anomaly.
- Verdict semantics: `CorpusAuditReport.is_pass` requires every audited dataset COMPLETE in coverage AND zero data-quality integrity failures AND at least one dataset; otherwise `REVIEW REQUIRED`. CORPUS COMPLETENESS and DATA-QUALITY ANOMALIES are reported INDEPENDENTLY (a dataset can be COMPLETE in coverage while still requiring review because of an invalid persisted candle). `integrity_failed` counts only FAIL checks (N_A on missing/empty datasets is not an integrity failure).
- Operator CLI `scripts/audit_historical_corpus.py`: `--start` (required) / `--end` (required) / `--timeframes` (space- or comma-separated, default `15m 1D`) / `--instruments` (space- or comma-separated, default the configured research universe) / `--data-dir` (default `default_historical_data_directory()` = `./data/historical`) / `--json` (machine-readable projection). Exit codes: 0 whenever the audit ran and concluded PASS or REVIEW REQUIRED (a data-quality/completeness finding is reported honestly, NEVER treated as CLI failure); 1 on a service/runtime exception or invalid audit window; 2 for bad CLI args. No `UPSTOX_ANALYTICS_TOKEN` required; no network. Example: `python scripts/audit_historical_corpus.py --start 2023-01-01 --end 2026-08-01 --timeframes 15m 1D --instruments HDFCBANK ICICIBANK NIFTY RELIANCE TCS`.
- Tests `tests/test_corpus_audit.py`: 46 deterministic, network-free tests covering all 14 required areas (all 10 datasets discovered; correct candle counts; chronology detection; duplicate detection; timezone detection; OHLC validation incl. NaN row / open-outside-range / high-below-low / close-outside-range; volume validation incl. negative + non-finite volume; gap reporting; coverage classification COMPLETE/PARTIAL/MISSING/EMPTY/UNAVAILABLE; missing / empty / corrupt dataset handling; known HDFCBANK anomaly reporting for both a clean and an invalid persisted dataset; confirming the audit performs no writes [file-byte snapshot before/after]; confirming the audit does not instantiate/use a live HTTP provider [source-scan + no-provider attribute check]; no future-candle API params; CLI output + exit status incl. space-separated args, `--json`, defaults, token-free run; determinism + model invariants + planner-consistency + existing-pipeline-import regression). No network calls; fixtures written directly into the store layout.
- Full suite post-audit: 4026 passed (3980 + 46 new; 1 pre-existing StarletteDeprecationWarning). Pipeline baseline unchanged (signals=4, trades=3); Checkpoint 3B planner/ingestion demos + tests unchanged (19 checks / 63 tests; 8 checks / 35 tests). Real-corpus audit is an explicit operator action on the Windows machine (`data/historical` present there, not in the sandbox). No Sprint 11A-12E / Product-Phase-1-6F / Checkpoint-3A / Checkpoint-3B regression.

## CHECKPOINT 7 — AUTOMATIC HISTORICAL DATA AVAILABILITY & ACQUISITION LAYER (added)
This checkpoint adds a THIN orchestration / data-availability layer on top of the COMPLETED historical-data architecture (Phase 6A-6D + Checkpoint 3A/3B). It answers ONE question: "The engine needs historical data for (instrument, timeframe, start, end) — is it already available in the persisted corpus, and if not, can the missing monthly chunks be acquired automatically?". It is AVAILABILITY + ACQUISITION ONLY: no trading strategy, prediction, evidence, setup, trade-plan, paper-trading or execution logic; no new HTTP client/provider; no second coverage definition; no completion database. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-12E / Product-Phase-1-6F / Checkpoint-3A / Checkpoint-3B public API signature was changed, no existing engine/model was modified (only 3 new files), and the prior 4026 tests still pass (now 4076 with 50 new in tests/test_historical_data_availability.py; fixture path remains fully deterministic and OFFLINE).
- New models `src/engine/models/historical_availability.py` (frozen+slots): enum `HistoricalAvailabilityStatus` (COMPLETE/ACQUIRED/INCOMPLETE/UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUMENT/UNSUPPORTED_TIMEFRAME/UNPLANNED_TIMEFRAME/INVALID_REQUEST/CREDENTIAL_MISSING/NO_STORE_LOCAL_DATA/NO_ACQUISITION_PATH/NO_STORE_ACQUISITION/ERROR; is_available/is_success/requires_acquisition properties), `AcquisitionFailure` (one failed monthly chunk: instrument/timeframe/start/end/reason — reason is the existing redacted safe string, never a credential), `HistoricalDataAvailabilityResult` (the return contract: instrument/timeframe/request_start/request_end/status/chunks_required/chunks_covered/chunks_acquired/chunks_skipped/chunks_still_missing/acquired_chunk_keys/failures/candles (`OHLCVCandle`)/reference_now/acquisition_attempted/request). `__post_init__` enforces count invariants: `chunks_covered + len(chunks_still_missing) == chunks_required` (final-coverage accounting), `chunks_acquired + chunks_skipped <= chunks_covered`, failures never overlap acquired chunks, every failed chunk is also in `chunks_still_missing`, candle/identity/aware-timeframe invariants; `timeframe` is kept as supplied (NEVER upper-cased — "15m" must not become "15M"; only instrument is upper-cased); INVALID_REQUEST is the one legitimately-empty identity.
- New service `src/engine/data/historical_data_availability.py` (data/__init__.py stays empty; import via `from engine.data.historical_data_availability import HistoricalDataAvailabilityService`): `HistoricalDataAvailabilityService(planner, service=None, *, store=None, timeframes=(), acquisition_engine=None)`. Primary API `get_historical_data(request, *, reference_now=None, label="", metadata=None) -> HistoricalDataAvailabilityResult` + optional `get_historical_data_batch(requests, ...)` (sequential, failure-isolated). FLOW: (1) structural request validation (is-a, naive-timestamp, start<end, unknown provider) -> honest INVALID_REQUEST; (2) planner gates (instrument not in universe -> UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUMENT; uncanonical timeframe -> UNSUPPORTED_TIMEFRAME; canonical but not in the planner's config timeframes and not in the service `timeframes` extension -> UNPLANNED_TIMEFRAME); (3) INITIAL COVERAGE comes from the EXISTING `CorpusPreparationPlanner.plan(...)` row coverage — the single source of coverage/chunk truth (its `DatasetCoverage.required_chunks`/`covered_chunks`/`missing_chunk_keys` semantics, NO copied algorithm); (4) no persisted store -> NO_STORE_ACQUISITION (nothing durable is possible); (5) fully covered -> `_complete_result`: load the canonical window from the EXISTING store/service, ZERO provider requests, NO Upstox token required (offline serve); (6) missing/partial -> resolve the acquisition path (see rules below), build backlog via the EXISTING `CorpusIngestionEngine.build_backlog(start, end, instruments=[instrument])`, then SCOPE the backlog to the REQUESTED timeframe (`scoped_jobs = [job for job in backlog.jobs if job.timeframe == canonical]`, rebuilt as a `CorpusBacklog`) — a 15m request must NEVER ingest 1D chunks; (7) run `engine.run(scoped_backlog, reference_now=now, label=, metadata=)`, mapping COMPLETED->acquired_keys, FAILED->AcquisitionFailure, SKIPPED->skipped count; (8) RE-CHECK coverage AFTER acquisition via a second `planner.plan(...)` (fall back to the pre-run view only on planner exception); (9) status = ACQUIRED when no final missing chunks, INCOMPLETE otherwise; (10) best-effort window load returns the canonical stored candles that ARE available (partial valid data on INCOMPLETE; the still-missing part is NEVER fabricated).
- ACQUISITION-PATH RESOLUTION `_resolve_acquisition(request, now, label, metadata)`: explicit `request.provider` wins (unknown explicit provider -> NO_ACQUISITION_PATH); otherwise the configured `planner.config.provider` is used and the service NEVER silently substitutes a different provider when the configured one is not registered (-> honest NO_ACQUISITION_PATH with the missing chunks listed); `upstox-historical` requires `UPSTOX_ANALYTICS_TOKEN` via the EXISTING `check_upstox_analytics_token()` BEFORE any provider/network request (missing/empty -> CREDENTIAL_MISSING, zero API requests; `UPSTOX_ACCESS_TOKEN` is NEVER read/used/copied); `CorpusIngestionError` containing "UPSTOX_ANALYTICS_TOKEN" is mapped to CREDENTIAL_MISSING; an injected `acquisition_engine` is preferred, otherwise a `CorpusIngestionEngine(self.planner, self.service, CorpusIngestionConfig(provider=resolved, require_upstox_token=False, reference_now=now, ...))` is built ON DEMAND (no second ingestion logic).
- SECURITY: the service module itself defines NO HTTP client, NO URL construction, NO token/env-file reading (`os.environ` never accessed in the module — the existing check_upstox_analytics_token is delegated to), NO Authorization/Bearer construction; all failure reasons are passed through the EXISTING `CorpusIngestionEngine._safe_reason` redaction (Bearer-token patterns and token env-var names removed). Results, errors and logs never expose credentials.
- NO FUTURE DATA / REFERENCE BOUNDARY: the deterministic boundary is the `reference_now` param (default `request.end`); `service.load_historical(instrument, timeframe, start=request.start, end=request.end)` already enforces a windowed load so candles outside `[start, end]` — including future-dated ones — can never be returned; no new future-data rule is invented.
- DETERMINISM/RESUMABILITY: identical inputs + identical corpus state produce identical results; the store is the ONLY resumability state (a re-run finds already-persisted chunks via the planner and re-fetches only the still-missing ones; a failed chunk is never marked covered and is retried on the next call). Multi-chunk acquisition is sequential (NO threads/pools/async — Checkpoint 7 §15).
- Regression-tested guarantees: fully-covered request returns local data with ZERO provider calls and NO token; missing/partial acquisition fetches + persists through the EXISTING service.ingest pipeline only; existing chunks are never re-fetched; rerun after success makes zero provider calls; per-chunk failures are resumable (one failed chunk does not erase acquired chunks); deterministic multi-chunk processing; CREDENTIAL_MISSING before any request; ACCESS_TOKEN never a fallback; invalid provider data cannot surface as canonical candles; future candles cannot be returned; returned candles canonical + chronological; NO completion database / `.done` file / second store (only candles.json + provenance.jsonl). Existing `scripts/ingest_corpus_data.py` and `scripts/ingest_historical_data.py` are UNCHANGED (bulk preparation stays separate from on-demand availability).
- Files: `src/engine/models/historical_availability.py`, `src/engine/data/historical_data_availability.py`, `tests/test_historical_data_availability.py` (50 tests: fully-covered; zero-provider-calls; no-token-when-covered; missing-identification; partial-acquisition; existing-chunks-not-refetched; persistence + next-request availability; rerun-zero-calls; failed-chunk-not-covered/retried/does-not-erase; deterministic multiple chunks; missing-ACCESS-token-prevention + zero-requests + ACCESS-never-fallback + no-token-leakage + Bearer redaction; invalid-provider-data; existing-validation-enforced; future-candle-exclusion; canonical-chronological; planner/store/ingestion-pipeline reuse probes; no-second-HTTP source-scan; no-completion-DB; request-validation incl. naive/reversed/unsupported; timeframe-scoped acquisition; only-requested-dataset-loaded; NO_ACQUISITION_PATH; batch; determinism; regression + no-trading-dependency + no-future-API-params).
- Full suite post-Checkpoint-7: 4076 passed (4026 + 50 new; 1 pre-existing StarletteDeprecationWarning). Pipeline baseline unchanged (signals=4, trades=3); Phase 6A/6B/6C/6D/3A/3B demos unchanged. No Sprint 11A-12E / Product-Phase-1-6F / Checkpoint-3A / Checkpoint-3B regression.

## CHECKPOINT 9 — HISTORICAL SETUP RESEARCH & EVIDENCE PIPELINE (added)
This checkpoint phase establishes the complete historical setup research and evidence pipeline, from setup discovery through evidence aggregation, statistical analysis, quality indicators, and evaluation. It is RESEARCH ONLY: no trading strategy, no decision-engine replacement, no prediction/ML/LLM, no live/paper-trading behavior change. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-12E / Product-Phase-1-7 public API signature was changed, no existing engine/model was modified (only additive new files), and all prior tests continue to pass.

### Checkpoint 9.1 — Setup Discovery
- Models (`src/engine/models/historical_setup_discovery.py`, frozen+slots): `HistoricalSetupCandidate`, `SetupDiscoveryResult`, `SetupCoverageReport`.
- Research boundary only: identifies setup candidates at time T using only information available at T.
- Point-in-time safe: no future candle access.

### Checkpoint 9.5/9.8 — Outcome Observations
- Models (`src/engine/models/historical_setup_outcome.py`, frozen+slots): `ForwardReturnObservation`, `PriceExcursionObservation`, `SetupObservation`, `ObservationStatus` (AVAILABLE/INSUFFICIENT_DATA).
- Direction-neutral fixed-horizon forward price return and max upward/downward price excursion.
- Forward return = (endpoint_price - reference_price) / reference_price.
- Upward excursion = (max_high - reference_price) / reference_price.
- Downward excursion = (min_low - reference_price) / reference_price.
- None when insufficient data (never computed over partial horizon).

### Checkpoint 9.9 — Evidence Aggregation
- Models (`src/engine/models/historical_setup_evidence.py`, frozen+slots): `SetupEvidenceOccurrence`, `SetupEvidenceBatch`.
- One occurrence of a setup criterion with paired neutral observations.
- Immutable collection of occurrences for the same setup criterion.
- Deterministic dedup by occurrence_id, chronological ordering.

### Checkpoint 9.10 — Statistical Evidence Analysis
- Models (`src/engine/models/historical_setup_statistics.py`, frozen+slots): `SetupEvidenceStatisticsReport`.
- Neutral descriptive statistics only: forward return mean/median/min/max, upward/downward excursion mean/median, observation counts.
- Per-metric sufficient-data gating, preserves zero/missing/insufficient distinction.

### Checkpoint 9.11 — Setup Quality Analysis
- Models (`src/engine/models/historical_setup_quality.py`, frozen+slots): `SetupQualityReport`.
- All statistics PLUS: positive/negative/zero forward return counts, proportion_positive_forward_return, forward_return_direction_consistency.
- Consistency defined as max(positive, negative) / (positive + negative), ranges 0.5 (perfectly split) to 1.0 (all agree).
- Does NOT reuse Sprint 11Y EvidenceStrength (incompatible semantics).

### Checkpoint 9 — Key Design Principles
- Deterministic immutable models (frozen+slots dataclasses).
- Optional fields use None so "unobserved" is never silently a real value.
- No business logic in models; __post_init__ structural validation only.
- Explicit rejection of trade-level evidence abstractions.
- Zero/missing/insufficient handling preserved throughout.

## CHECKPOINT 10.1 — HISTORICAL DATA ACQUISITION BOUNDARY AUDIT (added)
- Historical data acquisition boundary with availability checking, missing chunk detection, provider selection.
- Yahoo historical provider and Upstox historical provider integration.
- Validation, persistence, corpus readiness.

## CHECKPOINT 10.2 — AUTOMATIC HISTORICAL DATA ACQUISITION WIRING (added)
- `auto_acquire` automatic acquisition trigger during corpus build.
- Availability-service delegation, existing acquisition pipeline reused.

## CHECKPOINT 10.3 — SETUP EVIDENCE EVALUATION (added)
