## CHECKPOINT 17.6 — REAL BROKER ADAPTER DESIGN (added)

- Status: COMPLETE / PASS.
- Objective: produce the complete, implementation-ready architectural and technical design for the FIRST real broker-specific adapter (selected target broker: Upstox, official V3 REST trading API) against the frozen broker-neutral execution architecture. This is a DESIGN checkpoint ONLY: it answers "exactly how will a real broker adapter be implemented without contaminating the core Trading Intelligence Engine or weakening any execution safety invariant?".
- Design scope: concrete UpstoxBrokerAdapter architecture; complete translation matrix (ExecutionCommand -> Upstox request); instrument/order-type/product/validity/exchange mapping; capability model; authentication/credential boundary + isolation; client-order-ID strategy; broker idempotency strategy; submission protocol (22 validation gates + failure behaviors); timeout/UNKNOWN/reconciliation design; order-state and error mapping; rate-limit/retry policy; network-failure policy; response validation; cancellation design; paper/live isolation; startup safety; live-execution guard checklist; observability; persistence sufficiency audit (existing persistence SUFFICIENT, no schema change); broker API versioning; clock/timing requirements (adapter-boundary clock abstraction DESIGNED for 17.7, not frozen modules); 26-scenario failure-injection test spec; contract-conformance strategy (reuse the frozen reusable conformance suite); dependency direction; concrete 17.7 implementation blueprint; Upstox-specific design appendix (publicly-verified facts vs REQUIRES IMPLEMENTATION-TIME VERIFICATION vs UNKNOWN); security review (20 threats, 0 BLOCKER); repository safety sweep.
- Target broker: Upstox. No broker connection, no SDK, no credentials, no API calls, no network introduced. Existing UPSTOX_ANALYTICS_TOKEN remains a HISTORICAL-DATA-only credential (data provider + corpus precheck) — NOT an execution credential; the future live adapter uses a SEPARATE access-token credential that does not exist in 17.6.
- Major architectural decisions: UpstoxBrokerAdapter is a THIN translation boundary below the frozen BrokerAdapter protocol (BrokerAdapter unchanged); adapter owns all broker-specific mapping tables and translation/normalization; credentials injected into an adapter-owned broker client from a credential provider (adapter never reads the token); place-order maps via instrument_token (NSE_EQ|INE...), transaction_type BUY/SELL, order_type MARKET/LIMIT/SL/SL-M, product D (first scope), validity DAY, tag=deterministic client_order_id mapping; target NOT transmissible (no bracket target on Upstox place) — explicitly dropped, never fabricated; quantity may be floored to lot size but NEVER increased (risk invariant); unsupported fields/semantics always fail closed (no silent discard, no default-product fallback).
- Translation-boundary decisions: core keeps instrument/direction/economics; adapter owns symbol/exchange/order-type/product/validity/price-precision/quantity-step/tag mapping; broker order ids/status strings/products/credentials never enter core or persistence; 10-column translation matrix documents core meaning/broker-neutral repr/Upstox repr/direction/validation/failure/loss/transformation/rounding/capability-check per field.
- Authentication boundary: Execution Engine -> BrokerAdapter (no auth) -> UpstoxBrokerClient (owns auth handshake) -> Credential Provider (injected) -> Broker Authentication. Access token attached ONLY by the client at the network boundary; lifetime/rotation managed by the provider; missing/invalid token -> AUTHENTICATION_FAILURE (TRANSPORT) fail closed; paper/live credential providers isolated.
- Credential isolation: credentials never enter ExecutionCommand/TradePlan/Intent/SubmissionLifecycle/AdapterResult/BrokerError/persistence/logs/audit/test fixtures; 17.7 must implement the broker-client redaction rule (reuse the existing _BEARER_RE pattern).
- Idempotency design: command_id -> client_order_id -> Upstox tag (application identity for reconciliation); idempotency_key stays local duplicate detection only; broker-generated order_id recorded downstream-only on SubmissionLifecycle.broker_order_id; adapter NEVER claims tag/client_order_id provides broker-side idempotency (UNKNOWN — REQUIRES IMPLEMENTATION-TIME VERIFICATION); duplicate response -> normalize with the broker order id (accept-as-duplicate) or UNKNOWN if ambiguous.
- Reconciliation design: primary key = deterministic client_order_id/tag (Upstox order-history supports lookup by tag or order_id — VERIFIED FROM PUBLIC DOCUMENTATION); fallback = broker order_id (justified: returned for the same submission); exact match/no match/multiple matches/malformed/broker-unavailable/stale/duplicate/conflicting outcomes handled; multiple/conflicting -> UNKNOWN, never fabricated; everything normalized into AdapterResult.
- Timeout/UNKNOWN design: bounded request/reconcile timeouts -> BrokerErrorCode.TIMEOUT (AMBIGUOUS) -> AdapterResult.UNKNOWN (never FAILED); UNKNOWN requires reconciliation (frozen enforcing); still-UNKNOWN stays UNKNOWN, retry remains prohibited; TIMEOUT -> FAILED -> RETRY and TIMEOUT -> SUBMIT AGAIN both never; connection reset/partial/malformed responses are AMBIGUOUS -> UNKNOWN; connection-refused/DNS/TLS-handshake are KNOWN FAILURE -> FAILED (safe fresh retry — no request reached the broker).
- Error mapping design: Upstox error codes public examples (UDAPI1004/1007/1056/1055/1008/1036/1003/1154/1158/100041) -> frozen taxonomy; unknown codes -> UNKNOWN_OUTCOME (AMBIGUOUS); insufficient funds/market closed -> BROKER_REJECTION (adapter-mapped); rate limit -> RATE_LIMIT; malformed -> MALFORMED_RESPONSE; broker-specific exceptions never cross the boundary.
- Retry/rate-limit design: SAFE RETRY = GET/reconciliation only; UNSAFE RETRY prohibited; a rate-limit on submit is treated as AMBIGUOUS (broker may have accepted) -> UNKNOWN -> reconcile; bounded backoff + bounded retry budget; never an unbounded loop; "retryability depends on operation semantics + known broker outcome, not merely on the presence of a transport error".
- Paper/live isolation: NO LIVE->PAPER or PAPER->LIVE fallback anywhere; UpstoxBrokerAdapter bound to one ExecutionMode; validate_adapter_mode before EVERY operation; cp17_mode binding verified on reconcile/cancel; live adapter requires a live credential provider; missing/mismatched adapter fails closed.
- Security findings: 20-item threat model — all PASS (frozen or design), 0 CONCERN requiring pre-17.7 remediation, 0 BLOCKER. Credential leakage/wrong broker/mode/instrument/order type/duplicate/unknown/unsafe-retry/stale reconcile/malformed response/outage/rate-limit/schema-change/expiry/auth-failure/cross-contamination/misconfig/persistence-corruption/audit-corruption/dependency contamination all addressed. Repository sweep: 0 .env, 0 secrets, 0 network imports in execution/broker modules, 0 credentials.
- 17.7 implementation blueprint (DESIGNED — do NOT implement now): upstox_broker_models.py (adapter-owned request/response models), upstox_broker_adapter.py (concrete adapter implementing BrokerAdapter; translation/normalization/mapping; paper_upstox_adapter/live_upstox_adapter factories), upstox_broker_client.py (broker client boundary; owns HTTP/auth/redaction/timeout; the ONLY module allowed to reference Upstox endpoints), upstox_credential_provider.py (broker-specific provider protocol; injected into client), tests/test_checkpoint_17_7_*.py (26-scenario failure-injection with mocked client + reusable contract-conformance suite + integration with frozen SubmissionInfrastructure). Generic contract must need NO change; mocked client only, NO network/credentials/SDK.
- Files changed: docs/checkpoint_17_6_real_broker_adapter_design.md (NEW, 53 sections), AGENTS.md (this entry appended). ZERO production-code changes. ZERO test-code changes. NO frozen file modified.
- Test results: 17.2-17.4 suites 342 passed; frozen 14-16 execution suite 627 passed; FULL SUITE 5823 passed / 0 failed / 2 warnings — EXACTLY matches the 17.4/17.5 baseline (no regression). No test modified.
- Confirmation: NO real broker was connected. NO SDK was introduced. NO credentials were added. NO network path was introduced. NO live/sandbox order was submitted/cancelled/modified/reconciled. The only broker interactions remain the deterministic FakeBroker / ReferenceBrokerAdapter simulations.
- Frozen Checkpoints 10-16 confirmation: UNCHANGED architecturally; none reopened; none refactored; no frozen test modified to accommodate 17.6.
- Findings: 40 PASS, 10 CONCERN (design-time implementation details for 17.7, all documented), 0 BLOCKER.
- Overall verdict: PASS — the real broker adapter has been sufficiently designed to proceed to the implementation checkpoint. A PASS does NOT authorize live trading or real broker connection.

The adapter is a TRANSLATION BOUNDARY ONLY: no trading intelligence, no setup analysis, no signal generation, no risk calculation, no authorization decisions, no TradePlan/ExecutionCommand creation.
- Module summary:
  - src/engine/intelligence/upstox_broker_models.py — adapter-owned frozen+slots request/response/error models + enums (UpstoxTransactionType/UpstoxOrderType/UpstoxProduct/UpstoxValidity/UpstoxExchange/UpstoxOrderState/UpstoxErrorKind; UpstoxBrokerRequest/UpstoxOrderData/UpstoxPlaceOrderResponse/UpstoxOrderStateResponse/UpstoxCancelResponse/UpstoxClientFailure). Stdlib only; no core imports.

Missing/invalid credential -> FAIL CLOSED (AUTHENTICATION_FAILURE).
  - src/engine/intelligence/upstox_broker_client.py — UpstoxBrokerClient Protocol (place_order/get_order/cancel_order/check_health; performs NO network operations) + MockUpstoxBrokerClient (deterministic, in-memory, network-free, scenario-injectable) + redact_sensitive (scrubs Authorization: Bearer and UPSTOX_* env-var values). No URL literals; no network imports; no SDK.
  - src/engine/intelligence/upstox_broker_adapter.py — concrete UpstoxBrokerAdapter implementing the frozen BrokerAdapter: request translation, verified instrument map (RELIANCE/TCS/HDFCBANK/ICICIBANK/NIFTY tokens mirroring the historical provider), order-type/product/validity/exchange mapping (fail closed on unknown), capability mapping (SUBMIT+RECONCILE+CANCEL), deterministic client_order_id (derive_client_order_id default broker context so it matches the lifecycle identity) + bounded Upstox tag (uptag- + sha256[:12]), Decimal-safe quantity floor (never increased), LIMIT entry passed verbatim, stop/trigger passed verbatim, target NOT transmitted (documented loss), error/state/response normalization, reconciliation by tag (primary) + broker order id (fallback), cancellation behavior, mode binding (validate_adapter_mode + select_adapter; NO PAPER<->LIVE fallback), paper_upstox_adapter / live_upstox_adapter factories (live factory requires a credential provider yielding a non-empty token; missing -> FAIL CLOSED).
- Translation mapping summary: direction LONG/SHORT -> Upstox BUY/SELL; entry -> LIMIT price (verbatim, positive, fail closed on invalid); stop -> trigger_price (valid geometry: BELOW entry LONG / ABOVE entry SHORT; invalid -> fail closed); quantity floored to positive integer lot multiple (never increased; floors-to-zero -> fail closed); product D (first scope), validity DAY (first scope), exchange from token prefix (NSE_EQ/NSE_INDEX), unsupported -> fail closed; target_2 not transmitted (never fabricated).
- Instrument mapping: verified _DEFAULT_UPSTOX_INSTRUMENT_KEY_MAP (RELIANCE NSE_EQ|INE002A01018, TCS NSE_EQ|INE467B01029, HDFCBANK NSE_EQ|INE040A01034, ICICIBANK NSE_EQ|INE090A01021, NIFTY NSE_INDEX|Nifty 50); configurable via UpstoxBrokerConfig.instrument_key_map; unknown/missing/ambiguous -> fail closed (NEVER a guessed instrument).
- Order-type mapping: LIMIT (entry present, first scope) -> LIMIT; MARKET / SL / SL-M trigger-based variants capability-gated pending real-semantics verification; unsupported semantics -> UnsupportedOrderSemantics (fail closed, never silently changed/exported).
- Capability mapping: AdapterCapabilities (SUBMIT+RECONCILE+CANCEL, mode-bound); supports returns False (never raises) for non-commands/unmapped instruments/invalid geometry; check raises ValueError (fail closed); unadvertised capability call raises ValueError.
- Authentication/credential boundary: injected provider Protocol only; no hard-coded credentials; no real credential loading; credentials NEVER enter ExecutionCommand/TradePlan/Intent/SubmissionLifecycle/AdapterResult/BrokerError/persistence/logs/audit/fixtures; missing/invalid -> AUTHENTICATION_FAILURE (TRANSPORT) fail closed; redaction scrubs Bearer + UPSTOX_* env-var values from every reason string.
- Client-order-ID implementation: client_order_id = derive_client_order_id(command_id, broker_context="default") deterministic + restart-stable; the adapter uses the SAME default broker context as the frozen infrastructure so request identity == lifecycle identity; Upstox tag = "uptag-" + sha256(client_order_id)[:12] bounded collision-safe (the full client_order_id is carried on the request and is never truncated for identity).
- Idempotency behavior: four-way distinction preserved (command_id / client_order_id / idempotency_key / broker_order_id); adapter NEVER claims broker-side idempotency for tag/client_order_id; broker duplicate response normalized safely (accept-with-order-id); ambiguous broker behavior -> UNKNOWN.
- Timeout/UNKNOWN behavior: client TIMEOUT/unknown/malformed -> AMBIGUOUS -> AdapterResult.UNKNOWN (never FAILED); rate-limited submission -> UNKNOWN (RATE_LIMIT, broker may have accepted) -> reconcile; UNKNOWN requires reconciliation and survives restart (RECONCILE_REQUIRED); blind retry of an UNKNOWN submission is prohibited (ReconciliationRequiredError).
- Reconciliation behavior: primary lookup by client_order_id-derived Upstox tag; fallback broker order id where supported; exact match -> confirmed state; no match/multiple matches/malformed/conflicting -> UNKNOWN; broker unavailable -> BROKER_UNAVAILABLE; everything returned to core is AdapterResult (no Upstox response objects cross the boundary).
- Cancellation behavior: valid cancel -> CANCELLED; already-cancelled -> CANCELLED; race-with-fill -> REJECTED (fill authoritative, no false cancellation); timeout/unknown outcome -> UNKNOWN (reconcile); cancel requires the CANCEL capability (fail closed).
- Error normalization: documented Upstox codes -> broker-neutral codes (validation/order-type/broker-rejection); broker-confirmed rejection (documented code OR rejection-text hint on unknown code) -> REJECTED (terminal); deterministic validation failures -> FAILED; unknown error codes -> UNKNOWN/UNKNOWN_OUTCOME; unknown broker errors never escape the boundary.
- Rate-limit/retry behavior: rate-limited submit -> UNKNOWN (not FAILED) -> reconcile; safe retry = GET/reconcile only; blind submission retry prohibited.
- Paper/live isolation: paper_upstox_adapter / live_upstox_adapter mode-bound; validate_adapter_mode before every operation; select_adapter fails closed on missing/mismatched mode; cp17_mode recorded + verified on reconcile; NO LIVE->PAPER or PAPER->LIVE fallback anywhere.
- SubmissionInfrastructure integration: full flow proven end-to-end (Authorized ExecutionCommand -> SubmissionInfrastructure -> SubmissionLifecycle -> UpstoxBrokerAdapter -> MockUpstoxBrokerClient -> AdapterResult -> lifecycle update -> persistence), including UNKNOWN persistence + restart recovery, reconciliation resolving UNKNOWN -> ACCEPTED with the SAME deterministic client_order_id, no-blind-retry, terminal-no-resubmit, duplicate guard, mode binding, deterministic identity, broker-neutrality of the persisted lifecycle.
- Test count: 166 new (97 adapter + 52 client [incl. 26-scenario matrix + no-network + credential-leakage + redaction] + 17 integration). Prior 17.2-17.4 conformance 342 passed; frozen 14-16 execution suite 627 passed.

The deliverable is a fail-closed, deterministic, credential-free live-execution-gate DESIGN plus 97 new deterministic, network-free hardening tests and a complete 53-section audit document. The gate is a design/test artifact only and is NOT wired into any submission path; there is no `live_enabled` shortcut anywhere.
- 17.1-17.8 baseline: 17.1 boundary audit COMPLETE/PASS; 17.2 contract design COMPLETE/PASS; 17.3 infrastructure COMPLETE/PASS; 17.4 reference adapter COMPLETE/PASS; 17.5 real-broker boundary COMPLETE/PASS WITH LIMITATIONS; 17.6 Upstox design COMPLETE/PASS; 17.7 Upstox implementation COMPLETE/PASS; 17.8 sandbox/paper validation COMPLETE/PASS WITH LIMITATIONS (no sandbox credential available; real Upstox behavior unverified). 17.8 baseline 6066 passed / 6 skipped / 2 warnings.
- New files (ADDITIVE ONLY; no existing production file modified): `src/engine/intelligence/execution_gate.py` (live-execution gate DESIGN: LiveExecutionGateState, LiveExecutionGateInput, LiveExecutionGateVerdict, GateVerdict, MANDATORY_GATE_CONDITIONS - 20 mandatory conditions, LiveExecutionGate.evaluate pure/stateless/deterministic, fail-closed, DISABLED default, credential-free, network-free, no execution path), `tests/test_checkpoint_17_9_hardening.py` (97 tests), `docs/checkpoint_17_9_broker_integration_hardening_and_execution_gate_audit.md`. AGENTS.md appended (this entry).
- Architecture audit (Phase 1): single-responsibility chain proven - ExecutionAuthorization -> ExecutionCommand -> SubmissionInfrastructure -> SubmissionLifecycleEngine -> BrokerAdapter -> UpstoxBrokerAdapter -> UpstoxBrokerClient (Protocol) -> MockUpstoxBrokerClient. Proven by source + tests: strategy / TradePlan / ExecutionCommand cannot submit orders; BrokerAdapter cannot authorize or create authorization (no authorize/create_authorization/grant methods); broker client cannot authorize or modify risk/strategy decisions; persistence cannot authorize; reconciliation cannot authorize; recovery cannot create authorization; adapter cannot silently switch execution mode (validate_adapter_mode + select_adapter + cp17_mode binding; no automatic fallback/downgrade/upgrade/substitution).
- Authorization hardening (Phase 3): AUTHORIZED-only command construction verified (UNAUTHORIZED/ELIGIBLE/EXPIRED/REVOKED/SUPERSEDED all rejected by create_execution_command); authorization cannot be forged (intent binding + content-fingerprint verification); altered command -> different command_id; replayed command -> identical command_id (detectable); authorization state immutable and unreachable by broker code; recovery cannot create authorization. FAIL CLOSED verified.
- Execution-mode hardening (Phase 4): PAPER->LIVE and LIVE->PAPER both fail closed; missing/invalid mode fail closed; wrong adapter fail closed; no credential substitution (live_upstox_adapter requires a credential provider; EmptyUpstoxCredentialProvider fails closed AUTHENTICATION_FAILURE); cp17_mode bound on lifecycle; explicit, immutable mode for the operation. Environment identity for sandbox is NOT modeled (documented gate precondition).
- Live execution gate design (Phase 5): LiveExecutionGate requires 20 mandatory conditions simultaneously (explicit live mode, correct broker, correct adapter, valid live credential, credential provenance, authorization state, command validity, risk/quantity constraints, capability support, environment identity, operator explicit authorization, execution gate enabled, startup safety checks, broker health/readiness, reconciliation readiness, audit readiness, no outstanding UNKNOWN, no conflicting recovery, no configuration ambiguity, no safety override). Gate fails closed if ANY is missing; credentials are necessary but NOT sufficient. Gate is DISABLED by default and NOT wired into any submission path.
- Explicit human authorization (Phase 6): NO live authorization mechanism exists and NONE was invented. Documented required future gate: explicit, per-intent, time-bounded, recorded authorization from an approved operator/human; never inferred from env vars/credentials/config/connectivity/startup/previous state. Scope/lifetime/identity/revocation/persistence/audit/restart behavior specified in the document (Phase 6 section).
- Credential hardening (Phase 7): credentials injected only at the broker-client boundary via UpstoxCredentialProvider Protocol; credentials never enter ExecutionCommand / SubmissionLifecycle / SubmissionInfrastructure state / persistence / audit / results / logs / exceptions / fixtures / AGENTS.md / documentation / git. Missing/empty/malformed/invalid credentials fail closed (AUTHENTICATION_FAILURE). redact_sensitive scrubs Authorization: Bearer + UPSTOX_EXECUTION_ACCESS_TOKEN values. Tested end-to-end (store files + audit blob + results contain no token material).
- Credential rotation (Phase 8): EnvironmentUpstoxCredentialProvider reads lazily (rotation picked up at next read); command identity is credential-independent (no credential required to reconstruct an ExecutionCommand); rotation does not change command_id/client_order_id; UNKNOWN submissions remain reconcilable with the same deterministic client id (no credential in the identity); recovery never resubmits; credentials never persisted in lifecycle records.
- Connection lifecycle (Phase 9): Network failure MUST NOT be interpreted as order failure - TIMEOUT -> AMBIGUOUS -> UNKNOWN -> RECONCILE. Never TIMEOUT->FAILED->RETRY SUBMIT. Verified: timeout maps to BrokerResultStatus.UNKNOWN / BrokerErrorCode.TIMEOUT / BrokerErrorCategory.AMBIGUOUS / retryable False.
- UNKNOWN-state (Phase 10): UNKNOWN cannot become FAILED (timeout), CANCELLED, FILLED, ACCEPTED, or safe-to-resubmit without broker confirmation; UNKNOWN survives restart (RECONCILE_REQUIRED via recovery_for_command); UNKNOWN -> RECONCILE_REQUIRED; no blind retry (request_submission on UNKNOWN raises ValueError).
- Reconciliation (Phase 11): primary lookup client_order_id/Upstox tag; fallback broker_order_id documented (adapter boundary currently has no broker_order_id - callers pass it in the integration layer; documented); exact/no/delayed/multiple/conflicting/malformed/stale/filled/cancelled/rejected/partial/unknown behaviors documented; ambiguous -> UNKNOWN; NEVER choose arbitrarily among conflicting records.
- Reconciliation window (Phase 12): Upstox documentation indicates limited order-history retention (17.8 CONCERN #3). Documented: maximum safe reconcile window must be operator-confirmed against the broker contract; after history is unavailable the adapter must return UNKNOWN (never claim a reconciled outcome); audit retention is separate and preserved locally; UNKNOWN escalation + manual-review state designed (documented; no false success claim tested).
- Idempotency (Phase 13): four identities kept distinct - command_id ("cmd-"), client_order_id ("co-"), idempotency_key ("idem-"), broker_order_id (downstream-only). Same command -> same command_id -> same client_order_id -> same idempotency identity across restart; different broker_context -> different client_order_id. **BROKER-SIDE IDEMPOTENCY NOT VERIFIED.** UNKNOWN -> RECONCILE -> determine outcome -> only then consider retry.
- Duplicate prevention (Phase 14): duplicate command -> same identity (detectable); duplicate submission blocked in-process (DuplicateSubmissionError) and after restart (store.command_exists + load_by_command single-record guard + infra pre-check); terminal states never resubmit; repeated reconciliation idempotent; same command can never generate a new order identity during retry/recovery.
- Concurrency / race (Phase 15): single-process serialization via store guard + infra pre-check; two sequential submits of the same command -> DuplicateSubmissionError; store load_by_command raises SubmissionIntegrityError on two records for one command; true multi-worker concurrency is NOT lock-protected - documented: multi-process execution MUST use an external serialization mechanism or fail closed (no unsafe path since submission requires an explicit, serialized caller path).
- Persistence (Phase 16): one current submission record per command; atomic same-dir temp + fsync + os.replace; crash-before-submit -> SAFE_TO_SUBMIT; crash-after-UNKNOWN/submission -> RECONCILE_REQUIRED; terminal -> NO_ACTION; UNKNOWN persisted + reloadable; no automatic live resubmission (recovery is a read-only decision view).
- Audit trail (Phase 17): infra.audit surface reconstructs command identity, client_order_id, idempotency_key, state, terminal flag, provider, execution mode; no credentials. Full forensic chain authorization -> command -> submission -> broker identity -> client order id -> broker order id -> result -> lifecycle -> reconciliation -> recovery decision is reconstructable with deterministic ids + timestamps (Phase 40 verified by test).
- Observability (Phase 18): lifecycle states distinguishable (CREATED/SUBMISSION_REQUESTED/SUBMITTED/ACCEPTED/REJECTED/UNKNOWN/PARTIALLY_FILLED/FILLED/CANCELLED/FAILED); recovery actions SAFE_TO_SUBMIT/RECONCILE_REQUIRED/NO_ACTION; verdict blocking reasons explicit; UNKNOWN is never presented as FAILED and FAILED is never presented as UNKNOWN when the broker outcome is actually known.
- Broker state drift (Phase 19): broker-confirmed state is the authoritative outcome; conflicts reconciled into a NEW lifecycle event (never silently overwriting the prior snapshot); state advances only on confirmed outcome (verified by test).
- Broker response hardening (Phase 20): missing order ids -> UNKNOWN; multiple order ids -> UNKNOWN (never a guessed pick); unknown status -> UNKNOWN; success-envelope-with-error-fields is malformed -> UNKNOWN; malformed/ambiguous -> UNKNOWN; never manufacture success (verified).
- Error taxonomy (Phase 21): broker-specific errors normalized to the broker-neutral BrokerErrorCode/BrokerErrorCategory at the adapter boundary; unknown broker codes -> UNKNOWN_OUTCOME (AMBIGUOUS); auth/validation/rejection/transport/timeout/rate-limit/unavailable/malformed/ambiguous/unknown mapped; no raw Upstox error object leaks (AdapterResult is the only result type).
- Retry policy (Phase 22): SAFE RETRIES = GET/reconciliation/status/non-mutating metadata; UNSAFE RETRIES = ambiguous order submission / unknown submission outcome / any mutating request where outcome unknown; no automatic blind retry of an ambiguous submission; retry budget/delay/exhaustion/manual-intervention/reconciliation-escalation designed; no infinite retries.
- Rate limit (Phase 23): rate-limited submission -> AMBIGUOUS/TRANSPORT -> UNKNOWN (never treated as "not submitted") -> RECONCILE; documented bounded retry/backoff/budget/reconciliation priority/failure escalation.
- Cancellation (Phase 24): open-order/already-cancelled/already-filled/rejected/timeout/unknown/race cases handled; cancellation_timeout -> UNKNOWN (TIMEOUT); cancellation_race_fill -> REJECTED (fill authoritative); unknown cancellation result -> UNKNOWN -> RECONCILE; never assume cancellation succeeded because the request was accepted.
- Startup / restart (Phase 25): startup must identify execution mode / broker / adapter / credential availability / outstanding/UNKNOWN submissions / reconciliation-required records / authorization state / gate state (documented); startup NEVER auto-submits, NEVER auto-retries UNKNOWN, recovery is read-only (verified).
- Config / environment (Phases 28-29): safe default is NO LIVE (adapters default PAPER; gate DISABLED); no live_enabled shortcut; changing configuration cannot silently bypass authorization; explicit environment identity (dev/test/paper/sandbox/production) is NOT modeled - a future gate MUST confirm broker/account/environment identity independently of a mode string (documented).
- Live gate negative matrix (Phase 30): 20-condition negative matrix verified by test - each single missing condition blocks; all-conditions-true allows only with gate enabled + explicit operator authorization; credentials alone never allow; missing condition key treated as False; verdict deterministic.
- Live gate positive matrix (Phase 31): ALLOWED only when all 20 mandatory conditions simultaneously True AND gate enabled AND explicit operator authorization - defined/tested but NOT activated.
- Safety override audit (Phase 32): zero --force / --live / skip_auth / disable_checks / unsafe / debug-execution / test-execution-override / environment-override / direct-broker-call bypass markers in execution modules (AST sweep + test).
- Direct broker access (Phase 33): place_order/get_order/cancel_order exist ONLY on the injected UpstoxBrokerClient boundary; no core module calls Upstox directly (AST sweep + test).
- Network boundary (Phase 34): zero requests/httpx/urllib/aiohttp/socket/websocket imports across execution/broker/persistence modules (AST sweep + test); broker transport isolated to the broker-client boundary; core execution modules broker-neutral.
- Secret/credential sweep (Phase 35): repository-wide sweep (AST + grep): NONE committed, NONE logged, NONE persisted, NONE documented, NONE in fixtures; the ONLY execution-credential env-var reference is the env-var NAME in upstox_credential_provider.py (no default value, no dump).
- Dependency direction (Phase 36): core -> broker-neutral contract -> broker-specific adapter -> broker client -> transport; never core -> Upstox SDK/API; never broker client -> strategy; never adapter -> authorization engine (verified by import graph + tests).
- Test architecture (Phase 37): three categories maintained - (1) deterministic offline unit tests (this suite + all 17.x suites), (2) controlled broker/sandbox integration (MOCKED - no real Upstox), (3) future live tests (NOT built; require explicit separate authorization + env gating + credential gating + environment verification + fail closed). Normal CI remains network-free and deterministic; real-broker tests opt-in only.
- Failure-injection matrix (Phase 38): expanded matrix (credential/auth/authz/timeout/reset/rate-limit/unavailable/malformed/unknown/duplicate/restart/concurrent/reconcile-mismatch/stale/cancel-race/capability/env) - every row has Input/Expected/Actual/Safety-invariant/PASS-CONCERN-BLOCKER classification (documented in the 17.9 doc).
- Operational hardening (Phase 39): repeated submissions/reconciliations/restarts/credential-rotation/disconnect-reconnect/long sessions/stale records/UNKNOWN states - no memory leaks, no state accumulation, no duplicate records, no stale locks, no retry loops, no repeated submission attempts (documented; 17.9 runs deterministic offline tests; no dangerous broker stress tests).
- Auditability/forensics (Phase 40): full execution event chain reconstructable with deterministic ids + timestamps; credentials excluded (verified by test).
- Operator safety (Phase 41): UI/CLI/API cause no accidental execution (no execute paths in dashboard; plan_trade/create_paper_trade/run_paper_trading_cycle NEVER create intents/commands; no default buttons/commands submit; no live-mode indicators exist in UI - there is no live mode; future LIVE requires a separate explicit confirmation boundary). PAPER vs SANDBOX vs LIVE distinction is documented; PAPER/SANDBOX/LIVE are NOT defined as interchangeable.
- Documentation consistency (Phase 42): documentation classifies every claim VERIFIED FROM OFFICIAL DOCUMENTATION / VERIFIED USING MOCKS / VERIFIED AGAINST CONTROLLED BROKER / NOT VERIFIED / DEFERRED; no false claims of live broker support, live order safety, broker-side idempotency, verified sandbox behavior, guaranteed reconciliation beyond broker retention, guaranteed cancellation, or guaranteed fills.
- Regression results (Phase 43): 17.9 tests 97 passed; 17.2-17.4 suites 342 passed; 17.7 Upstox suites 186 passed; frozen 14-16 execution suite 627 passed; planning/paper-trading regression 350 passed; FULL suite 5989 passed baseline + 97 new = 6086 passed, 0 failed, 2 pre-existing deprecation warnings. NOTE: the local sandbox ran the full suite; 0 skipped with optional deps installed. The 17.8 baseline (6066/6 skipped/2 warnings) used a different env.
- Frozen Checkpoints 10-16 confirmation (Phase 44): git status clean except the intended new files; ZERO tracked frozen file modified; frozen tests untouched; broker-neutral contract remains broker-neutral; SubmissionLifecycle/SubmissionInfrastructure broker-neutral; no Upstox dependency leaked into core.
- PASS findings: authorization fail-closed; mode isolation no-fallback; UNKNOWN/reconcile discipline; duplicate/determinism; atomic persistence + recovery; credential isolation + redaction; response/error normalization; cancellation safety; no network/direct-call/override leaks; deterministic gate negative/positive matrix; audit reconstruction; full suite green.
- CONCERN findings (documented, none unsafe): (C01) no temporal re-validation of the command's valid_from/valid_until at the submission boundary (a far-future/expired-window command can be submitted; the command factory enforces valid_until > valid_from at construction, but the PIPELINE does not re-check the window against the submission time - the future gate MUST enforce time validity); (C02) broker health check (check_health) is defined on the client but NOT consulted by the adapter/infrastructure before submit/reconcile/cancel - the gate must add broker-health as an explicit precondition; (C03) sandbox/paper environment identity is NOT modeled (no sandbox mode) - the gate must add explicit environment identity; (C04) multi-process concurrency is not lock-protected (single-process store guard + serialized caller path only) - multi-worker execution requires an external serialization mechanism or fail-closed (no unsafe path exists because submission requires explicit, serialized caller invocation); (C05) reconciliation window vs broker retention + broker-side idempotency + real tag constraints remain UNVERIFIED (deferred to any future controlled connection); (C06) live factory/mock distinction: live_upstox_adapter is still MOCK-backed in 17.9 (no network client exists).
- BLOCKER findings: NONE.

The spec states: "Credentials are necessary but NOT sufficient for live execution."
- Future live state machine (Phase 48): DISABLED -> CONFIGURED -> PRECHECK -> AUTHORIZED -> GATE_VERIFIED -> READY; any failure -> BLOCKED; execution READY -> SUBMISSION_REQUESTED -> SUBMITTED/UNKNOWN -> RECONCILE -> CONFIRMED TERMINAL STATE; UNKNOWN never auto-resubmits.
- Emergency stop (Phase 49): designed - prevents NEW submissions; does NOT blindly mutate existing broker state; existing UNKNOWN orders remain subject to reconciliation; stops on broker-unavailable/reconcile-fail/credential-revoked/gate-revoked/operator-disable/audit-unavailable/corruption-conflict/duplicate-risk.

## CHECKPOINT 19.3 — CONTINUOUS MARKET SCANNING (added)

- Status: COMPLETE / **PASS**. Third checkpoint of the fixed Checkpoint 19 series. Builds the CONTINUOUS-MARKET-SCANNING foundation on the COMPLETED 19.1 universe correctness + 19.2 intraday coverage foundations. The scanner is CONTINUOUS OBSERVATION ONLY — it does NOT decide which stocks are good trades. **BROKER EXECUTION REMAINS COMPLETELY DEFERRED THROUGHOUT CHECKPOINT 19.x.** No 19.4-19.9 work started; nothing committed to git. Full audit document: docs/checkpoint_19_3_continuous_market_scanning_audit.md.
- PURPOSE: "At each scan cycle, what is the current market-data state of the NIFTY Top 200 universe?" A scan cycle result describes MARKET STATE / DATA AVAILABILITY — never whether a trade should be taken.
- AUDIT (20 questions): (1) NO in-process continuous scanner/polling loop/scheduler/worker existed pre-19.3; (2) acquisition flow = IntradayCoverageEngine.assess_universe (19.2); (3) universe = UniverseBuilder.nifty200()/NIFTY200_SYMBOLS (19.1); (4) 19.2 layer via dashboard/intraday_coverage.py; (5) canonical structures = IntradayCoverageReport/IntradayInstrumentCoverage + canonical OHLCVCandle; (6) no scan models/snapshots/schedulers existed (frozen 11U MarketScanner is trade-opportunity intelligence, deliberately NOT reused); (7) no in-process repeated-market observation existed (only external Windows Task Scheduler for the paper-trading CLI); (8) paper-trading/history-research loops unrelated, NOT reused; (9) no existing scanner coupled to signals/planning/execution/brokers; (10) DashboardDataProvider + IntradayCoverageEngine consumable repeatedly (completed-candle boundary preserved; no 19.2 bypass); (11) no hidden once-only-fetch assumption; (12) aware-UTC datetimes with explicit reference_now; (13) NO clock abstraction pre-19.3 — injected clock+waiter DI introduced (only where meaningful); (14) no scheduling utilities — simplest loop chosen; (15) logging = print in CLIs only; (16) exceptions captured per-cycle + per-symbol (never kill the loop); (17) overlap risk guarded by no-overlap policy; (18) FULL/PARTIAL/UNAVAILABLE/COMPLETE_FAILURE/SKIPPED now distinct; (19) repeated-acquisition coverage existed only in 19.2 tests; (20) conventions preserved: frozen+slots models, empty __init__.py full-path imports, deterministic + point-in-time safe, aware timestamps, additive-only changes, formatter full-path import (reporting/__init__.py NOT extended).
- NEW FILES (additive; no existing source modified): src/engine/models/continuous_scan.py (models), src/dashboard/continuous_scanner.py (orchestration), src/engine/reporting/continuous_scan.py (cycle formatter), scripts/scan_market.py (CLI), scripts/test_checkpoint_19_3.py (demo 18 PASS), tests/test_continuous_scanner.py (43), tests/test_scan_market_cli.py (10), docs/checkpoint_19_3_continuous_market_scanning_audit.md.
- MODELS (frozen+slots): MarketScanStatus (FULL_SUCCESS/PARTIAL_SUCCESS/COMPLETE_FAILURE/UNAVAILABLE/SKIPPED — data-availability, never a market verdict); ScannerState (STOPPED/RUNNING/SCANNING/WAITING/STOPPING/FAILED — SCANNER lifecycle, not trade-setup lifecycle); ScanSourceKind (AUTO/MANUAL); ContinuousScanConfig (scan_interval_seconds default 900 documented conservative; skip_intervals_on_overrun; max_cycles; run_forever); ScanCycleMetadata (started/ended/duration/requested/attempted/successful/unavailable/failed + count invariant); PerSymbolScanResult (wraps REUSED 19.2 IntradayInstrumentCoverage BY REFERENCE; status = reused IntradayCoverageStatus; derived available/fresh/needs_attention; optional latest_price via injected price source — absent -> None, never fabricated); MarketScanCycleResult (deterministic cycle_id = "cycle-"+sha256[:16] over timeframe+universe+reference_now+source+manual_run_id — same instant -> same id; __post_init__ enforces EXACTLY-ONE result per requested constituent, no dup/missing/extras); ScanOnceRequest. No trading/setup/score/rank/entry/stop/target/order/position/broker field names (AST-checked).
- ENGINE: ContinuousScannerEngine STATELESS+PURE — run_cycle() executes ONE cycle via the EXISTING 19.2 IntradayCoverageEngine.assess_universe (provider-isolated; no network/broker imports, AST-verified) and projects the coverage report into the cycle result; build("fixture") deterministic default / build("yahoo") OPT-IN live. ContinuousScanner scheduling loop: injected clock + waiter (default time.sleep) for fully deterministic testing; scan_once() manual on-demand honors no-overlap -> SKIPPED when busy; run(cycles=N) blocking bounded loop; start()/stop()/join() background daemon worker; deterministic start/stop/restart (restart resets schedule + cycle list); STOPPED->RUNNING->SCANNING/WAITING->STOPPING->STOPPED.
- NO-OVERLAP: a new cycle is NEVER started while the previous cycle is still running (simple deterministic policy; full 19.8 concurrency/recovery NOT built). Scheduled tick while busy -> SKIPPED record + schedule re-anchored; slow-provider tests prove no concurrent cycles and distinct reference times.
- FAILURE ISOLATION: 200 requested -> 198 valid + 1 stale + 1 provider error still yields complete cycle result with all 200 per-symbol results; per-symbol fetch raise -> PROVIDER_ERROR (safe message), cycle continues; no fabricated data; no silent indefinite retry (deferred 19.8). Only a cycle-level coverage raise -> COMPLETE_FAILURE (empty results + error).
- PARTIAL vs FULL vs COMPLETE vs SKIPPED all distinct and explicit (FULL==every symbol VALID; PARTIAL==cycle completed with >=1 symbol needing attention, never FULL; UNAVAILABLE==empty universe; COMPLETE_FAILURE==no per-symbol results; SKIPPED==no-overlap refusal).
- DETERMINISM: results sorted by canonical instrument (frozen 19.1 order); caller/input/provider order never affects visible result order; same (universe,timeframe,reference_now) -> same cycle_id.
- SCAN INTERVAL configurable (default 900s documented conservative; respects 19.2 provider limits). Tests use injected instant waiters + fake clocks — no real-time sleeping in interval logic.
- MARKET SESSION: REUSED 19.2 engine.data.market_session helpers; each cycle attaches market_session + seconds_until_next_open; session does NOT gate scanning (informational only); 19.2 holiday limitation preserved.
- CLI (scripts/scan_market.py): --provider fixture|yahoo (default fixture; OPT-IN live), --timeframe, --instruments (default NIFTY Top 200), --cycles/--interval/--once/--timeout, --reference-now (aware ISO; deterministic fixture default = Monday 11:00 IST sentinel), --json (pure JSON). Exit 0 = ran (honest findings), 2 = bad args. Banner: MARKET-DATA SCANNING ONLY — no prediction, no setups, no ranking, no broker execution. No credentials required for the normal path.
- TESTS: test_continuous_scanner.py 43 tests across 10 areas (universe acceptance incl. benchmark-201; single-cycle every-symbol + deterministic ordering + per-symbol data + metadata + latest-price; explicit unsupported/stale/provider-error/empty; one-failure-does-not-kill; FULL/PARTIAL/UNAVAILABLE/COMPLETE_FAILURE; cycle-id determinism; continuous loop 3 cycles + interval respected by fake clock + configurable interval + max_cycles cap; SKIPPED-while-busy + slow-provider-no-overlap; graceful stop/duplicate stop/restart/no-orphan; market session OPEN+WEEKEND; boundaries no setup/rank/execution fields + no network imports + pipeline baseline). test_scan_market_cli.py 10 tests (fixture once exit 0, Top-200 default label, pure JSON, deterministic JSON ordering, multi-cycle mode, bad args exit 2, repeated determinism, disclaimers). Demo 18/18 PASS.
- REGRESSION: focused + 19.1 + 19.2 (nifty200_universe, market_session, intraday_coverage, intraday_coverage_yahoo, intraday_coverage_cli) 200 passed; dashboard/provider/data (dashboard, live_data_integration, watchlist_scanner, workstation, yahoo_range_fix, upstox_historical) 480 passed; FULL SUITE **6506 passed / 12 skipped / 2 warnings** (Checkpoint 19.2 baseline 6453 -> +53 net new; skips = opt-in real-broker/sandbox; warnings = pre-existing third-party deprecations). Pipeline baseline unchanged (signals=4, trades=3); 19.1/19.2 regression-safe.
- LIMITATIONS: fixture serves 4 (other 196 honestly UNSUPPORTED; partial never full); live full-200 operator-opt-in and NOT claimed; default waiter is real time.sleep in production (injected waiter = deterministic test path); no-overlap is a simple _busy guard (multi-process locking/supervisor deferred 19.8); scanner state in-memory (no persistence, deferred 19.8); session classification informational only; PriceSource optional presentation hook.

## CHECKPOINT 19.4 — MULTI-TIMEFRAME ANALYSIS (MTF) (added)

This checkpoint is the fourth checkpoint of the fixed Checkpoint 19 series (NIFTY Top 200 → Market Data → Continuous Scanner → **Multi-Timeframe Analysis** → Setup Intelligence → Trade Plan → Ranking → User Alert → User → Manual Execution). It builds the MULTI-TIMEFRAME MARKET-STATE foundation on the frozen 19.1 universe correctness, 19.2 reliable intraday data coverage and 19.3 continuous market scanning. The MTF layer answers: "What does the current market state of this instrument look like across the configured timeframes, and are those timeframe states aligned, mixed, or conflicting?" It is MARKET-STATE ANALYSIS ONLY — it does NOT decide whether something is a high-quality trade (that is 19.5). **BROKER EXECUTION REMAINS COMPLETELY DEFERRED THROUGHOUT CHECKPOINT 19.x** (Checkpoints 13-18 frozen; execution gate DISABLED). No 19.5-19.9 work started; nothing committed to git. Full audit document: `docs/checkpoint_19_4_multi_timeframe_analysis_audit.md`.

- ARCHITECTURE-FIRST AUDIT findings (24 questions, all answered; documented in the audit doc): (1) NO existing multi-timeframe intraday analysis exists; the Sprint 11U `MTFAlignmentEngine` maps ONE higher-context to ONE lower decision direction (11U opportunity flow) and is NOT reused as a same-kind multi-frame aggregation; (2) existing analytical state that is reusable safely: Sprint 11P `MarketContextEngine.analyze_at` (market structure / swing structure / descriptive trend / range / support-resistance; deterministic; point-in-time safe; LIVE-PATH-PROVEN by the Product dashboard); (3) Sprint 11O candle patterns, 11Q setup confluence, 11R/11S candidates/decisions, 11W-X/12x evidence/decision intelligence, and Checkpoints 12-18 execution layers are deliberately NOT imported (setup/decision/execution domains); (4) end-to-end data flow reuses the frozen 19.2 `DashboardDataProvider` + `IntradayCoverageEngine` capability-discovery / normalization / completed-candle / freshness path; (5) no existing component representing a multi-frame "snapshot" of same-kind states existed (the 19.3 scanner is a single-frame universe cycle; reused only by convention: per-symbol failure isolation, deterministic ordering, explicit reference time); (6) canonical candle model = `OHLCVCandle` with `DataValidator` semantics (reused); (7) canonical timeframe vocabulary = `engine/data/historical_times.py` (`HISTORICAL_TIMEFRAME_SECONDS`, `canonical_timeframe`, aliases 15M→15m / 1D→1d / 60m→1h); (8) timeframes are represented across the project as canonical labels; (9) NO timeframe resampling/conversion exists anywhere (so none was built — provider-native intervals are preferred); (10-11) resampling question moot (no resampling added); provider-native is used instead, keeping look-ahead risk at zero; (12-13) provider intervals audited: Fixture = 15m ONLY (offline), Yahoo OPT-IN live = 1m/2m/5m/15m/30m/60m/1h/90m/1D native, Upstox = historical 15m/1D verified keys only; (14) 19.2 freshness/session logic reused verbatim (`IntradayCoverageStatus` + `market_session`); (15-16) the 19.3 scanner obtains per-instrument single-frame coverage via the 19.2 layer; it assumes ONE timeframe per cycle; (17) a clean MTF boundary was introduced WITHOUT modifying the scanner (19.4 is an additive consumer of the same canonical layer); (18) the Sprint 11P `MarketContext` IS the existing market snapshot (reused as the per-frame payload); (19-21) existing tests cover timeframe alignment (11U scanner) and completed candles / point-in-time (19.2/11U); new 19.4 tests explicitly prove MTF alignment-without-identical-closes and anti-lookahead; (22) no future-bar usage found (all completed-candle boundaries structural); (23) broker/execution is completely uncoupled from analysis (nothing execution-related imported; AST-tested); (24) conventions from Checkpoints 9-19.3 respected: frozen+slots models, empty packages + full-path imports, models <- data <- dashboard dependency direction, formatter full-path import (reporting/__init__.py NOT extended), documented descriptive-only statuses, explicit reference-now determinism, additive-only changes, operator CLI.

- SELECTED MTF TIMEFRAME SET: **("15m", "1h")** — the smallest set supported by the existing architecture for live intraday MTF analysis: 15m is the canonical 19.2/19.3 layout frame (fixture/Yahoo/Upstox native); 1h is a strictly higher intraday frame, **provider-native** on the OPT-IN Yahoo live provider (NO resampling required). The offline fixture honestly reports 1h as UNSUPPORTED_TIMEFRAME → default MTF results are explicitly INCOMPLETE (never fabricated). Configuration is future-proof: `MtfAnalysisConfig(timeframes=...)` accepts any non-empty strictly-ascending-duration subset of the canonical intraday set (e.g. ("5m","15m","1h")); duplicates / unknown / unordered / 1D / non-canonical labels are rejected at construction.

- NEW FILES (additive; no existing source modified — git status shows only untracked new files): `src/engine/config/mtf_analysis_config.py` (`MtfAnalysisConfig`, `canonicalize_timeframes`, `max_lookback_bars=150`, `min_candles_per_timeframe=8`); `src/engine/models/mtf_analysis.py` (frozen+slots `MTFDirection` [BULLISH/BEARISH/NEUTRAL/RANGE/UNKNOWN], `MarketStateCompleteness` [COMPLETE/INCOMPLETE/UNSUPPORTED/UNAVAILABLE], `MtfAlignmentState` [ALIGNED/MIXED/CONFLICTING/INCOMPLETE/UNAVAILABLE], `MtfTimeframeState`, `PerSymbolMtfResult`, `MtfCounts`/`MtfUniverseAnalysis`, pure helpers `classify_alignment`, `compute_completeness`, `mtf_analysis_id`; model layer uses a LAZY `_canonical_timeframe()` helper so it never imports `engine.data` at module scope — repo model-layer convention); `src/dashboard/mtf_analysis.py` (`MtfAnalysisEngine` orchestration consuming the canonical 19.2 `DashboardDataProvider` + `IntradayCoverageEngine` per frame + reused Sprint 11P `MarketContextEngine` on the completed prefix; per-frame and per-symbol failure isolation; `MtfAnalysisViewBuilder` JSON projection); `src/engine/reporting/mtf_analysis.py` (`MtfAnalysisFormatter` — str, deterministic, sections incl. TIMEFRAME STATES + ALIGNMENT REASON + DISCLAIMER "ALIGNED/CONFLICTING/MIXED are TIME FRAME RELATIONSHIP classifications — NOT trade signals/not setups/not predictions"; full-path import, reporting/__init__.py NOT extended); `scripts/analyze_mtf.py` (operator CLI: `--provider fixture|yahoo` [default fixture, offline/deterministic; live OPT-IN], `--timeframes`, `--instruments` [default = NIFTY Top 200], `--reference-now` [deterministic Friday 11:00 IST sentinel for fixture], `--json` [pure machine JSON]; exit 0 ran / 2 bad args; banner "MULTI-TIMEFRAME MARKET-STATE ANALYSIS ONLY — no setups, no ranking, no broker execution"); `tests/test_mtf_analysis.py` (71 tests), `tests/test_mtf_analysis_cli.py` (13 tests), `scripts/test_checkpoint_19_4.py` (demo, 21 PASS/0 FAIL), `docs/checkpoint_19_4_multi_timeframe_analysis_audit.md`.

- ENGINE BEHAVIOUR: per analysis at reference time T (caller-supplied aware UTC; NO wall-clock): for each symbol (canonical sorted) and each configured timeframe (ascending duration), ONE provider request only (200×N logical requests per full-universe cycle, documented); `is_timeframe_supported` / `supports_instrument` short-circuit (never fetch an unsupported frame — tested); raw response normalized by the REUSED 19.2 `IntradayCoverageEngine` (canonical `OHLCVCandle` rebuild, completed-candle boundary via `split_completed_candles`, `DataValidator` re-validation, session-aware freshness, intraday gap refinement); per-frame `MtfTimeframeState` uses the 19.2 `IntradayCoverageStatus` taxonomy VERBATIM (no parallel status set) and carries the Sprint 11P `MarketContext` (structure/trend/range/S-R) computed ONLY from completed candles <= T; derived `MTFDirection` (BULLISH/BEARISH explicit; RANGE/NEUTRAL -> NEUTRAL frame; no structure/data -> UNKNOWN; missing evidence never fabricated). COMPLETED-CANDLE RULE: a candle is COMPLETED when `timestamp + duration <= T`; the currently-FORMING candle is carried separately and never fed to the market-state engine; future-dated candles are excluded + counted. TIMESTAMP ALIGNMENT: frames align at the reference instant by each frame's OWN latest completed candle — identical candle timestamps are NEVER required (5m@05:15 UTC with 1h@04:00 UTC at T=05:30 UTC is a valid alignment; documented + tested). ALIGNMENT/RELATIONSHIP classification (`classify_alignment`, pure + deterministic + direction-symmetric): ALIGNED = all usable frames share the same directional token; CONFLICTING = >=2 usable frames observe opposite directional tokens; MIXED = >=2 usable frames not all-same and no opposite pair (e.g. directional + NEUTRAL/RANGE, or NEUTRAL + NEUTRAL); INCOMPLETE = <2 usable frames but >=1 usable; UNAVAILABLE = no usable frame. This NEVER means BUY/SELL/TRADE/NO-TRADE (tests assert those strings never appear in the vocabulary). COMPLETENESS: COMPLETE (all frames usable) / INCOMPLETE (any frame lacks usable state) / UNSUPPORTED (all frames unsupported) / UNAVAILABLE (no usable frame). MISSING/STALE/ERROR: every requested timeframe and symbol always yields an explicit result (state/timeframe_count always == len(config timeframes)); one timeframe failure -> INCOMPLETE symbol (all frames preserved); one symbol failure -> that symbol error result, universe continues (failure isolation at both levels). UNIVERSE-WIDE: `MtfUniverseAnalysis` with `analysis_id = "mtf-"+sha256[:16]` (deterministic over canonical inputs incl. reference now), per-symbol results sorted exactly-once, explicit counts (tested/complete/incomplete/unsupported/unavailable + aligned/mixed/conflicting/alignment_incomplete/alignment_unavailable + completeness_ratio) — partial coverage NEVER reported as full (fixture default reports complete == 0 honestly).

- POINT-IN-TIME SAFETY (anti-lookahead, proven by tests): appending future candles to ANY frame (including a future 1h candle that would CLOSE after T and one opening exactly at T) leaves the market-state, completeness, alignment, reason and analysis_id at the earlier T unchanged (per-symbol AND universe-wide); the 15m boundary candle closing EXACTLY at T is included; the forming 15m/1h candle is never treated as completed. The engine consumes ONLY completed candles <= T for each frame and never calls wall-clock.

- CLI/OPERATOR: `scripts/analyze_mtf.py` — fully offline default (`--provider fixture`), deterministic `--reference-now` sentinel, `--json` pure output, universe default = NIFTY Top 200 manifest, per-symbol + universe reports, explicit testing that the default fixture 1h frame stays UNSUPPORTED (never fabricated). Live Yahoo is an explicit opt-in; NOT claimed to be demonstrated for the full Top 200.

- TESTS ADDED: `tests/test_mtf_analysis.py` 71 (universe acceptance incl. exactly-200 + every-symbol-once; config validation incl. duplicates/unknown/unordered/alias/daily-rejected; explicit per-timeframe valid/stale/provider-error/unsupported-instrument/unsupported-timeframe/insufficient/invalid-empty; completed-candle boundary + forming + future rejection; anti-lookahead per-symbol + universe-wide; timestamp alignment without identical closes; ALIGNED/MIXED/CONFLICTING/INCOMPLETE/UNAVAILABLE classification incl. RANGE-as-neutral-never-directional; failure isolation; determinism + id stability + input-order independence; data efficiency single-fetch-per-frame; no forbidden trading semantics + no broker/network imports; historical-pipeline baseline guard); `tests/test_mtf_analysis_cli.py` 13 (fixture run exit 0, default Top-200 label, JSON purity, deterministic JSON, unsupported 1h explicit, bad-args exit 2, manual timeframe set, no BUY/SELL/setup language). Demo `scripts/test_checkpoint_19_4.py`: 21 PASS / 0 FAIL (deterministic scripted multi-TF provider; RELIANCE ALIGNED, TCS CONFLICTING, MIXED RANGE-neutral case, INCOMPLETE/UNSUPPORTED states, anti-lookahead, pipeline baseline signals=4/trades=3).

- REGRESSION: focused 84 passed; 19.1 `test_nifty200_universe.py` 48 passed; 19.2 `test_market_session` + `test_intraday_coverage*` 200 passed; 19.3 `test_continuous_scanner` + `test_scan_market_cli` 53 passed; market-data/provider/dashboard subset (dashboard, live_data_integration, watchlist_scanner, workstation, yahoo_range_fix, upstox_historical, historical_data_foundation/availability/consumer, corpus_preparation/ingestion/audit, research_corpus) 905 passed; FULL SUITE **6590 passed / 12 skipped / 2 warnings / 0 failed** (~123 s; collected 6602 tests = 6518 baseline + 84 new). This EXACTLY equals the documented 19.3 baseline **6506 passed** + **84 new** with the same 12 opt-in real-broker/sandbox skips and the same 2 pre-existing third-party deprecation warnings (StarletteDeprecationWarning + anyio BlockingPortal). ## CHECKPOINT 19.5 — SETUP-QUALITY INTELLIGENCE (added)

- Status: COMPLETE / **PASS**. Fifth checkpoint of the fixed Checkpoint 19 series (NIFTY Top 200 → Market Data → Continuous Scanner → Multi-Timeframe Analysis → **Setup Intelligence** → Trade Plan → Ranking → User Alert → User → Manual Execution). Builds the SETUP-QUALITY INTELLIGENCE layer on the FROZEN 19.1 universe correctness, 19.2 intraday coverage, 19.3 continuous scanning and 19.4 MTF analysis. It answers: "Given the market state currently available for this instrument, does it contain a potentially meaningful trading setup, how strong is that setup according to the defined quality model, and why?" — SETUP DETECTION / QUALITY ASSESSMENT / EXPLANATION / CANDIDATE RANKING ONLY. **BROKER EXECUTION REMAINS COMPLETELY DEFERRED THROUGHOUT CHECKPOINT 19.x** (Checkpoints 13-18 frozen; execution gate DISABLED). No 19.6-19.9 work started; nothing committed to git. Full audit document: `docs/checkpoint_19_5_setup_quality_intelligence_audit.md`.
- ARCHITECTURE-FIRST AUDIT findings (all documented in the audit doc): (1) NO existing setup-quality layer existed; (2) existing reusable LIVE-PATH-COMPATIBLE intelligence = Sprint 11P `MarketContextEngine`/`MarketTrendEngine`/`RangeDetectionEngine`/`SupportResistanceContextEngine`/`SwingEngine`/`MarketStructureEngine`/`StructureAnalysisEngine` (product-dashboard-proven), Sprint 11O `CandlePatternEngine`, Sprint 11Q `SetupConfluenceEngine`; (3) HISTORICAL/RESEARCH-ONLY (NOT live-intraday-path compatible at 19.5) = BOS/CHOCH/signal-path trend/liquidity/volume/strength, Sprint 11R-11T candidate/decision/opportunity, 11U `MarketScanner`, Checkpoint 9.x historical setup quality; (4) existing setup taxonomy = Sprint 11R `SetupType` (BREAKOUT/TREND_CONTINUATION/STRUCTURE_CONTINUATION/SETUP_CANDIDATE/RANGE_REJECTION) REUSED verbatim WITHOUT any geometry; (5) existing setup detection = Sprint 11Q `SetupConfluenceEngine.assess` REUSED verbatim (POTENTIAL_SETUP/WATCH/NO_SETUP + confluence_score + structured evidence) — NO new detection heuristic invented; (6) existing scoring conventions = Sprint 11S `DecisionScore` (mapped, defect-scored) is a trade-plan DECISION score, NOT setup quality — 19.5 defines a NEW non-overlapping quality classification; (7) 19.4 `PerSymbolMtfResult` is the PRIMARY input (consumed BY REFERENCE); (8) 19.3 scan/19.2 data-status/19.1 universe conventions all preserved; (9) no existing setup logic uses future data (all reused engines point-in-time safe); (10) no broker/execution imports near the intended layer (AST-verified).
- NEW `src/engine/config/setup_quality_config.py`: `SetupQualityConfig` (frozen+slots, validated). Weights sum to 100: `mtf_alignment_weight=30`, `setup_confirmation_weight=30`, `setup_confluence_weight=25`, `structure_quality_weight=15`; `max_score=100`. Quality bands: `excellent_threshold=80`/`high_threshold=65`/`medium_threshold=50`/`low_threshold=35` (below 35 = REJECTED). Classification CAPS (each can only reduce): `watch_max_classification=LOW`, `no_setup_classification=REJECTED`, `conflict_max_classification=MEDIUM`, `stale_data_max_classification=LOW`, `incomplete_mtf_max_classification=LOW`; `partial_fraction=0.5`; `primary_timeframe=None` (lowest-duration); `max_qualified=None` (top-N cap). `classification_for_score()` + `snapshot()`.
- NEW `src/engine/models/setup_quality.py` (frozen+slots): `SetupQualityStatus` (QUALIFIED/WATCH/NO_SETUP/INCOMPLETE/UNAVAILABLE — evaluation state, NOT a lifecycle), `SetupQualityClassification` (EXCELLENT>HIGH>MEDIUM>LOW>REJECTED quality bands + INCOMPLETE/UNAVAILABLE data-gate states; `rank_value`/`is_qualified_band`), `SetupQualityScoreComponent` (name/points/max_points/reason), `SetupQualityResult` (instrument/reference_now/mtf BY REFERENCE/primary_timeframe/status/classification/score/score_components/positive_factors/negative_factors/setup_classification [reused 11Q]/setup_direction/setup_type [reused 11R taxonomy]/confluence_score/has_conflict/data_complete/stale_data/explanation/reason; `score_component_map`; `__post_init__` enforces mtf.instrument==instrument, data-gate states carry no score, QUALIFIED requires a score), `SetupQualityCounts` (qualified/watch/no_setup/incomplete/unavailable + tested + qualified_ratio + band counts; invariants), `SetupQualityUniverseResult` (analysis_id "sq-"+sha256[:16]/timeframes/primary/universe_instrument_count/instruments/results EXACTLY-ONCE-per-constituent/counts/ranked_qualified/top_n/error; `result_for`), `setup_quality_analysis_id(...)` (deterministic over timeframes+universe+reference instant+config snapshot = scoring-rule version).
- NEW `src/dashboard/setup_quality.py`: `SetupQualityEngine(config).evaluate(mtf_result, primary_candles) -> SetupQualityResult` + `.evaluate_universe(analysis, primary_candles_by_instrument) -> SetupQualityUniverseResult`. STATELESS + PURE + DETERMINISTIC. DATA GATE (node fabricated values): 19.4 completeness UNAVAILABLE -> UNAVAILABLE/None; UNSUPPORTED -> INCOMPLETE/None; primary frame missing/no market state -> INCOMPLETE/None; STALE primary -> STALE flag + LOW cap; INCOMPLETE MTF -> zero MTF credit + LOW cap. DETECTION REUSE: `CandlePatternEngine.detect(primary_candles)` (11O) at the LAST index of the SUPPLIED completed candles -> `SetupConfluenceEngine.assess(...)` (11Q) -> SetupAssessment. SCORING: mtf_alignment (ALIGNED=30/MIXED=15/CONFLICTING=0/INCOMPLETE=0/UNAVAILABLE=0), setup_confirmation (POTENTIAL_SETUP=30/WATCH=15/NO_SETUP=0), setup_confluence (`round(confluence/5*25)`), structure_quality (intact & >=2 swings=15 / >=1 swing=8 / none=0); total = integer sum clamped [0,100]. CLASSIFICATION: band from score then CAPS (conflict/stale/incomplete/watch/no-setup). SETUP TYPE: reuses the 11R taxonomy + documented rules (BREAKOUT when price beyond a structural level in the candidate direction; TREND_CONTINUATION when trend+structure aligned at a constructive pullback location; STRUCTURE_CONTINUATION when structure aligned but trend not; else SETUP_CANDIDATE) WITHOUT computing any geometry. RANKING (deterministic, strongest-first): classification rank desc -> score desc -> confluence desc -> completeness (COMPLETE first) -> fewer negative factors -> instrument name ascending (final tie-break). Direction is NOT a ranking key. Top-N (`max_qualified`) keeps the full ranked set internally. `SetupQualityViewBuilder` (result_to_jsonable/universe_to_jsonable, deterministic).
- NEW `src/engine/reporting/setup_quality.py`: `SetupQualityFormatter(width=80)` — `.format_result(result)` / `.format_universe(result)`. Stateless, deterministic, returns str (no print()). Sections: SETUP-QUALITY INTELLIGENCE header, instrument/reference/primary/MTF alignment+completeness/status/classification/score/setup (11Q)/direction/type/confluence/conflict/data-complete/stale, SCORE COMPONENTS (auditable per-component points/max/reason), POSITIVE EVIDENCE, NEGATIVE EVIDENCE, EXPLANATION, REASON + WARNING ("Setup-quality classifications are descriptive technical evidence... NOT a trade signal... does NOT constitute a trading recommendation. No trade execution, order placement, alert, or setup lifecycle is performed."). Full-path import (reporting/__init__.py NOT extended, matching 11O-19.4 convention).
- NEW `scripts/analyze_setup_quality.py`: operator CLI. `--provider fixture|yahoo` (fixture default deterministic offline; yahoo OPT-IN live), `--timeframes 15m,1h` (canonicalized+validated), `--instruments` (default = NIFTY Top 200 from the 19.1 manifest), `--reference-now` (deterministic fixture sentinel; naive rejected), `--top-n`, `--json` (pure deterministic machine JSON). Exit 0 ran / 1 runtime / 2 bad args. Banner: SETUP-QUALITY INTELLIGENCE ONLY — no trade execution, no alerts, no setup lifecycle, no broker execution.
- POINT-IN-TIME SAFETY (structural + adversarial regression-tested): the engine is a pure function of the 19.4 `PerSymbolMtfResult` (already derived from completed candles <= T by the frozen 19.4/19.2 boundary) + the caller-supplied COMPLETED primary candles; it never reads a provider/store/future candle and attributes patterns only to the last index of the supplied candles. Adversarial tests prove appending arbitrary future candles (future breakout / BOS / higher-timeframe confirmation / trend change / support-resistance event) leaves an already-computed setup-quality result at T byte-identical (status/score/classification/components/factors/explanation).

- NEW `src/engine/config/setup_lifecycle_config.py`: `SetupLifecycleConfig` (frozen+slots, validated) — `max_consecutive_missing_observations=5`, `identity_version=1`, `confirm_on_qualified=True` (single-shot confirmation is the ONLY implemented mode; the field truthfully documents it); `LIFECYCLE_MODEL_VERSION=1`; `transition_rule_version(config)` = 16-char sha256 of the config snapshot (transition-rule versioning). NO magic numbers in the engine.
- NEW `src/engine/models/setup_lifecycle.py` (frozen+slots): `SetupLifecycleState` (DETECTED/CONFIRMED/INVALIDATED/EXPIRED — the smallest deterministic machine; `is_terminal` = INVALIDATED/EXPIRED; no execution vocabulary), `LifecycleObservationStatus` (OBSERVED/ABSENT/DATA_UNAVAILABLE/SUPERSEDED/LATE_REJECTED — per-observation statuses, DELIBERATELY distinct from 19.5 SetupQualityStatus and execution/paper-trade statuses), `SetupIdentity` (instrument/direction/setup_type/primary_timeframe/identity_version + setup_id), `SetupLifecycle` (immutable snapshot: lifecycle_id, instance_discriminator, setup_id, identity, state, created_at, last_observation_timestamp, consecutive_missing, latest_observation_id, observations tuple, transitions tuple, latest_score/classification/status, latest_observation_status, explanation), `SetupLifecycleObservation`, `SetupLifecycleTransition`, `SetupLifecycleObservationResult`, `SetupLifecycleCounts`, `SetupLifecycleCycleResult`; helpers `build_setup_id` (`"setup-"+sha256[:16]`), `opposite_direction`, `LIFECYCLE_MODEL_VERSION`.
- NEW `src/dashboard/setup_lifecycle.py`: `SetupLifecycleEngine(config, store)` (stateless apart from the injected store) — `process(result, scan_cycle_id, timestamp)` (per-assessment), `process_universe(SetupQualityUniverseResult, scan_cycle_id, timestamp)` (per-cycle, deterministic counts), `close`/`advance` lifecycle helpers; `resolve_setup_identity` (directional candidate only; WATCH/NO_SETUP/data-gate → None); `is_qualified_assessment` (POTENTIAL_SETUP + QUALIFIED status + not-stale — a stale-data-capped LOW assessment NEVER confirms); `SetupLifecycleStore` (IN-MEMORY deterministic repository: save/load/delete/list/active_lifecycles/terminal_lifecycles/active_for_instrument/active_for_setup/lifecycles_for_*/counts/reset; no file I/O — 19.8 owns persistence/recovery); `build_lifecycle_id`, `build_observation_id`, `build_transition_id`.
- SETUP IDENTITY: setup_id = `setup-` + sha256[:16] over (instrument[canonical], direction BULLISH/BEARISH, setup_type[19.5 SetupType], primary_timeframe[canonical], identity_version=1). Deterministic, reproducible, explainable, point-in-time safe. NEVER depends on scan-cycle id, wall-clock time, provider order, object identity, UUIDs, score/classification/MTF-state/transient data-quality. Direction IS an identity component (documented: LONG-scale vs SHORT-scale candidates are analytically distinct; an opposing-direction appearance SUPERSEDES + INVALIDATES the tracked lifecycle). SetupType IS an identity component (BREAKOUT vs TREND_CONTINUATION are distinct; a type change is a different setup by construction). Score/band/stale/MTF/status are assessment fields, NOT identity.
- LIFECYCLE STATE MACHINE (deterministic; previous state + previous assessment + current assessment → same next state): first observation → DETECTED unless the confirmation predicate holds on the FIRST observation (→ CONFIRMED directly); DETECTED → CONFIRMED on the first qualified observation (sticky, one-way, no un-confirm); DETECTED/CONFIRMED → INVALIDATED ONLY via SUPERSEDED (opposing direction, same instrument/timeframe); DETECTED/CONFIRMED → EXPIRED after `max_consecutive_missing_observations` (5) consecutive CLEAN ABSENT cycles. ABSENT = clean non-observation (NO_SETUP/WATCH-without-direction), advances the miss counter, state unchanged until threshold. DATA_UNAVAILABLE = INCOMPLETE/UNAVAILABLE/stale/missing-symbol — state UNCHANGED and the miss counter NOT advanced (data failure is never invalidation, never advances expiry). LATE_REJECTED = genuinely out-of-chronological-order observation — rejected (`late_rejected=True`), NOT persisted, state unchanged. Terminal lifecycles cannot reopen; reappearance after terminal starts a NEW instance (same setup_id, distinct lifecycle_id via instance_discriminator).
- POINT-IN-TIME SAFETY: engine NEVER calls wall-clock, NEVER reads candles/data, consumes only the supplied assessment + prior history. Tests prove future observations/confirmation/invalidation/MTF information cannot mutate earlier state or history; history is an accurate record of what was known at the time.
- IDEMPOTENCY: same (scan cycle, setup id, assessment fingerprint) processed twice → duplicate result, NO new observation/transition; observation IDs are deterministic (lifecycle_id + scan_cycle_id + timestamp + status + fingerprint); process_universe twice → identical cycle results + history.
- ORDERING: universe processed in canonical instrument order; provider/scan/parallel/dict order cannot determine state; explicit timestamps + scan-cycle identity drive decisions; naive timestamps rejected at the CLI boundary.
- NEW `src/engine/reporting/setup_lifecycle.py` (full-path import; reporting/__init__.py NOT extended): `SetupLifecycleFormatter` — `format_lifecycle_snapshot(...)` returns str with all lifecycle fields + observations + transitions + WARNING ("setup lifecycle state is descriptive technical evidence ... NOT a trade signal OR execution state ... no alert / no broker"). No print() in formatter.
- NEW `scripts/analyze_setup_lifecycle.py`: operator CLI. `--json` (pure deterministic machine JSON), `--timestamp <aware-UTC ISO>` (naive rejected with exit 2), default deterministic fixture sequence (RELIANCE/TCS lifecycle creation, confirmation, ABSENT, DATA_UNAVAILABLE pause, EXPIRED after 5 clean misses, opposite-direction INVALIDATED + reappearance as new instance). Exit 0 ran / 1 runtime / 2 bad args. Banner: SETUP LIFECYCLE ANALYSIS ONLY — no trade execution, no alerts, no persistence, no broker execution.

## CHECKPOINT 19.7 - USER ALERTS (added)

- Status: COMPLETE / **PASS**. Seventh checkpoint of the fixed Checkpoint 19 series (NIFTY Top 200 -> Market Data -> Continuous Scanner -> Multi-Timeframe Analysis -> Setup Intelligence -> Setup Lifecycle -> **User Alerts** -> User -> Manual Execution). Builds the USER ALERTS layer on the FROZEN 19.1 universe correctness, 19.2 intraday coverage, 19.3 continuous scanning, 19.4 MTF analysis, 19.5 setup-quality intelligence and 19.6 setup lifecycle. It answers: "Which lifecycle events are important enough to notify the user about, what should the notification contain, and how should duplicate / noisy notifications be prevented?" - LIFECYCLE EVENT vs ALERT EVENT are SEPARATE concerns: a lifecycle event means "something happened to this setup"; an alert means "this event is important enough to tell the user". **BROKER EXECUTION REMAINS COMPLETELY DEFERRED THROUGHOUT CHECKPOINT 19.x** (Checkpoints 13-18 frozen; execution gate DISABLED). No 19.8-19.9 work started; nothing committed to git. Full audit document: `docs/checkpoint_19_7_user_alerts_audit.md`.
- ARCHITECTURE: 19.5 Setup Quality -> 19.6 Setup Lifecycle (events) -> Lifecycle Transition / Event -> 19.7 Alert Eligibility -> Alert Construction -> Identity-Based Deduplication / Suppression -> AlertEvent -> Delivery Channel -> User. 19.7 consumes the FROZEN 19.6 lifecycle EVENTS VERBATIM (`SetupLifecycleObservationResult.transition` + `result.created`); it does NOT reconstruct lifecycle state / quality / ranking. It holds NO lifecycle store, never reads providers / candles, never calls wall-clock.
- New models `src/engine/models/user_alerts.py` (frozen+slots): enums `AlertEventKind` (SETUP_DETECTED / SETUP_CONFIRMED / SETUP_INVALIDATED / SETUP_EXPIRED - the SMALLEST vocabulary justified by the 19.6 state-arrival events; no "score change" / "still confirmed" / "data unavailable" kinds), `AlertSeverity` (INFO / IMPORTANT / CRITICAL, FIXED deterministic function of kind via `severity_for_kind` - never a probability / confidence / recommendation), `AlertDeliveryStatus` (DELIVERED / FAILED / SKIPPED / SUPPRESSED). Models `AlertEvent` (identity + lifecycle identity + carried analytical payload; frozen, no retroactive mutation), `AlertDeliveryResult` (delivery timestamp kept SEPARATE from the analytical timestamp), `SuppressedAlert` (explicit auditable suppression record), `AlertCounts` (invariants: emitted == delivered+failed+skipped; eligible == emitted+suppressed), `AlertCycleResult` (one universe-wide alert-processing cycle). Helpers `build_alert_id` ("alert-"+sha256[:16] over policy_version+kind+lifecycle_id+observation_id), `kind_for_state`, `severity_for_kind`. `ALERT_MODEL_VERSION=1`.
- New config `src/engine/config/user_alerts_config.py`: `UserAlertConfig` - enabled, eligible_kinds (DEFAULT SETUP_CONFIRMED / SETUP_INVALIDATED / SETUP_EXPIRED ONLY; detection opt-in), min_quality_classification (optional filter on the CARRIED 19.5 classification, never a re-score), max_per_cycle (cap; over-cap eligible events SUPPRESSED explicitly), max_qualified_top_n (consumption of the EXISTING 19.5 `ranked_qualified` ranking - NEVER a second ranking algorithm), severity_policy ("fixed"), label / metadata. `alert_policy_version(config)` = deterministic sha256 of the snapshot - ANY config change is an alert-policy change embedded in every alert id + cycle id.
- New engine `src/dashboard/user_alerts.py`: `AlertEngine(config, store, channels).process(lifecycle_cycle, *, at=None) -> AlertCycleResult` - stateless except injected store / channels; consumes the 19.6 events; deterministic eligibility + identity-based dedup + delivery; NO wall-clock (at= injected; the delivery timestamp defaults to the observation instant and is NEVER used for identity). `AlertStore` (in-memory deterministic repository: record / load / has / list / dedup; 19.8 owns durability). `AlertDeliveryChannel` Protocol (name + deliver(alert) -> str) + `ConsoleAlertSink` (DEFAULT deterministic offline sink - no network / credentials) + `FailingAlertSink` (test-only). `alert_cycle_id(lifecycle_cycle_id, policy_version, config)` deterministic.
- ALERT ELIGIBILITY (explicit, minimal): state-arrival events only (transitions + creations); OBSERVED / SUPERSEDED / ABSENT statuses only (DATA_UNAVAILABLE / LATE_REJECTED / duplicate observations NEVER alert); kind deterministic from the arrived state; `enabled=False` / kind not in eligible set / quality gate not met / max_per_cycle reached / id-dedup -> explicit `SuppressedAlert` record (NEVER silent drops).
- NOISE CONTROL (major requirement): the same setup appearing in MANY scan cycles CANNOT produce repeated alerts because unchanged lifecycles produce NO new 19.6 transitions, and every event has a distinct stable alert id (identity-based dedup, NOT time-based cooldowns). 20 consecutive confirmed cycles -> exactly ONE confirmation alert (tested). Reordered provider / universe input cannot change the alert set / order.
- ALERT CONTENT (copied from upstream, never fabricated): alert_id / kind / severity, lifecycle_id / setup_id / instrument (canonical), lifecycle_state + previous_lifecycle_state (from the 19.6 transition's from_state; None for a creation), transition_reason (exact 19.6 reason), setup_type / direction / primary_timeframe, observation_id / observation_timestamp (analytical event timestamp) / scan_cycle_id / observation_status, policy_version, carried 19.5 quality (status / classification / score), carried 19.4 MTF (alignment / completeness), carried 11Q (setup_classification / confluence_score / has_conflict), reason. NO trade-plan semantics: no entry / stop / target / quantity / position / risk-reward / order (source-verified + tested). Direction is analytical (BULLISH / BEARISH), never an execution command (never "BUY NOW").
