## CHECKPOINT 19.8 — RELIABILITY / RECOVERY / OBSERVABILITY (added)

- Status: COMPLETE / **PASS**. Eighth checkpoint of the fixed Checkpoint 19 series (NIFTY Top 200 -> Market Data -> Continuous Scanner -> Multi-Timeframe Analysis -> Setup Intelligence -> Setup Lifecycle -> User Alerts -> **Reliability / Recovery / Observability** -> You -> Manual Execution). Builds the OPERATIONAL HARDENING layer that WRAPS the FROZEN 19.1-19.7 continuous intelligence pipeline. It answers: "Can this system continuously operate, detect failures, recover safely, and tell the operator what is happening without corrupting analytical state?" It is OPERATIONAL HARDENING ONLY — NOT another intelligence checkpoint. **BROKER EXECUTION REMAINS COMPLETELY DEFERRED THROUGHOUT CHECKPOINT 19.x** (Checkpoints 13-18 frozen; execution gate DISABLED). No 19.9 work started; nothing committed to git. Full audit document: `docs/checkpoint_19_8_reliability_recovery_observability_audit.md`.
- ARCHITECTURE-FIRST AUDIT (28 questions, all answered; documented in the audit doc): NO project logging framework / structured logging / log schema / heartbeat / pid-file / retry / backoff / timeout-boundary / health-model / operational diagnostics existed for the 19.x pipeline; the only process-management was the external Windows Task Scheduler smoke-check sidecar (`scripts/windows/keep_awake*`, OS-specific live-session utility, NOT scanner reliability infra, NOT reused); the 19.x scanner/lifecycle/alert stores are IN-MEMORY; the dashboard `/api/health` exposes data-provider status only; 19.2 `market_session` arithmetic + 19.3 `ContinuousScanner` no-overlap/state-machine + 19.7 `AlertDeliveryChannel` are the reusable reliability-adjacent primitives; per-symbol failure isolation already existed in 19.3 and is PRESERVED (never weakened).
- New config `src/engine/config/reliability_config.py`: `RetryPolicy` (max_attempts total incl. first; base_delay; backoff_factor>=1 multiplicative; `retryable_categories` default PROVIDER_TIMEOUT/PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE/DELIVERY_FAILURE; unknown/duplicate category names rejected; `delay_for(n)=base*factor**n` NO jitter; `sweep()` exact), `TimeoutPolicy` (provider 15s / cycle 300s / delivery 5s; None disables; positive-when-set), `HeartbeatPolicy` (interval 30s / max_quiet 120s / max_since_cycle 3600s; quiet>=interval validated), `HealthThresholds` (degradation 3 / recovery 2), `ReliabilityConfig` (bundles + deterministic `snapshot()` + `reliability_policy_version()` = "rv-"+sha256[:16] of the canonical snapshot; default policy version `25e4d9befd844b11`). Documented defaults as module constants: `DEFAULT_HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_SECONDS=30.0`, `DEFAULT_HEARTBEAT_MAX_QUIET_SECONDS=120.0`, `DEFAULT_STALLED_SCANNER_MAX_SINCE_CYCLE_SECONDS=3600.0`, `DEFAULT_HEARTBEAT_NAIVE_MARKER=False`, `DEFAULT_MARKER_STORE_FAIL_CLOSED=True`. `OPERATIONAL_STATE_SCHEMA_VERSION=1`. Configuration errors FAIL CLOSED at construction (invalid interval/timeout/retry/backoff/threshold/persistence all raise; no unsafe default substitution).
- New models `src/engine/models/operational_health.py` (frozen+slots): enums `ReliabilityFailureCategory` (PROVIDER_TIMEOUT/PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE/INVALID_RESPONSE/DELIVERY_FAILURE/CYCLE_FAILURE/HEARTBEAT_STALE/STALLED_SCANNER/RECOVERY_FAILURE/CONFIGURATION_FAILURE/PROCESS_FAILURE/UNKNOWN — explicit OPERATIONAL vocabulary, NEVER overlapping analytical enums; tested), `HealthStatus` (HEALTHY/DEGRADED/FAILED/UNKNOWN), `OperationalHealthState` (HEALTHY/DEGRADED/RECOVERING/FAILED/UNKNOWN), `HeartbeatState` (NONE/FRESH/STALE). Models `ProviderHealth`, `CycleHealth`, `DeliveryHealth`, `RetryRecord` (retry_id "retry-"+sha256[:16] + alert_id bound), `PerSymbolFailure` (attempts>=1), `CycleFailureSummary` (count invariants + start<=end), `RecoveryEvent` (recovery_id "recovery-"+sha256[:16]), `OperationalEventRecord` (event_id "opevt-"+sha256[:16]), `OperationalStateBundle` (snapshot_id "ops-"+sha256[:16] over recorded_at+provider+cycle+alert+policy; set_ids/alert_ids dedup identity), `OperationalHealthReport` (health_state/scanner_state/per-domain health/heartbeat/cycle_counts/last_cycle/provider+delivery counts/per-symbol failure count/retry+recovery+event counts/failure_counts/policy_version/rationale). Pure helpers `classify_heartbeat` (naive + future-dated -> STALE fail-closed), `derive_domain_health` (consecutive failures/successes), `derive_aggregate_health` (FAILED wins > RECOVERING > DEGRADED > HEALTHY; stale heartbeat or stalled scanner -> FAILED), `operational_snapshot_id`.
- New structured logging `src/dashboard/structured_log.py`: `LogLevel` (DEBUG<INFO<WARNING<ERROR<CRITICAL with rank), `StructuredEvent` (timestamp/component/event/level/cycle_id/setup_id/alert_id/symbol/status/duration/error_category/retry_count/recovery_state/detail; deterministic event_id "log-"+sha256[:16]; validation), `StructuredLogger(component, sink, clock)` (level-disciplined; expected per-symbol partial failures -> WARNING not CRITICAL; NO repr() object dumps in lines), `ListLogSink` (deterministic in-memory; `level_at_least`/`component` filters), `PrintLogSink` (default human output).
- New reliability engine `src/dashboard/reliability.py`: `HeartbeatTracker` (injected clock; refresh/state; last_heartbeat_at; reset), `bounded_call` (deadline; hung op -> `OperationTimedOut`; never an analytical signal), `RetryRunner` (bounded category-gated; `RetryExhausted` with attempts; `on_attempt` callback records `RetryRecord` bound to alert_id; deterministic delays; no infinite loops), `SingleInstanceGuard` (fail-closed: a LIVE marker raises even with force=True; a STALE marker (pid not alive) is recoverable; release/is_held/lock_info; shared marker store required for multi-instance detection — documented), `ReliabilityTracker(config, clock, store, logger)` — the operational observer: `refresh_heartbeat`, `record_cycle` (consumes 19.3 `MarketScanCycleResult`; enum-aware status; cycle counts completed/failed/partial/skipped/total + last_cycle_id/duration; never mutates the cycle), `record_provider_result` (consecutive streak -> provider health), `record_per_symbol_failure` (bounded), `observe_lifecycle_cycle`/`observe_alert_cycle` (collects setup_ids/alert_ids for restart dedup), `record_expected_exception` (PROCESS_FAILURE), `record_recovery` (RecoveryEvent; failure -> FAILED), `mark_recovery_pending`, `health(reference_now)` (derives OperationalHealthReport), `snapshot`, `startup_recovery` (fresh-start / recovered / corrupt-fail-closed), `shutdown` (flush when store configured). No wall-clock in engines/models (injected clock only); setup/alert ids NEVER derived from wall-clock.
- New alert outbox `src/dashboard/alert_outbox.py`: `AlertOutbox(channels, retry_policy, clock)` — OFFLINE deterministic delivery reliability over the FROZEN 19.7 `AlertDeliveryChannel`: enqueue (dedup by outbox_id/alert_id; deterministic "outbox-"+sha256[:16]), `process(now)` (due checks; delivery; failure -> retry per policy preserving the ORIGINAL alert_id/observation_id/setup_id/payload; budget exhaustion -> terminal FAILED entries; no channels -> SKIPPED explicit; delivered/failed/pending counts + entry lists). Retrying Alert A 20 times stays Alert A (never Alert B, never duplicate analytics). Delivery failure NEVER mutates lifecycle state (tested). `PendingOutboxEntry` + `build_outbox_id`.
- New persistence `src/engine/persistence/reliability_serialization.py` + `reliability_store.py` (established `__enum__`/`__dataclass__`/`__datetime__`/`__tuple__` tags; `OPERATIONAL_STATE_SCHEMA_VERSION=1`; lossless round trip; schema checked BEFORE reconstruction; future schema rejected; no pickle/eval/exec; deterministic sorted-key bytes) + `OperationalStateStore(directory=None)` (atomic same-dir temp + fsync best-effort + os.replace; safe-id regex; idempotent identical writes; conflicting content -> `OperationalStateIntegrityError` unless overwrite; corrupted payload -> `OperationalStoreError` fail-closed; `default_operational_state_directory()` = `./data/operational_state`). Additive exception classes in `src/engine/persistence/exceptions.py`: `OperationalStoreError`/`OperationalStateNotFoundError`/`OperationalStateIntegrityError`/`UnsupportedOperationalStateSchemaVersionError`.
- New reporting `src/engine/reporting/reliability.py` (full-path import; reporting/__init__.py NOT extended): `OperationalHealthFormatter(precision=2, width=80)` — `format(report)` (OPERATIONAL HEALTH REPORT sections: health state, per-domain health, heartbeat, cycle counts, last cycle, provider, delivery, retries, recovery, failure counts, rationale + DISCLAIMER) + `health_report_to_json` (deterministic sorted-key JSON). No print(); no predictive/BUY-SELL language.
- New operator CLI `scripts/check_system_health.py`: offline/deterministic default (`--provider fixture`; deterministic reference sentinel); `--json` pure machine JSON; `--instruments` (default NIFTY Top 200 via the frozen 19.1 manifest), `--timeframes` (default 15m), `--state-dir`, `--load-state`, `--reference-now` (must be timezone-aware; naive -> exit 2), `--provider`. Exit 0 = run completed with honest health findings; 1 = runtime; 2 = bad args. Banner: RELIABILITY/HEALTH DIAGNOSTICS ONLY — no trade execution, no alerts, no setup lifecycle, no broker execution. No credentials required.
- New demo `scripts/test_checkpoint_19_8.py`: 87/87 PASS (default config + policy version; retry sweep; heartbeat; single-instance live/stale/force; market-session weekend no-false-alarm; per-symbol isolation 1/2 failures; provider DEGRADED/FAILED + recovery; cycle observation; alert outbox id preservation + 20-retry stays alert A + delivery-failure-no-lifecycle-mutation; persistence round-trip + corrupt fail-closed + restart identity continuity; graceful start/stop/restart + no-overlap; structured logging; point-in-time no-wall-clock-in-identity; existing pipeline baseline signals=4/trades=3; health CLI). Exits 0.

- ARCHITECTURAL BOUNDARY: 19.8 MAY determine operational failure taxonomy, reliability configuration, heartbeat, retry/backoff/timeout policy, per-symbol failure isolation accounting, cycle/provider/delivery health, recovery classification, durable operational state (identity/dedup snapshot only), crash consistency, alert-delivery reliability (retry preserves alert identity; delivery failure never mutates lifecycle), graceful shutdown, single-instance protection, no-overlap preservation, observability, structured logging, configuration validation, operator diagnostics. It does NOT implement user notifications to external channels (19.7 boundary), forward-testing/backtesting (19.9), trade plans / execution / broker orders / portfolio-position management / automated entries-exits (deferred through 19.x). - FINAL VERDICT: **PASS** — the existing 19.1-19.7 analytical pipeline is semantically unchanged and regression-safe; operational failures are explicitly observable and isolated (one failed symbol can never terminate the Top-200 cycle); scanner/cycle/provider/delivery health are each observable and derived from observable facts (never from setup quality); heartbeat/stale/hung detection is explicit and testable (a live-but-stalled process is never healthy; market-closed periods never create false scanner alarms); retries and timeouts are bounded + deterministic (no infinite loops; no blind retry of deterministic failures); recovery is explicit and observable (DEGRADED -> RECOVERING -> HEALTHY); alert retry preserves the original alert id and never creates duplicate analytical alerts; delivery failure never mutates lifecycle state; durable operational state is atomic/schema-versioned/corruption-fail-closed and restart-safe where configured (setup/alert identity continuity preserved; corrupt state never fabricates analytical state); graceful shutdown + no-overlap preserved; single-instance protection fail-closed with stale-marker recovery; structured logging + operator diagnostic CLI give actionable operational information; the failure taxonomy and health model are explicit and distinct from analytical vocabulary; 117 new deterministic offline tests + an 87-check demo pass with ZERO regressions (full suite 7013 vs 6896 baseline, +117 net); broker-execution boundary intact (AST-verified); architecturally ready for 19.9 forward validation. **STOP after Checkpoint 19.8; 19.9 is NOT implemented; broker execution remains deferred.**
